# GoProperty — Progress Ledger

The status of every task in [IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md § 3](../IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md). Rules for updating it are in [IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md § 3.1](../IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md) and [AGENT.md § 6](../AGENT.md).

Status values: `Not started` · `In progress` · `Blocked` · `In review` · `Complete (YYYY-MM-DD)`.

## Current state

- **In progress:** wave 3 of P05 (started 2026-10-30): P05-03 (SMS credits) and P05-05 (delivery health dashboard). **P05-06 (WhatsApp from the agency's own Business account) is complete (2026-10-30)**; its defaults 23–35 in `docs/plans/P05-subtasks.md` are for the product owner to confirm. The product owner confirmed on 2026-10-30 that GoText reports delivery through `GET /sms/{uid}` (no webhook). **P05-02 (SMS through GoText) is complete (2026-10-30)**, built before GoText's answers (docs/plans/sms-provider-2026-10-29.md). **P05-04 (email templates) is complete (2026-10-30)**. P05 started 2026-10-29 at the product owner's request ("Start P05"), planned in three waves with defaults for its open questions in `docs/plans/P05-subtasks.md` (for the product owner to confirm); **P05-01 (the reminder engine) is complete (2026-10-29)**. Before that: the product owner chose **GoText** as the SMS provider on 2026-10-29 (PRE-01's SMS item; GoProperty-owned, never-promotional SMS templates): `docs/plans/sms-provider-2026-10-29.md`, with the questions to put to GoText before P05-02. The product owner's 2026-10-29 owner-visibility changes are complete (`docs/plans/owner-visibility-2026-10-29.md`): P03-16 (owners see their tenants, contracts and billing in full; the contact switch removed) and P04-06 (the agency chooses which tickets the owner sees), so P03 and P04 are complete again (2026-10-29). The product owner accepted all of their defaults and judgement calls on 2026-10-29 (`docs/plans/owner-visibility-2026-10-29.md`, "Accepted by the product owner"). Before that: Before that: P04 was complete (2026-10-28): all five tasks; P04-05 (open tickets move with the building on a handover) was the product owner's one change to the P04 decisions (`docs/plans/P04-subtasks.md` "Settled by the product owner"); the product owner accepted all P04 decisions, P04-05's included, on 2026-10-29 (ticket lifecycle, thread with internal notes and the owner's repairs, inboxes and notifications, handover follow-ups), built with the defaults in `docs/plans/P04-subtasks.md`; the product owner accepted those decisions on 2026-10-28 except for tickets on a handover. P08-05 (owner statements, brought forward by the product owner on 2026-10-28) and P04-04 (handover follow-ups) are complete; their ledger entries list the decisions for the product owner to confirm. P03 is complete (2026-10-28): all 15 tasks, and the phase-exit RentLoopTest journey passes on all four databases in GitHub CI (`docs/plans/P02-P03-subtasks.md`). P03 was built with default answers to its open questions; each task's ledger entry lists the decisions for the product owner to confirm

P00-12 (backups): built and drilled locally; needs one real run to an S3-compatible bucket and a Google Shared Drive (PRE-03 accounts)

### P05-06 — Complete (2026-10-30): WhatsApp from the agency's own WhatsApp Business account: connection settings, template sync from Meta, event → template mapping with variable mapping and preview, Cloud API sending logged in `notification_deliveries`, signed status webhooks, tenant opt-in, per-event channel order and fallback to SMS

**Delivered** ([ARCHITECTURE.md § 6.7 "How WhatsApp is built (P05-06)", § 8](../ARCHITECTURE.md); [DATABASE_SCHEMA.md § 7 `organization_whatsapp_connections`, `whatsapp_templates`, `notification_channel_mappings`, `whatsapp_opt_ins`, `reminders`, `notification_deliveries`](../DATABASE_SCHEMA.md); [BILLING_PAYMENTS.md § 1.2](../BILLING_PAYMENTS.md) (Growth and above); new defaults 23–35 of [docs/plans/P05-subtasks.md](plans/P05-subtasks.md)):
- **New module `Modules\WhatsApp`** (4 module migrations `2026_12_02_120101`–`_120104`: the four tables; Core migration `2026_12_02_120100`: index `(organization_id, provider_message_id)` on `notification_deliveries`). Reversible. Two new permissions: `whatsapp.connection` (org-owners; blocked while impersonating; not while suspended) and `whatsapp.messages` (owners and administrators; not while suspended). No new package.
- **Core contract (`App\WhatsApp`):** `WhatsAppSender::available($organization, ?$event)` (invitations, sign-in links and payment notices now pass their event) and `gaveUp()`; `WhatsAppMessage` gains a dedupe key, source and user; `SendWhatsAppMessage` gives every message a dedupe key, never retries a refusal, and fires `WhatsAppSent` / `WhatsAppFailed`; `WhatsAppRefused`. `App\Notifications\Channels`: `WhatsAppEvents` / `WhatsAppEvent` (every message that may go by WhatsApp, with its whitelisted placeholders; registered by Reminders, Tenants, Properties and Payments) and `WhatsAppRouting` (default `NoWhatsAppRouting`: SMS as before). Panels: Core `NotificationSettingsPanels` (Settings → Notifications), Tenants `Contracts\TenantPagePanels` (the tenant's record).
- **Connection** (Settings → WhatsApp, `WhatsAppSettingsPage`, `Connections\WhatsAppConnections`): WABA id, phone number id, permanent token and app secret checked with Meta, encrypted, never shown back (token masked to owners), check again, new verify token, disconnect; webhook URL + verify token shown to the owner; Growth plan and above (`Connections\WhatsAppPlan`, `services.whatsapp.plans`).
- **Meta** (`Meta\CloudApi`): `HttpCloudApi` only in production with `WHATSAPP_DRIVER=cloud`; `LogCloudApi` elsewhere.
- **Templates** (`Templates\TemplateSync`, `TemplateLayout`, `Jobs\SyncWhatsAppTemplates` daily at 05:20 Lagos, `whatsapp-templates-sync`): upserted by name and language, gone ones kept `disabled`; a template no longer approved/utility, or with changed variables, switches its mappings off on that sync with the reason.
- **Mapping** (Messages and templates, `WhatsAppMessagesPage`, `Mappings\ChannelMappings`): per event an approved utility template, each variable → one of the event's placeholders, channel order, on/off, preview with samples, copy to a ladder's other rungs.
- **Sending** (`Sending\CloudWhatsAppSender`, the `cloud` driver): delivery row first (channel `whatsapp`), claimed before Meta is called, at most once; refused → `failed`; 5xx → retried; refused token → connection `failing`; opted-out numbers refused.
- **Webhook** `/webhooks/whatsapp/{ulid}` (`WhatsAppWebhookController`, `Webhooks\WhatsAppWebhooks`, `Webhooks\StatusUpdates`, `Jobs\ProcessWhatsAppStatuses`): GET verify-token handshake; POST HMAC-SHA256 with the app secret, bad or missing → 401 and nothing recorded; statuses only forward, replay-safe; `failed` → `undelivered` + `WhatsAppFailed`.
- **Opt-in** (`OptIns\WhatsAppOptIns`): tenant in the portal (`PortalOptIns`, verified number), agency in person on the tenant's record (`TenantOptIn`, `tenants.portal_access`), opt-out at once; history kept.
- **Reminders:** `ReminderChannel::WhatsApp`, `SkipReason::FellBack`, `Reminders\ReminderEvents` (`reminder.rent_due.t7` style keys); the planner picks at most one phone channel per rung in the event's channel order; the sender falls back when WhatsApp cannot be used at send time; `Listeners\FallBackFromWhatsApp` (Meta refused or reported failed, on the reminder's own day) and `MarkReminderWhatsAppSent`. SMS fallback through `SmsOutbox::queue()` unchanged.
- **Docs:** ARCHITECTURE.md § 6.7 (contract, how WhatsApp is built), § 8 (module list); DATABASE_SCHEMA.md § 7 (four tables, reminders and deliveries as built); PLATFORM_CONSOLE.md (delivery log shows WhatsApp); ONBOARDING_COMPLIANCE.md § 2 (invitations by WhatsApp); `.env.example`; docs/plans/P05-subtasks.md defaults 23–35.

**Tests and evidence:**
- **AC: a template paused by Meta disables its mapping on next sync and that event falls back to SMS** — `Modules/WhatsApp/tests/Feature/WhatsAppRemindersTest.php` › "acceptance: … › sends rent T−7 by WhatsApp, then, once the daily sync finds the template paused, rent T−1 by SMS" (the real Meta request checked: template, language, parameters, URL-button suffix; the scheduled `whatsapp-templates-sync` turns every rent mapping off with `template_paused`; T−1 then goes by SMS, no further Meta call), › "falls back to SMS the same day when the template was paused after the reminder was planned" (`fell_back`); `WhatsAppMappingTest` › "shows a mapping a sync switched off, and why", › "switches a mapping off when Meta changed the template's variables".
- **AC: a status webhook with a bad signature is rejected** — `Modules/WhatsApp/tests/Feature/WhatsAppWebhookTest.php` › "acceptance: … › refuses a call signed with the wrong secret, another organization's, or not signed, and records nothing" (also a body changed after signing; delivery and `last_event_at` unchanged), › "accepts the same call signed with the organization's app secret"; › "answers Meta's verify handshake only with the organization's verify token", › "moves a delivery forward only…", › "applies only statuses for this organization's own number and messages", › "refuses calls to a disconnected organization's address (404) and too large a body (413)", › "queues the statuses on `notifications`…".
- **Fallback and routing** — `WhatsAppRemindersTest`: not opted in → SMS unchanged; channel order (WhatsApp-only rung, SMS first); phone-only tenant with recorded consent; opt-out honoured at once; Meta refuses → SMS at once; WhatsApp only → no SMS; refused token → connection failing, SMS; Meta reports failed later → SMS on that day only; Starter plan → SMS.
- **Connection** — `WhatsAppConnectionTest` (12 incl. datasets): connect from the page (encrypted at rest, never in the page, snapshot or JSON), refused token, shapes refused before calling Meta, replace keeping token/secret, failing and active again, disconnect + audit, admins see/sync but cannot change credentials, agents 403, other organization 404, impersonating staff refused in both modes, Starter plan, suspended read-only, log transport outside production.
- **Mapping** — `WhatsAppMappingTest`: sync keeps status and variables, preview, refusals (marketing, unknown placeholder, missing variable, SMS for invitations, SMS on a non-SMS rung, no template, unknown event), another organization's template, the events list, the page flow with copy to rungs, agents refused, suspended read-only.
- **Opt-in and invitations** — `WhatsAppOptInTest`: agency records consent and opt-out on the tenant page, consent again after a number change (`replaced`), panel hidden until connected, another organization's tenant refused, the portal panel (verified number only, other agencies 404, hidden without WhatsApp), invitations offered and sent with their mapped template, nothing to an opted-out number.
- Existing suites unchanged and passing (reminders, invitations, owner invitations, payment notices, cash receipts); new models in the cross-organization probes; `whatsapp.connection` in `ImpersonationGuardTest`.
- Local gate (MariaDB 11.4, PHP 8.4): `composer ci` — Pint passed; Larastan 0 errors; Pest 3,782 tests: 3,712 passed, 70 skipped (Gotenberg halves), 0 failed. With `APP_DEBUG=true`: WhatsApp 58, Reminders 26, Tenants 196 (4 skipped), Owner portal 102 (2 skipped), Payments 130 (3 skipped), Settings 14, Sms 32 — all passed. MySQL 8 runs only in GitHub CI.

**Follow-ups:**
- Production: set `WHATSAPP_DRIVER=cloud` (the default stays `log`).
- Inbound messages (a tenant replying STOP) as an opt-out; bill-issued, ticket-update and renewal-notice WhatsApp messages; P05-05's dashboard to count WhatsApp deliveries; Meta Tech Provider / Embedded Signup (ARCHITECTURE.md § 6.7 "Later").
- **For the product owner to confirm:** defaults 23–35 (notably 24 owner-only credentials, 31 fallback after a later `failed`, 32 no opt-in for invitations and payment notices).

**Next:** P05-03 and P05-05 (wave 3), then the P05 exit.

### P05-02 — Complete (2026-10-30): SMS through GoText for bill reminders: verified numbers only, GoProperty-owned one-page SMS templates, the `notification_deliveries` log with retries and a console delivery log, delivery-status polling, and per-user channel preferences

**Delivered** ([ARCHITECTURE.md § 6.7 "How SMS is built", § 7](../ARCHITECTURE.md); [DATABASE_SCHEMA.md `users.preferences`, `email_templates.channel`, `reminders`, `notification_deliveries`](../DATABASE_SCHEMA.md); [PLATFORM_CONSOLE.md § 5](../PLATFORM_CONSOLE.md); defaults 14–15 and new 17–22 of [docs/plans/P05-subtasks.md](plans/P05-subtasks.md); [docs/plans/sms-provider-2026-10-29.md](plans/sms-provider-2026-10-29.md) "Built in P05-02"):
- **Schema (4 migrations):** Core `2026_11_30_110101` `users.preferences`, `_110102` `email_templates.channel`, `_110103` `notification_deliveries`; Reminders `2026_11_30_110104` `reminders.recipient_phone`. Reversible. No new permission (`delivery_logs.view` existed) and no new package.
- **SMS (Core `App\Sms`):** `SmsSender` with `GoTextSmsSender` (JSON POST, token in the body, never the GET form; status by `GET /sms/{uid}`; GoText's `data` read defensively) and `LogSmsSender`; bound to GoText only in production with `SMS_DRIVER=gotext` and `GOTEXT_API_TOKEN` (`.env.example`, `config/services.php`), as SocietyFlow configures it. `SmsOutbox` (delivery row first, unique dedupe key, E.164, one GSM page), `SendSms` (4 tries, backoff 1/5/30 min, at most once: an attempt that died mid-call is marked failed, never resent), `CheckSmsStatus` (5 min, 30 min, 2 h, 12 h), `SmsSent` event, `SmsText` (GSM-only, NGN for ₦, accents dropped, masking), `DeliveryStatus`. Model `App\Models\NotificationDelivery` (organization-scoped, never deleted; added to the cross-organization probes).
- **SMS templates:** `TemplateChannel`; `Placeholder::$max`; the catalog takes SMS only for transactional and reminder templates; `EmailTemplates::renderSms()` cuts each value to its length so a message never passes 160; a version is saved only when it fits with every value at its longest. Console → Message templates: channel column and filter; the SMS editor shows the text with samples and both lengths, no subject, button or test send. Four SMS templates seeded (`reminder.rent_due.sms`, `rent_upcoming.sms`, `bill_due.sms`, `bill_overdue.sms`).
- **Reminders:** `ReminderChannel::Sms`; `ReminderType::smsRungs()` (rent T−7, T−1; utility T−0; overdue +1, +7); `ReminderRecipients` gives a tenant's verified account phone and the channels they turned off (staff too); the planner writes SMS rows only on SMS rungs with `recipient_phone`; `ReminderMessages` skips an SMS whose number changed or is no longer verified (`unreachable`) and any channel turned off since planning (new `SkipReason::OptedOut`), and gives the SMS values; `ReminderSender` sends SMS through `SmsOutbox`; `Listeners\MarkReminderSmsSent`.
- **Preferences:** `App\Notifications\Preferences\NotificationPreferences`; Settings → Notifications (`/settings/notifications`, `pages::settings.notifications`; reminders by email and by SMS, SMS shown only with a verified number; `#[BlockedWhileImpersonating]` on save).
- **Console:** Operations → Delivery log (`DeliveryLogResource`, read model `App\Platform\Deliveries\DeliveryLogEntry`, `DeliveryLogEntryPolicy`: `delivery_logs.view`): every SMS across organizations, masked number, no text, failed and undelivered counted on the menu for 7 days.
- **Docs:** ARCHITECTURE.md § 6.7 (SMS, preferences, how it is built) and § 7 (on-demand SMS jobs); DATABASE_SCHEMA.md (four changes as built); PLATFORM_CONSOLE.md § 5 (SMS templates, delivery log); docs/plans/P05-subtasks.md (defaults 15 as built, 17–22 new); docs/plans/sms-provider-2026-10-29.md (what was built before GoText's answers).

**Tests and evidence:**
- **AC: SMS template stays under 160 chars** — `tests/Feature/Sms/SmsTemplatesTest.php` › "ships SMS templates that fit one page with every value at its longest, on a long site address too", › "sends at most 160 GSM characters whatever the values: long names cut, accents and the naira sign made plain" (a 78-character Yoruba agency name with an emoji, ₦999,999,999.99), › "refuses to save an SMS that could pass 160 characters, or that uses characters outside one GSM page", › "edits an SMS in the console with its length…"; `SmsRemindersTest` checks every sent text is ≤ 160 and GSM-only.
- **AC: failed sends retry with backoff then surface in the health dashboard** — `tests/Feature/Sms/SmsDeliveryTest.php` › "retries after 1, 5 and 30 minutes, then marks the delivery failed and shows it in the console delivery log" (the real database queue and worker: not before its backoff, four attempts, `failed` with the error, counted on the menu, listed with the number masked and no text), › "sends after a failed attempt once GoText takes it, and checks what became of it", › "never sends a message twice…", › "keeps checking an unknown status…", › "shows the delivery log to support only, filtered to problems". The full health dashboard is P05-05, on the same rows.
- **AC: unverified numbers never texted** — `Modules/Reminders/tests/Feature/SmsRemindersTest.php` › "texts nobody whose number is not verified: an unverified account, a tenant with no account, a phone-only tenant", › "stops texting a number that is no longer verified, or was changed after the reminder was planned".
- **Rungs, wording, once** — › "texts a verified number on rent T−7 and T−1 and overdue +1 and +7 only, once each…" (every day from 25 Nov 2026 to 20 Jan 2027: 4 SMS, 9 emails), › "texts a utility bill on its due day only", › "plans and sends nothing twice when the job runs again and again".
- **Preferences** — › "plans no SMS or no email for a tenant who turned that channel off…", › "skips a reminder planned before the tenant turned its channel off" (`opted_out`), › "lets staff turn off renewal alert emails, and never texts renewal alerts"; `tests/Feature/Settings/NotificationSettingsTest.php` (3).
- **GoText driver** — `tests/Feature/Sms/GoTextSmsSenderTest.php`: the JSON POST with the token in the body and no `+`, unreadable `data` tolerated, errors without the token, status words mapped, and real SMS only in production with GoText chosen and a token.
- Local gate (MariaDB 11.4, PHP 8.4.27): Pint clean; Larastan 0 errors; full `php artisan test --parallel` 3,704 tests: 3,634 passed, 70 skipped (Gotenberg halves), 0 failed; new and touched suites 360 passed with `APP_DEBUG=true`. MySQL 8 runs only in GitHub CI.

**Follow-ups:**
- **GoText's answers** (docs/plans/sms-provider-2026-10-29.md "To confirm"): the fields of `data`, a delivery-report webhook, the DND route and `dlt_template_id`, per-agency sender IDs, test mode. Adjust `App\Sms\GoTextSmsSender` once they arrive; set `SMS_DRIVER=gotext`, `GOTEXT_API_TOKEN` and `GOTEXT_SENDER_ID` in production (PRE-03).
- P05-03: SMS credits, charged from `notification_deliveries` (sending stops at zero).
- P05-05: the delivery health dashboard on these rows, and email deliveries in the same table.
- SMS for other transactional messages (bill issued, receipt for cash) when they get SMS templates.
- **For the product owner to confirm:** defaults 17–22 in docs/plans/P05-subtasks.md.

**Next:** wave 3 — P05-03 (SMS credits), P05-05 (delivery health dashboard) and P05-06 (WhatsApp), which can run side by side.

### P05-04 — Complete (2026-10-30): the email template system: GoProperty's emails as database templates with whitelisted placeholders (never Blade), versions with rollback, a live preview and "send test to me" in the console, categories, the agency's branding in a trusted layout, and bounce/complaint suppression that every outgoing email obeys; the reminders now send through it

**Delivered** ([PLATFORM_CONSOLE.md § 5 "How it is built (P05-04)"](../PLATFORM_CONSOLE.md); [DATABASE_SCHEMA.md "email_templates / email_template_versions", "email_suppressions", `webhook_log`](../DATABASE_SCHEMA.md); [ARCHITECTURE.md § 2, § 6.7](../ARCHITECTURE.md); default 16 of [docs/plans/P05-subtasks.md](plans/P05-subtasks.md)):
- **Schema (3 Core migrations):** `2026_11_30_100101` `email_templates`, `_100102` `email_template_versions` (adds `action_label` and `restored_from_version` to the planned columns), `_100103` `email_suppressions`. Platform tables (no `organization_id`). Reversible. No new permission (`templates.edit`, `delivery_logs.view`, `settings.email_provider` already existed) and no new package (league/commonmark ships with Laravel).
- **Engine (`App\Mail\Templates`):** `TemplateCatalog` (code's templates; modules add theirs from their providers), `TemplateDefinition`, `Placeholder`, `TemplateContent`, `TemplateCategory` (security, transactional, reminder, marketing), `TemplateRenderer` (whitelisted `{{ name }}` only; values Markdown-escaped; raw HTML escaped, unsafe links dropped; Blade, PHP and unknown placeholders shown as typed), `EmailTemplates` (only writer: save → new version, rollback → new copy, switch off, seed; audited under log `email`; falls back to the code's wording when a template is off or not seeded), `EmailBrand` (agency or platform), `BrandedMail` + `resources/views/mail/branded{,-text}.blade.php` (trusted layout, logo embedded), `TemplatePreview`, `TemplateTestEmail`. `EmailTemplatesSeeder` added to InstallSeeder (idempotent, never undoes edits).
- **Suppression (`App\Mail\Suppression`):** `Suppressions` (suppress, lift, audited; in-app `Notifications\EmailAddressSuppressed` to accounts with the address), `DropSuppressedRecipients` (on `NotificationSending` and `MessageSending`: no email reaches a suppressed address, whatever sends it), `EmailWebhooks` + `EmailWebhookDelivery` + `Http\Controllers\Webhooks\EmailWebhookController` at `POST /webhooks/email/{postmark|mailgun}/{token}` (routes/webhooks.php; `MAIL_WEBHOOK_TOKEN`, `MAILGUN_WEBHOOK_SIGNING_KEY`; calls kept in `webhook_log`; permanent bounces and complaints suppress).
- **Console:** Content → Email templates (`EmailTemplateResource`: list by category, editor with placeholders, live preview in a sandboxed frame with warnings for unknown placeholders and typed code, Send test to me (5 per 10 minutes), versions table with Roll back to this; `EmailTemplatePolicy`) and Content → Suppressed addresses (`EmailSuppressionResource`: list, Allow again, Add an address; `EmailSuppressionPolicy`).
- **Reminders:** `Reminders\ReminderTemplates` (six templates seeded with P05-01's wording, unchanged), `ReminderMessages` gives template keys and values, `ReminderSender` renders, `ReminderNotice` carries the rendered email and brand and builds it with `BrandedMail`; new `SkipReason::Suppressed`.
- **Docs:** PLATFORM_CONSOLE.md § 5 "How it is built (P05-04)"; DATABASE_SCHEMA.md (the three tables as built, `webhook_log` email rows, `suppressed` skip reason); ARCHITECTURE.md § 2 email row, § 6.7 (wording from templates, suppressed skip); `.env.example`; docs/plans/P05-subtasks.md default 16 as built.

**Tests and evidence:**
- **AC: Blade/PHP in a template renders as plain text** — `tests/Feature/Email/EmailTemplatesTest.php` › "fills in only whitelisted placeholders and shows Blade, PHP, HTML and unknown placeholders exactly as typed, escaped" (`{{ $user->password }}`, `{{ 7 * 7 }}` not 49, `{!! phpinfo() !!}`, `@php`, `<?php`, `<script>`, `javascript:` links, a non-whitelisted value never filled), › "never lets a value become Markdown or HTML", › "sends a tenant reminder in the trusted layout with the code typed into the template as text", › "warns in the editor…"; `ReminderTemplatesTest` › "sends what the content manager wrote, in the agency's branding…" (typed Blade arrives escaped in the real email).
- **AC: rollback restores the previous version** — › "rolls back to the version before, as a new version, keeping every version as it was" (render uses version 1's wording; v1, v2 unchanged; audited with both versions), › "rolls back from the versions table in the console", › "refuses a version that does not exist or is already being sent".
- **AC: suppressed addresses are skipped** — `EmailSuppressionTest` › "sends no notification email to a suppressed address, on demand or to an account…", › "drops suppressed To, Cc and Bcc addresses from any email, and sends nothing when none is left", › "mails an address again once staff lift its suppression…"; `ReminderTemplatesTest` › "skips the email to a suppressed address, recorded as suppressed, and still tells the tenant in the app", › "skips a renewal alert to a staff member whose address is suppressed".
- **Versions, live preview, test send, categories, branding, webhooks, console access** — versions on every change and none unchanged, validation, seeding twice without undoing edits, switched-off fallback; console open for content-manager and super-admin only (403 for support, analyst, technical-admin, finance-admin), list filter by category, save → version 2, live preview of unsaved wording, test email to oneself only and rate-limited; Postmark hard bounce / complaint / soft bounce / duplicate, 404 for wrong or unset token, Mailgun signature, in-app notice once; suppressed list for support and technical admins, lift and add by technical admin only, audited.
- P05-01's reminder suites unchanged in substance (one test builds the notice with the new arguments) and passing.
- `StaffTwoFactorEnforcementTest`'s crawl of every console page now opens the template editor with a real template (it had fallen back to a staff id).
- Local gate (MariaDB 11.4, PHP 8.4.27): Pint clean; Larastan 0 errors; full `php artisan test --parallel` 3,656 tests: 3,585 passed, 70 skipped (Gotenberg halves), 1 failed (the console crawl above, fixed and passing: 4 passed); the new and touched suites (tests/Feature/Email, Modules/Reminders, the console crawl, InstallSeederTest) 52 passed with `APP_DEBUG=true`. MySQL 8 runs only in GitHub CI.

**Follow-ups:**
- Move the other emails onto templates (invitations, sign-in and security, payment, receipt and ticket notices, platform billing); their wording is still in code.
- SES bounce and complaint notifications (through SNS: subscription handshake and signature check).
- Marketing category: opt-in consent and an unsubscribe link in the layout once a marketing email exists.
- P05-02 adds SMS versions of the transactional and reminder templates (same engine, under 160 characters) and per-user channel preferences.
- Set `MAIL_WEBHOOK_TOKEN` (and `MAILGUN_WEBHOOK_SIGNING_KEY` for Mailgun) on each install and point the provider's bounce and complaint webhooks at `/webhooks/email/{provider}/{token}` (PRE-03 accounts).

**Next:** P05-02 (SMS through GoText, wave 2; built against the `log` driver until the GoText test account and answers arrive).

### P05-01 — Complete (2026-10-29): the reminder engine: a new `Modules\Reminders` with the `reminders` table and an hourly job that plans each day's rent, utility, overdue and lease renewal rungs and sends them from 08:00 Lagos, each once (unique dedupe key), by email and in the app, in the agency's name

**Delivered** ([ARCHITECTURE.md § 6.7 "How the reminder engine is built", § 7, § 8](../ARCHITECTURE.md); [DATABASE_SCHEMA.md § 7 `reminders`](../DATABASE_SCHEMA.md); defaults 1–13 of [docs/plans/P05-subtasks.md](plans/P05-subtasks.md)):
- **Schema (1 migration, Reminders):** `2026_11_29_090101` `reminders` (one row per rung, channel and recipient; `dedupe_key` unique per organization; `notification_id` unique; indexes for dispatch, remindable and tenant). Reversible. No foreign keys into other modules' tables.
- **Reminders module:** `Jobs\DispatchReminders` (`reminders-dispatch`, `Schedule::job` hourly at :35, `notifications` queue; unique, cache-locked, 40 s budget then resumes from the organization it was on; all organizations but cancelled ones; a failing organization is reported and skipped). `Reminders\ReminderPlanner` (today's rungs: bills due on a rung's day, a lease's next rent period before its bill, active leases ending without a drafted renewal and once started, none while suspended; rungs at most half the bill's period, none for periods under 7 days; already-planned keys read first, a concurrent insert caught by the unique index). `Reminders\ReminderSender` (pending rows whose `send_at` has come, each locked and checked again by `Reminders\ReminderMessages`; sent or skipped with `missed`, `settled`, `lease_ended`, `renewal_drafted`, `unreachable` or `organization_inactive`). `Reminders\ReminderRecipients` (tenants through TenantReach, by email and in the app; renewal alerts to the building's assigned agents holding `leases.manage`, else owners and administrators holding it). `Notifications\ReminderNotice` (one channel each, branded sender and reply-to, retry-safe through `notification_id` and `delivered_at`), `Listeners\MarkReminderDelivered`. Enums `ReminderType` (with the ladders), `ReminderChannel`, `ReminderStatus`, `SkipReason`; model `Reminder` (plan never changes, never deleted).
- **New contracts:** Billing `Contracts\RemindableBills` (`dueOn`, `find`, `rentFor`) + `RemindableBill` → `Bills\RemindableBillRecords`; Leasing `Contracts\EndingLeases` (`endingOn`, `findActive`) → `Leases\OwnerLeaseRecords` (shares the renewal lookup with `endingBetween`); Tenants `Contracts\TenantReach` (`contactable`: not archived; imported only once invited or with a portal account) → `Profiles\TenantReachRecords`. Read only; no other module's internals used.
- **Docs:** ARCHITECTURE.md § 6.7 (as built), § 7 schedule row (hourly at :35, replacing "daily 00:25"), § 8 module list; DATABASE_SCHEMA.md § 7 `reminders` as built; `docs/plans/P05-subtasks.md` (waves, defaults; default 2 worded as "at most half the period", default 10 adds "not before the lease starts").

**Tests and evidence:**
- **AC: time-travel tests prove exact send days** — `ReminderLaddersTest` › "sends the annual rent ladder and then the overdue ladder on their exact days…" (every day from 25 Nov 2026 to 20 Jan 2027 lived with the real scheduled jobs: rent T−30, T−14, T−7, T−3, T−1, T−0 and overdue +1, +7, +14 on exactly those days, by email and in the app; T−30/T−14 from the lease, T−7 on from the bill), › "uses only the rungs that fit the cycle: monthly from T−14, weekly from T−3, none at all for daily rent", › "does not catch up a day the job never ran, and records … as missed"; `RenewalRemindersTest` › "alerts the agent assigned to the building on the exact rung days…" (the days either side of each rung lived too).
- **AC: zero duplicates across reruns** — › "plans and sends nothing twice when the job runs again, twice at once, or a notification is retried" (the job six more times that day, the planner twice and the sender straight after, a retried mail and in-app notification: rows, mails and in-app notices unchanged); the ladder test runs the job four times every day and counts nine emails and nine in-app notices.
- **Checked before sending** — › "stops reminding once the bill is paid in full, also between planning and sending, and says what is still owed after a part payment", › "uses the bill for a reminder planned from the lease once the bill is issued before the send time"; renewal › "stops once a renewal is drafted, also between planning and sending", › "sends no renewal alerts while the organization is suspended, but still reminds its tenants of their bills".
- **Who hears** — › "tells the tenant in the agency's name, with the amount, the payment reference and their link" (pay page without an account, portal bill page with one), › "never reminds a phone-only tenant, an archived tenant, or an imported tenant before the agency invites them", › "keeps each organization's reminders to its own tenants, in its own name"; renewal › "alerts the owners and administrators when no agent is assigned…".
- **Cross-org probes:** `Reminder` added to `tests/Support/OrganizationScopedModels.php`; `CrossTenantAccessTest` 260 passed.
- Local gate (this session; PHP 8.4.27 built from source, MySQL 8.0): Pint clean; Larastan 0 errors; `Modules/Reminders` 14 passed (1,901 assertions); full `php artisan test --parallel` 3,620 tests: 3,534 passed, 71 skipped (Gotenberg halves), 15 failed, all environmental: 11 drills "did not boot in time" under the 4-core parallel load (BillGeneration and TicketNumbering drills pass when run alone), CronDrill and BackupDrill because this session's Composer had to install packages as git clones (`vendor/sebastian/version/.git` makes it call `proc_open`, which those drills disable), FreshCopyDrill likewise; ModuleSkeletonTest (Composer as root without `COMPOSER_ALLOW_SUPERUSER`: passes with it) and CrossTenantAccessTest (fixed in this task). CI on GitHub installs archives and is the check for the drills.

**Follow-ups:**
- **For the product owner to confirm:** the defaults in `docs/plans/P05-subtasks.md` (ladders, half-period rule, no separate "bill issued" message, no catch-up, 08:00 send time, imported tenants silent until invited, renewal alerts to staff only).
- **P05-07 (proposed):** organization settings to switch a ladder off or pick its days, with the "days to rent due" preview.
- P05-02 adds the `sms` channel (and `notification_deliveries`), P05-06 `whatsapp`; P05-04 moves the wording into templates. Phone-only tenants hear nothing until then.
- A staff page listing a bill's or tenant's reminders (sent and skipped) is not built; the rows are there for it.
- Pending rows of an organization that is cancelled stay `pending` (the job no longer visits it).

**Next:** P05-04 (email templates) and then P05-02 (SMS through GoText; its questions to GoText are in `docs/plans/sms-provider-2026-10-29.md`).

### P03-16 — Complete (2026-10-29): owners see their tenants, contracts and billing in full, read-only: tenants in `/owner` with every detail (ID number masked to its last four characters), each unit's lease history as a timeline, the issued lease contracts, bills and receipts as on-screen pages with the payment history (no bank account, no payment reference), each lease's deposit ledger; the agency's per-owner "share tenant contact details" switch removed (column dropped) and the owner statement shows tenant contacts wherever it names the tenant

**Delivered** (product owner 2026-10-29, `docs/plans/owner-visibility-2026-10-29.md` items 1–3 and P03-16 defaults 1–6; [ARCHITECTURE.md § 4, § 5](../ARCHITECTURE.md); [DATABASE_SCHEMA.md § 4](../DATABASE_SCHEMA.md); [BILLING_PAYMENTS.md § 7.9](../BILLING_PAYMENTS.md); [BRANDING_DOCUMENTS.md § 4](../BRANDING_DOCUMENTS.md); [ONBOARDING_COMPLIANCE.md § 2](../ONBOARDING_COMPLIANCE.md); [UX_DESIGN.md § 2](../UX_DESIGN.md)):
- **Schema (1 migration, OwnerStatements):** `2026_11_28_090101`: drops `owner_statement_terms.share_tenant_contacts` (down() adds it back, default off). No new table, model, permission or package.
- **OwnerStatements:** the switch is gone from `StatementTerms`, `StatementTermsBook` (a legacy `shareTenantContacts` key is ignored, not refused; audits `before`/`after` without it), `OwnerStatementTerms`, `OwnerStatementIndex` (terms form and read-only view). Renewals always show the tenant's phone and email; `BillLine` and `CollectionLine` carry the tenant's phone and email **as the bill was issued** (new snapshot keys `tenant_phone`, `tenant_email`; a month settled before them reads null and shows what it recorded); the on-screen statement shows them under the tenant (`data-test="statement-tenant-contacts"`). The PDF names no tenant (units only): unchanged. `Contracts\TenantContactSharing` kept, its implementation (`SettledMonthRecords::sharesWith`) now always answers true, commented as kept only until the Tickets module stops reading it (P04-06); the orchestrator deletes it after both tasks merge.
- **Tenants:** `Contracts\OwnerTenants::details(ids)` + `TenantDetails` (name, phone, email, address, occupation, emergency contact name/phone/relationship, KYC status/label/colour, ID type/label, `idNumberMasked` = "···" + last four characters, "···" for four or fewer, null with none; archived) → `Profiles\OwnerTenantRecords` (decrypts only to cut the number). The full ID number never leaves the module.
- **Leasing:** `Contracts\OwnerLeases` gains `history()`, `forUnit()`, `forTenant()` with `LeaseHistory`, `LeaseEvent` (moved_in, renewed, rent_changed, terminated, moved_out — worked out from `start_date`, `renewal_of_lease_id` and that lease's rent, `terminated_at` (Lagos day) and reason, `status`, the activated renewal that followed), `DepositLine` (the `lease_deposit_entries` ledger, signed, with balance) and `hasContract`; every method now shows only **activated** leases (`activated_at` set), so a cancelled or lapsed draft (status terminated/expired, never let) is no longer found by `find()` either. `Contracts\OwnerLeaseContracts::pdf()` → `Documents\OwnerContractFiles`: the kept PDF of a contract this organization issued (rendered once if the queue has not yet); never issues one.
- **Billing:** `OwnerBills::idsFor()`; `OwnerBill` gains `tenantPhone`, `tenantEmail` (as issued, `bills.details`). Still never the payment reference.
- **Payments:** `Contracts\OwnerPayments` (`forBills`, `billIdsOfReceipt`, `receipt`) + `OwnerPayment` (a confirmed payment's part of a bill — refunded / receipt voided marked — or tenant credit used; method and day paid; receipt id and number) and `OwnerReceipt` / `OwnerReceiptLine` (number, issue date, whole amount, method, day paid, valid or voided, only the lines for the given bills, as issued) → `Receipts\OwnerPaymentRecords`. Never a pending payment, a rejected one, a proof, a reference or bank details.
- **Properties (owner portal):** `OwnerPortalRecords` reads through the new contracts (TenantContactSharing and TenantDirectory no longer used): `unitPage`, `tenantPage`, `receiptPage`, `contractPdf`, and richer `leasePage` (tenant contacts, `LeaseHistory`, payments with bill numbers, latest 50 + count) and `billPage` (payments, tenant link). Value objects `OwnerUnitPage`, `OwnerUnitLease`, `OwnerTenantPage`, `OwnerReceiptPage`; `OwnerLease::colorOf()`. Pages (`ReadOnlyOwnerPage`, `#[Locked]` id only): `/owner/units/{id}` (`OwnerUnitShow`), `/owner/tenants/{id}` (`OwnerTenantShow`), `/owner/receipts/{id}` (`OwnerReceiptShow`); `/owner/leases/{id}/contract` (`Http\Controllers\OwnerContractController`, signed media link, `#[BlockedWhileImpersonating]`, throttle 60/min). Views: `unit-show`, `tenant-show`, `receipt-show`, partials `timeline`, `payments`; `lease-show` (tenant contacts + link, "Lease history", "Contract (PDF)", timeline, deposit, payments, bills), `bill-show` (tenant contacts as issued + link, total, payments), `building-show` ("Lease history" per unit). Phone first: cards, 44 px links, no autofocus.
- **Docs:** ARCHITECTURE.md § 4 (owner path's contracts), § 5 role matrix (`property-owner`), "Owner portal" (P03-16 paragraph), "Owner statements" (contracts, owner pages); DATABASE_SCHEMA.md (`owner_statement_terms`, dropped column); BILLING_PAYMENTS.md § 7.9 (term removed, contacts on statements); BRANDING_DOCUMENTS.md (contract re-print from the owner portal; no invoice/receipt PDF for owners; statement PDF names no tenant); ONBOARDING_COMPLIANCE.md § 2 (what the owner gets, NDPA note); UX_DESIGN.md § 2 owner portal row.

### P04-06 — Complete (2026-10-29): the agency chooses which tickets the owner sees (product owner 2026-10-29, replacing P04-subtasks default 8): every ticket, existing ones included, is hidden from the building's owner until a member holding `tickets.manage` on its building shares it (and may stop sharing), audited and shown on the staff page; the owner's list, ticket page, files and reply-while-unassigned cover shared tickets only; the owner is told (in the app and by email) once when it is shared, then of each status change and public reply while shared, never an internal note or an assignment; a ticket moved on a handover arrives unshared; staff list badge, filter and export column; the owner's ticket page shows the tenant's phone and email without the removed switch

**Delivered** ([ARCHITECTURE.md § 5 "Owner portal" → Repairs, "Tickets shared with the owner", role matrix, ticket visibility and notifications; § 6.6 "As built (P04-06)"; § 6.7](../ARCHITECTURE.md); [DATABASE_SCHEMA.md § 5 `tickets`, `ticket_notices`](../DATABASE_SCHEMA.md); [UX_DESIGN.md § 2 customer app and owner portal](../UX_DESIGN.md); "Settled by the product owner" 4–5 and the P04-06 defaults 1–8 of [docs/plans/owner-visibility-2026-10-29.md](plans/owner-visibility-2026-10-29.md)):
- **Schema (1 migration, Tickets):** `2026_11_27_100101` `tickets.shared_with_owner_at` (TIMESTAMP nullable) and `shared_with_owner_by_user_id` (nullable FK `users`, null on delete), index `(organization_id, property_id, shared_with_owner_at)`. Existing rows stay null (unshared). Reversible. No new table, no new model.
- **`TicketService`** (still the only writer): `shareWithOwner()` / `stopSharingWithOwner()` (policy, ticket row locked; not a state-machine move: no history row, status untouched; any sent ticket incl. closed, not one left behind by a move; refused "no owner on record" when the building has no owner profile; idempotent no-ops); audit `ticket.shared_with_owner` (owner profile) / `ticket.unshared_with_owner` (who had shared it and when); `Events\TicketSharedWithOwner` after commit (key ULID); unsharing sends nothing. `receiveMoved()` leaves the copy unshared. `ownerProfileOf()` reads Properties' `PropertyDirectory`.
- **Policy:** `TicketPolicy::shareWithOwner` (share and stop) = `tickets.manage` + `BuildingAccess` (another building's agent 404). No new permission (dropped while suspended through the central guard, not blocked while impersonating). `viewAsOwner` / `replyAsOwner` / `downloadAttachmentAsOwner` (and so the owner's `attachFiles`) require the ticket to be shared (404 otherwise).
- **Owner side:** `Owner\OwnerTickets` lists and finds shared tickets only; `Messages\TicketMessages` re-checks the share on the locked row for an owner's reply ("The agency no longer shares this repair with you."). Copy of the owner's list updated ("No repairs shared with you here yet…").
- **Tenant-contact switch removed from Tickets:** `OwnerTickets::sharesTenantContacts()` and the `TenantContactSharing` injection are gone; `OwnerTicketShow` always shows the tenant's phone and email. `TicketOwnerPortalTest` no longer uses `StatementTermsBook`. Nothing outside `Modules/Tickets` changed but docs (no Properties contract change was needed: `PropertySummary::ownerProfileId`/`ownerUserId` and `OwnerSummary::portalUserId` suffice).
- **Notifications** (P04-03 pipeline): `TicketRecipient::OWNER` side (`owner`), `TicketRecipients::owner()` (only while shared; the portal account linked to the building's owner profile there, not archived, and `viewAsOwner` must pass; never the actor), `Notices\SharedNoticeText` (`ticket.shared`, key `shared:{ULID}`), owner wording in `StatusNoticeText` and `MessageNoticeText` (in the agency's name, about their building), owner links to `/owner/tickets/{id}` ("Open the repair"). `TellTicketParties`: the owner on a share, on each status move and each public reply by staff or the tenant; never an internal note, an assignment, a move-in or their own reply.
- **Staff pages:** the ticket page's "Building owner" card (Shared with the owner by {name} on {date} + "Stop sharing" (confirm) / the owner's name and "does not see this ticket" + "Share with the owner" / "no owner on record" / "no owner portal account yet"); the Tickets list: "Shared with owner" badge, filter `owner=shared|hidden` ("Shared or not"), export column "Shared with owner on" (Lagos time, empty when not shared).

**Tests and evidence (local, MariaDB 11.4):**
- `composer ci` (`--parallel`, on `72f1cd5`): Pint passed; Larastan 0 errors; Pest **3,564 tests: 3,494 passed, 70 skipped (the Chrome/Gotenberg halves, no Gotenberg locally), 56,964 assertions**, 0 failed.
- With `APP_DEBUG=true`: `Modules/Tickets/tests` 234 tests, all passed but 4 skipped (the Chrome halves) after the last fix (`TicketMessageNotificationsTest` re-run 8/8).
- New: `Modules/Tickets/tests/Feature/TicketOwnerSharingTest.php` (9 tests, 177 assertions) and two tests in `TicketHandoverTest.php`.
- **AC: an unshared ticket is absent from `/owner/tickets` and its page and files are 404 for the owner** — › "keeps an unshared ticket out of /owner: not listed, its page and files 404, and no reply" (list, page, file and thumb routes 404; `viewAsOwner`/`replyAsOwner`/`downloadAttachmentAsOwner` 404; the writer refuses the reply).
- **AC: sharing makes it appear and notifies the owner's linked users once (a retried job sends nothing twice)** — › "sharing shows the ticket to the owner and tells the owner's linked user once; a retried job tells nobody twice" (Ngozi app + mail only; audit; owner-portal link; listener replayed twice → nothing; sharing again → nothing).
- **AC: status changes and public replies on a shared ticket notify the owner once each, internal notes and assignments never** — › "tells the owner of a shared ticket once per status change and public reply, never of a note, an assignment or their own reply" (owner's own reply, tenant reply, staff reply, note, assignment, in progress, resolved (subject/link), closed by the tenant; replayed status and message events → nothing; 6 owner notices).
- **AC: unsharing hides it again and stops notices** — › "tells the owner nothing once the ticket is no longer shared, and nobody of the unsharing" (nobody told; audit; 404s; the reply form opened before → 404; a stale reply loses on the locked row; later moves and replies skip the owner; sharing again tells once more).
- **AC: only `tickets.manage` members on the building share (an agent of another building, a tenant and the owner are refused); cross-org probes** — › "lets only tickets.manage members who work on the building share or stop sharing (refusals and cross-organization probes)" (other building's agent 404, tenant/owner/other org's owner 403, other org's context, the staff page and Livewire calls with others' sessions; nothing written; owner, admin, both agents allowed; suspended organization refused).
- **AC: existing tickets are unshared after the migration** — › "leaves every existing ticket unshared after the migration" (columns nullable, no default; a row inserted without them, as before the migration, is unshared and 404 for the owner; new tickets start unshared). DDL cannot run inside the test transaction, so the migration itself is not re-run in the test.
- **AC: a moved ticket arrives unshared** — `TicketHandoverTest` › "P04-06 acceptance: a moved ticket arrives unshared with the owner, even when it was shared where it came from" (forced share on the landlord's side) and › "… a ticket the agency shared with the owner goes back unshared on a one-way return …" (real share, no owner notice on the copy).
- **Staff list / page** — › "marks shared tickets on the staff list, filters by them and exports the column"; › "shows staff whether the ticket is shared and by whom, offers the action, and says so when the building has no owner" (also: the tenant's pages never mention it); › "lets an owner without a portal account be shared with: nobody is told, and they see it once they join".
- Existing tests changed (not weakened; the tickets are shared in their setup): `TicketOwnerPortalTest` (contacts now always shown instead of the switch case; a shared-area ticket shows none), `TicketThreadTest`, `TicketThreadAccessTest`, `TicketThreadPagesTest`, `TicketInternalNotesTest`, `TicketThreadPhoneScreenTest` (Chrome; skipped locally), `TicketMessageNotificationsTest` (owner reply and internal-note cases). New helpers `shareTicket()` / `unshareTicket()` in `helpers.php`.

**Decisions to confirm with the product owner** (beyond defaults 1–8, which were followed):
1. Column `shared_with_owner_by_user_id` (the house's `*_by_user_id` naming) rather than `shared_with_owner_by`.
2. One ability `shareWithOwner` covers both sharing and stopping.
3. Closed tickets can be shared (history for the owner); a ticket left behind by a building move cannot.
4. Sharing a shared ticket, or stopping an unshared one, changes nothing (no audit, nobody told). Sharing again after a stop is a new share: the owner is told again.
5. The owner is told about a move or reply when the ticket is shared at the time the queued listener runs (not frozen at the event).
6. Owner notices speak in the agency's name (like the tenant's), never a member's name; the move's note (e.g. resolution note) is passed on, since the owner sees it in the history anyway.
7. An owner with no portal account: sharing allowed, the staff card says nobody is told; once linked they see every shared ticket but get no catch-up notice for earlier shares.
8. A ticket still shared after its building lost its owner keeps "Shared … by …" and "Stop sharing"; sharing anew needs an owner.
9. Export column is the share time ("Shared with owner on", Lagos), empty when not shared, rather than Yes/No; list filter values `owner=shared|hidden`.
10. The owner's ticket page shows the tenant's phone and email always (phone only when there is no email).

- None with the docs. `UX_DESIGN.md` § 2's owner-portal row and ARCHITECTURE § 5's `property-owner` row are single lines P03-16 may also edit: expect a textual merge conflict there.

- Phone-only owners hear of shared tickets with P05 (WhatsApp/SMS), like tenants.
- A "catch-up" notice for an owner who joins after tickets were shared, if wanted.

### P04-05 — Complete (2026-10-28): open tickets move with the building on a handover (product owner 2026-10-28, replacing P04-01 decision 10): on a management handover's acceptance, its end and a one-way return, each ticket of a moved building that is not closed becomes the receiving organization's own ticket (new number, "Moved from {organization}, was TKT-000012", the moved tenant and lease there, public messages and files copied, never an internal note; resolved stays resolved, a draft stays a draft, others `submitted`, unassigned) and the one left behind is closed for good with "Building moved to {organization} on {date}", in the same transaction; the tenant and the receiving owners and administrators told once per ticket

**Delivered** ([ARCHITECTURE.md § 6.6 "As built (P04-05)", § 7](../ARCHITECTURE.md); [BILLING_PAYMENTS.md § 7.4 "Open maintenance tickets", § 7.8](../BILLING_PAYMENTS.md); [DATABASE_SCHEMA.md § 5 `tickets`](../DATABASE_SCHEMA.md); defaults 1–6 of [docs/plans/P04-subtasks.md](plans/P04-subtasks.md) "Settled by the product owner"):
- **Schema (1 migration, Tickets):** `2026_11_26_100101` `tickets.moved_from_ticket_id` (nullable FK `tickets`, restrict), `moved_from_organization` (160), `moved_from_number` (20), `moved_to_organization` (160), `moved_out_at` (TIMESTAMP nullable). No new table, no new model.
- **The mover:** `Modules\Tickets\Handover\TicketsMover` (`MovesBuildingRecords`, order 50: after Tenants 20, Leasing 30, portal links 35, Billing 40; before media 90), registered into Core's `BuildingRecordMovers` from `TicketsServiceProvider` (no module, no mover: the null default). Handovers imports nothing of Tickets. Runs for every `MoveDirection` (acceptance, end of a management, one-way return). Locks the moved buildings' tickets that are not closed (`FOR UPDATE`, oldest first); per ticket: tenant there from `BuildingMove::tenantIn()`, lease kept when it moved and that tenant holds it there (Leasing's `TenantLeases::forTenant`, read in the receiving organization), else none; counts `tickets_moved`, `ticket_drafts_moved`, `ticket_messages_copied`, `ticket_files_copied` on the handover's move audit entry.
- **`TicketService` (still the only writer of tickets):** `receiveMoved()` (receiving organization: next `DocumentNumberer` number, a draft none; same unit/building/source/raiser/category/priority/title/description/`submitted_at`; resolved keeps `resolved_at`, draft stays draft, else `submitted`; unassigned; one history row from nothing with the "Moved from" note; audit `ticket.moved_in`; `Events\TicketMovedIn` after commit, not for a draft); `closeMovedOut()` (leaving organization: any open state → closed with the note, `moved_to_organization`/`moved_out_at`, audit `ticket.moved_out`, `TicketStatusChanged` actor `move` (new `ACTOR_MOVE`); a draft is soft-deleted instead); every later move of a ticket left behind refused ("closed when its building moved to … It goes on there."), `reopenableUntil()` null, no "Reopen" on its staff page.
- **`TicketMessages::copyShared()`** (still the only writer of the thread): public messages oldest first with author, words and original times; files read from the disk and stored again through `MediaUploads` as the copy's evidence in the receiving organization's folder (custom property `copied_from_media_id`); internal notes never; one audit entry `ticket.thread_copied`; no `TicketMessagePosted`. An unreadable file fails the whole move (`BuildingMoveFailed`); each copied file's folder is removed on failure (`BuildingMove::onFailure`).
- **Notices:** `Listeners\TellTicketParties` handles `TicketMovedIn` with `Notices\MoveNoticeText` (`ticket.moved_in`, key `moved:{ULID}`): the tenant ("Your repair request is now handled by {agency}", old and new numbers; confirm-or-reopen reminder when resolved) and the receiving owners and administrators, once each per ticket, P04-03 rules (phone-only: nothing; no account: email only); a move with actor `move` tells nobody.
- **Pages:** the staff, portal and owner ticket pages show "Moved from {organization}, was TKT-…" on a copy and "Building moved to {organization} on {date}" on a ticket left behind (no new Tailwind classes).
- **Core:** `MovesBuildingRecords` doc (Tickets 50; a mover may create the receiving organization's own records and close the old ones instead of reassigning).

**Decisions to confirm with the product owner** (beyond defaults 1–6, which were followed):
1. **Also at the end of a management**, not only acceptance and the one-way return: tickets move with the building every time it moves, so a ticket moved there and back comes back as a new landlord ticket (the landlord's first one stays closed).
2. **The old draft is discarded** (soft delete, audited, `moved_out_at` set) rather than "closed": nobody there ever saw it and it has no number; the draft lives on in the receiving organization.
3. **Nobody on the side the ticket left is told** (not even its assignee): the handover's own notices tell both organizations' owners; agents lose the building anyway.
4. **The accepting owner is told too** (owners and administrators "once per moved ticket", literally), as is every admin; drafts tell nobody.
5. **Carried over:** `submitted_at` (when first reported), source and raiser, category, priority, description; `reopen_count` restarts at 0 and the old reopen history stays behind. Copied messages keep their original times and authors (staff of the other organization show by name).
6. **No tenant or lease on the copy** when the tenant's lease did not move (ended lease), so that tenant is not told; a staff ticket for a vacant/common unit moves without a tenant.
7. Names shown are the organization names at the move ("Moved from Eze Properties", "Building moved to Adeyemi Realty Ltd on 4 Jan 2027", date `j M Y` Lagos like other ticket dates).

If they still owe bills there, they see both (the old one closed with "Building moved to …", no reopen).
- `TicketStatus::canMoveTo` is unchanged; the move's close from any open state is a separate system move in `TicketService::closeMovedOut()` (documented as "the one move that skips `resolved`").

**Decisions to confirm with the product owner** (beyond default 9, which was followed):
1. **Phone-only tenants get nothing at all**, not even an in-app notice when they have a phone-only portal account (default 9 read literally; there is no in-app notification list in the portal yet either).
2. **Tenants with an email address but no portal account** are emailed (from the tenant record), without a link.
3. **Staff side when the assignee can no longer see the ticket** (taken off the building): owners and administrators are told instead.
4. **The auto-close tells the tenant and the staff side** (the assignee, else owners and administrators), also while the organization is suspended (the job runs as the system).
5. **Assignment:** only the new assignee is told; the previous assignee and unassigning (by hand or because the member left) tell nobody. A ticket raised already assigned tells its assignee once ("assigned to you"), not also "new ticket".
6. **What staff read:** owners/admins of an unassigned ticket and the assignee get every move; agents of the building who are not assigned are not told.
7. **In-app notifications** are stored (`notifications` table) but there is no bell/list in the app or portal yet (follow-up).
8. **Messages:** a reply's notice quotes a one-line excerpt (at most 140 characters) and says how many files came with it; an internal note's notice quotes nothing, even to staff (email leaves the system), and goes to the staff side only. The building owner (landlord) is not told about messages either (follow-up).
9. **Status tabs:** the staff inbox still opens on "All" (as the P04-01 list did); "Open" is the first tab.

**Delivered** ([ARCHITECTURE.md § 5 "Ticket threads (P04-02)" and "Owner portal" → Repairs, § 6.6 "As built (P04-02)", § 9](../ARCHITECTURE.md); [DATABASE_SCHEMA.md § 5 `ticket_messages`, § 1 `media`](../DATABASE_SCHEMA.md); [PLATFORM_CONSOLE.md § 6](../PLATFORM_CONSOLE.md); [UX_DESIGN.md § 1 portal rows](../UX_DESIGN.md); defaults 7 and 8 of [docs/plans/P04-subtasks.md](plans/P04-subtasks.md)):
- **Schema (1 migration, Tickets):** `2026_11_25_100101` `ticket_messages` (`organization_id`, `ticket_id` (FKs, restrict), `author_kind` tenant/staff/owner, `author_user_id` (null on delete), `body` (text, nullable: a message may be files only; ≤ 4,000 characters), `is_internal` (default false), `attachment_count`, timestamps (nullable); indexes `(organization_id, ticket_id, is_internal)`, `(organization_id, created_at)`). No soft deletes: messages are evidence (the model throws on change and delete).
- **`Modules\Tickets`:** enum `MessageAuthor`; model `TicketMessage` (`HasMedia`, collection `ticket_attachments`); `Messages\TicketMessages` (the writer: `reply` (with `internal` for a note), `replyAsTenant`, `replyAsOwner`; policy first, every file checked from its bytes before anything is stored, then `DB::transaction(…, 3)` with the ticket locked: draft → "Send the ticket first", closed → "its conversation has ended", owner while assigned → refused (checked again on the locked row); message, files through `App\Media\MediaUploads`, audit `ticket.message_posted` / `ticket.note_added` with the files' media ids and sha256, event); `Messages\TicketThread` (reader by `ThreadViewer` Staff/Tenant/Owner: internal notes and their files only for Staff, in the query itself; `attachment()` finds a file only among the messages the reader may see), `ThreadMessage`, `ThreadAttachment`; event **`Events\TicketMessagePosted`** (`ShouldDispatchAfterCommit`); `Owner\OwnerTickets` (+ `OwnerAgencyTickets`: the owner portal's path through Properties' `OwnerPortalAccess`, `OwnerProfileDirectory::buildingsOf`, `PropertyDirectory`, and OwnerStatements' `TenantContactSharing`); `Http\Controllers\TicketFileController` (workspace / portal / owner, file and thumb, a redirect to a short-lived signed media link, `#[BlockedWhileImpersonating]`); Livewire `TicketConversation` (staff, in the page's "Conversation" slot), `Portal\PortalTicketConversation` (after "Is it fixed?" / "Reopen"), `Owner\OwnerTicketIndex`, `Owner\OwnerTicketShow` (both `ReadOnlyOwnerPage`), `Owner\OwnerTicketConversation` (the owner portal's only writing component) and the trait `Livewire\Concerns\WritesTicketMessages` (body, files, 20 messages a minute); views `partials/thread`, `partials/message-files`, `livewire/ticket-conversation`, `livewire/portal/ticket-conversation`, `livewire/owner/*`; `TicketPolicy` abilities `reply`, `addInternalNote`, `viewInternalNotes`, `downloadAttachment`, `replyAsTenant`, `downloadAttachmentAsTenant`, `viewAsOwner`, `replyAsOwner`, `downloadAttachmentAsOwner`, `attachFiles` (the `download…` and `attachFiles` ones `#[BlockedWhileImpersonating]`); the provider adds the media collection `ticket_attachments` (evidence); routes `/o/{slug}/tickets/{id}/files/{media}[/thumb]`, `/portal/tickets/{id}/files/{media}[/thumb]`, `/owner/tickets/{id}/files/{media}[/thumb]`, `/owner/tickets`, `/owner/tickets/{id}`.

**Decisions to confirm with the product owner** (beyond defaults 7 and 8, which were followed):
1. **Files and impersonation:** GoProperty staff in a support session read the thread (internal notes included) and, in write mode, reply and add notes, but never add or open a ticket file (`attachFiles` and the `download…` abilities are `#[BlockedWhileImpersonating]`), as for every other uploaded file (proofs, receipts, statements). No permission was added or reclassified (P04-01 decision 6 holds for the words).
2. **Who sees what:** every member who sees the ticket in the workspace sees its internal notes (no narrower "notes" permission); notes are written by holders of `tickets.respond` (all three staff roles today).
3. **Owner's reply** allowed on any sent, not closed ticket while `assigned_to_user_id` is empty (resolved included); the owner's name and an "Owner" badge show to the tenant and staff. The owner sees every non-internal message, the status history with its notes, and who handles the ticket (the member's name).
4. **Messages may be files only** (no words); up to 4,000 characters; 20 messages a person a minute from the pages; a resolved ticket still takes messages, a closed one does not (reopen first).
5. **HEIC:** accepted and kept as uploaded (no thumbnail with GD; shown as a file chip, since most browsers cannot draw it); with `MEDIA_IMAGE_DRIVER=imagick` the media library may convert them.
6. **Placement:** on the portal the conversation comes after "Is it fixed?" / "Reopen" (the answer stays at the top on a phone); the tenant's reopen reason stays in "What happened" (history), not copied into the thread.
7. **Unread markers** (`read_at_by_tenant`, `read_at_by_staff` in the schema doc) not built: they belong with the inboxes / notifications.

**Contradictions / findings:**
- docs/plans/P04-subtasks.md and ARCHITECTURE § 6.6 (as of P04-01) gave the owner's read-only list in `/owner` to P04-03; the orchestrator assigned the whole owner piece to P04-02, and the docs now say so.
- DATABASE_SCHEMA § 5 listed `read_at_by_tenant` / `read_at_by_staff`; not built (decision 7); `author_kind` and `attachment_count` added.
- ARCHITECTURE § 9 said only JPEG, PNG, WebP, PDF, .xlsx and .csv were storable while default 7 asks for HEIC: Core's validator now takes HEIC/HEIF where a collection lists it (no collection but `ticket_attachments` does).
- The local `public/build` lacked the new Tailwind classes (`size-20` etc.), which made a thumbnail link measure as a small target at 360 px until `npm run build` (ignored files only); CI builds its own.

- Unread markers / "new reply" badges in the inboxes.
- Archive ("remove") a ticket attachment uploaded by mistake (hidden, kept as evidence), if the product owner wants it; today nothing removes one.

All TIMESTAMPs nullable or `useCurrent()`.

**Decisions to confirm with the product owner** (beyond docs/plans/P04-subtasks.md's defaults, which were followed):
1. **Shortcuts in the machine:** staff may go `submitted → in_progress` or `→ resolved` and `under_review → resolved` (a quick fix need not pass every step); `reopened` goes back to review, work or resolved.
2. **Staff may close a resolved ticket** (the tenant confirmed by phone, or there is no tenant: common areas), besides the tenant's "Confirm it's fixed" and the auto-close.
3. **Reopen window** counted from the latest resolution, to the second (14 × 24 h), for a resolved or a closed ticket; reopening keeps `resolved_at` until the next resolution.
4. **Number at sending:** a draft has no number; the number is taken when the tenant sends it (or at once for staff tickets), so discarded drafts leave no gap. Fixed prefix `TKT`, not in branding, no year.
5. **Drafts** are visible only to their tenant (404 to every member) and discarding one is a soft delete; no ticket is ever deleted otherwise. No event for drafts.
6. **Permissions:** the existing catalogue entries kept: `tickets.view` read (kept while suspended), `tickets.create` and `tickets.manage` writes; none blocked while impersonating (no money, credentials or contact details; read-only sessions refuse writes centrally). Tenants raise, send, confirm and reopen through their portal identity, refused while the organization is suspended or cancelled (the portal shows why); the auto-close job (system) still runs for a suspended organization.
7. **Agents' visibility** follows building narrowing (an assigned agent sees their buildings' tickets whoever they are assigned to; an unassigned agent sees all), not "assigned agent only" — as for leases and bills. Assignment needs `tickets.manage` and the building (owners, admins, the building's agents, unnarrowed agents); a member who leaves has their open tickets unassigned.
8. **Tenants choose the priority** (with plain-words hints), staff can change it; the unit picker for staff offers every unit of the building, the tenant picker the unit's draft and active leases' tenants ("moving in" for a draft), the first chosen by default.
9. **Settings page** under Settings → Maintenance tickets: seen with `viewSettings`, saved with `updateProfile` (owners and administrators).
10. **Management handover:** tickets stay with the organization that holds them (history); open tickets of a moved building do not move (follow-up below).

**Contradictions / findings:**
- DATABASE_SCHEMA § 5 named `raised_by_tenant_profile_id`; built as `tenant_profile_id` (the tenant the ticket is about, also for staff tickets) plus `source` and `raised_by_user_id`. `ticket_status_history` got `organization_id` and timestamps (the cross-organization probes need both).
- ARCHITECTURE § 5's "assigned agent" visibility read as building narrowing (task brief; decision 7).
- The local `public/build` was stale (built 26 Oct, missing classes such as `min-h-11`), which made every portal page fail the 360 px check against Chrome until rebuilt with `npm run build` (ignored files only); CI builds its own.
- PHPStan's default temp dir is shared by worktrees (a stale result cache from P04-04 crashed it); runs here used a private `TMPDIR`.
- The drill needed the permission cache warmed before the processes start (several processes writing `spatie.permission.cache` to the database cache inside their transactions deadlock); a real site's cache is warm.

**Follow-ups:**
- Move open tickets with their building on a management handover (a `MovesBuildingRecords` mover, renumbering in the receiving organization), or close them with a note there.
- Unassign tickets when an agent is taken off a building (only member removal is handled).
- A tenant cannot withdraw a sent ticket (staff resolve/close it).
- Plan limit `tickets.limit` not enforced (default 11, P06).

- Writer: add thread writes as new `TicketService` methods (or a sibling writer) that lock the ticket row and refuse a closed ticket ("closed ends the thread"); `TicketPolicy` is the place for `reply`/`viewInternalNotes` (`tickets.respond`); `TicketHistory` shows moves only.
- Page slots: `tickets::livewire.ticket-show` and `tickets::livewire.portal.ticket-show` each carry a Blade comment "Conversation slot" where the thread goes; the portal page must never show internal notes.

- Portal: `Portal\TenantTickets` (per agency, `find`, `homes`, `within`), tabs via `App\Navigation\PortalTabs`; owners' `/owner` list through Properties' `OwnerPortalAccess` + `ReadOnlyOwnerPage`.

CI runs the real Gotenberg.

**Decisions to confirm with the product owner** (defaults the product owner asked for, plus my choices):
1. Commission is a percentage of rent collected, per owner profile, stored in basis points (0–100 %, two decimals); unset → "Commission not set", 0. Computed per building and summed.
2. Utilities are shown but count toward the net due only when "utilities pass to the owner" is on (default off).
3. Only expenses marked "charged to owner" are deducted (new flag, default off), in the month of `spent_on`.
4. A payment counts in the month it was confirmed; a receipt void counts negative in the month voided; voided bills are listed but hold no money; refunds are netted.
5. **Tenant credit counts when it is used for a bill** (the overpayment part of a payment is not counted when paid; refunded or handed-over credit is therefore never counted). Applies to imported opening credit and credit brought over at a handover too.
6. **Bill types:** utility bills → utilities; rent, opening-balance and other bills → rent (commission applies to them).
7. Who marks/sets terms: `org-owner` and `org-admin` (new `owner_statements.manage`); viewing statements too (`owner_statements.view`); agents see none.
8. Only an **ended** month can be settled; date paid between the month's first day and today; a reference is required (1–100).
9. An expense cannot be charged into a settled month, nor a charged one there changed (amount, date, building, charge) until unsettled; descriptions can change; archiving is not refused (the snapshot already holds).
10. Statements list the last 12 months (older months reachable by URL); renewals are active leases ending today to today + 90 days (Lagos), with state "Renewal drafted" / "Rolls on (auto-renew)" / "Not renewed yet".
11. Tenant contacts (when shared) show in the owner's upcoming renewals and on the owner's lease page; never on the monthly statement or its PDF.
12. The statement PDF has no number or verification code (an account between agency and landlord); settled → kept once per settling; unsettled → rendered on each download (not kept). Not blocked while staff impersonate (figures and names only).
13. A statement covers the owner's current buildings in that organization; a building handed over or re-assigned to another owner leaves the old organization's unsettled months (settled snapshots keep it).

**Follow-ups:**
- An organization-wide "Owner statements" overview (all owners, this month) and owner notifications when a month is settled (P05).
- Payment refunds (`refunded` with a timestamp) should count negative in their month when built; credit applied from a payment whose receipt is voided later is not taken back (edge case).
- P08-01 dashboards can reuse `OwnerCollections` for collections vs billed.
- Charts in `/owner` (P08 dashboards).

- **AC: archiving a moved tenant with an unpaid bill or unsettled credit is refused; the record archives itself once both are clear; the "Moved to" marker shows** — `MovedTenantsTest` › "refuses to archive a moved tenant with an unpaid bill or unsettled credit, shows where they moved, and archives the record once both are clear" (Bisi at once; Kemi/Tunde/Musa refused with reasons, page shows marker and the refusal; Kemi archives on her payment (event), Tunde on his recorded refund (MovedTenantsChanged), Musa by the daily sweep when the payment event did not reach Tenants; Femi untouched; sweep idempotent), › "restores a moved tenant's archived record when the management ends"; `HandoverReturnTest` › "settles returned tenants' credit with the roles swapped…" (the agency's Tunde archives itself after the landlord confirms the credit arrived; marker "Moved to Eze Properties on 4 January 2027").
- **AC: pending invitations of moved tenants on the old side are revoked** — `MovedTenantsTest` › "revokes moved tenants' pending portal invitations and sign-in links on the side they left, audited" (Kemi's invitation and Tunde's sign-in link cancelled; Femi's (stays) pending; audit entries; Tunde's account carried to the agency).
- **AC: cross-org probes** — › "keeps a return out of reach of every other organization (cross-organization probes)" (unrelated landlord 404 / not found, the landlord cannot query the agency's row in its own scope, `MovedTenants` answers only the organization the tenants left); `CrossTenantAccessTest` still probes `ManagementHandover`, `HandoverBuilding`, `HandoverTenant` (no new table).
- **AC: `RentLoopTest` and every existing handover test still pass** — `tests/Feature/Journeys` 3/3; `Modules/Handovers/tests` all pass. Existing tests changed (not weakened): `HandoverMoveTest` first test now counts the landlord's records with archived ones (5) and asserts Bisi's record archived itself (the new rule); `BuildingMoveTest` mover order includes `LeftPortalLinksRevoker`.

**Decisions to confirm with the product owner:**
1. **Safeguard (default):** a return goes only to an organization where an `org-owner` is the account linked to the buildings' owner record in the agency; all offered buildings must share that account ("one owner's buildings at a time"); re-checked on acceptance. Unlinked → refused, invite to the owner portal first.
2. **Tenant credit on a return (default):** decision 11 mirrored: the agency holds it and chooses "pass to the landlord" (default) or "refund"; the landlord confirms receipt to open it. Stored as `pass_to_agency` ("pass to the receiving organization").
3. **Auto-archive trigger:** event-driven (`PaymentConfirmed`, `BillVoided`, Core `MovedTenantsChanged` after acceptance and credit settlement; after commit) plus the daily sweep `tenants-archive-moved` 03:50 as the backstop. A record archives at acceptance when nothing is owed (e.g. a draft lease's tenant). "Clear" also needs no live lease left in that organization.
4. **Archive rule scope:** only moved tenants' records get the bills/credit refusal; other tenants archive as before (only a live lease blocks). "Credit" = the tenant's credit balance there (any credit, not only the handover line).
5. **The landlord's owner record in their own organization is not linked** (`OwnerLinks::link()` is not called): the landlord is an `org-owner` there. Matched by linked user, else email, else created as a company record from the organization's details.
6. **Return matching:** pairs from every accepted handover between the two organizations, both directions, newest wins; the original record's details (phone etc.) are left as they are; an archived original is restored.
7. **Portal links:** revoked only when the tenant no longer holds a lease on the side they left; the accepting owner is the audit causer (none for the hourly end). The new side re-invites; existing portal accounts are carried on the copy.
8. **Marker** also shows on the agency's record after a one-way return ("Moved to {landlord}"), and for a management still on; it disappears when a management ends and the tenant comes back.
9. `rent_collected_by` made nullable (null on returns) rather than storing a value that does not apply; `notice_days` 0 on returns.

**Contradictions / findings:**
- None blocking. The P03-14 note "the landlord's original is not archived" is superseded by the PO's 2026-10-28 rule (it now archives itself when clear); `HandoverMoveTest`'s first test was updated for that.
- PLATFORM_CONSOLE § 1B's column names changed to "Offered by" / "Offered to" (a return reverses landlord/agency).

**Follow-ups:**
- Credit the agency holds for tenants coming back at the end of a management (still not covered by decision 11; P03-14 follow-up).
- An in-app notifications list (unchanged); phone-only owners get no handover email.
- The "Moved to" marker is not shown on the tenants list (only the tenant page).

Reviewed the return safeguard (`HandoverService::returnOwner`: every building needs a linked owner account, one account for the whole offer, and that account must own the receiving organization; checked at offer and again at acceptance). The agent's `composer ci` ran on `db63381`; `f8327f3` only deleted an unused helper. **GitHub Actions** run [40681187616](https://github.com/thekingchild/Goproperty/actions/runs/40681187616): green on MySQL 8.0/8.4 and MariaDB 10.6/11.4 plus the Old PHP guard, 3,237 passed with **none skipped**.

### P03-14 — Complete (2026-10-28): management handover: P02-04's billing transfers reworked into handovers of chosen buildings (new `Modules\Handovers`: offer with building picker, rent collection and notice, inbox, accept screen with the bill before/after, accept / decline / withdraw / 14-day expiry, give / withdraw notice, hourly end), the Core `App\Tenancy\Moves` contract and sanctioned organization move called in one transaction (Properties, Tenants, Leasing, Billing, media), the per-tenant credit settlement (decision 11), the landlord linked as owner in `/owner`, console visibility; `billing_transfer_requests` dropped and its pages redirected

**Delivered** ([BILLING_PAYMENTS.md § 7](../BILLING_PAYMENTS.md) incl. the new § 7.8 "How it is built"; [ARCHITECTURE.md § 4, § 5, § 6.1, § 7, § 8](../ARCHITECTURE.md); [DATABASE_SCHEMA.md § 1, § 4, integrity rule 4](../DATABASE_SCHEMA.md); [PLATFORM_CONSOLE.md § 1B, § 6](../PLATFORM_CONSOLE.md); `docs/plans/P02-P03-subtasks.md` P03-14):
- **Decision: its own module, `Modules\Handovers`** (`module:make`, trimmed, in `modules_statuses.json`): a handover is about who manages buildings, not GoProperty's subscription. `Modules\SaasBilling` keeps only the bill quote, now a contract: `Contracts\SubscriptionQuotes` (`for(org, extraUnits)`, `cheapestFor`, `nextBillingDay` = the 1st of next month, Lagos) + `Contracts\MonthlyBill`, implemented by `Quotes\MonthlyBills` (P02-04's class, moved). Everything else of P02-04's billing transfers was removed (service, model, pages, lists, notifications, job, policy, tests); `/o/{slug}/billing-transfers` and `/o/{slug}/billing-transfers/{n}` answer 301 to `/o/{slug}/handovers` (members only; outsiders 404); the old `/billing-transfers/{token}` links are gone (404).
