- **Core, `App\Tenancy\Moves` (the sanctioned organization move):** `MovesBuildingRecords` (`order()`, `move(BuildingMove)`), `BuildingRecordMovers` (registry, singleton; modules register from their providers like import targets), `OrganizationMoves::run()` (inside the caller's transaction; each mover starts in the leaving organization's context; on failure undoes file work and rethrows; old-folder removal registered `DB::afterCommit`), `BuildingMove` (inputs + what moved, tenant map, owner profile, counts; `reassign(model, ids, attributes, withTrashed)` = the ONLY way `organization_id` changes: only while the move runs, only on `BelongsToOrganization` models, through the leaving organization's scope, refused (`BuildingMoveFailed`, whole move rolled back) when any row is not there; `BelongsToOrganization`'s save guard and the scope are unchanged), `MoveDirection`, `BuildingMoveFailed`. `App\Media\MediaMover` (order 90): every moved HasMedia record's files copied `{from}/{uuid}` → `{to}/{uuid}` on their disk(s), media rows reassigned, old folder removed after commit, copy removed on failure.
- **Movers (one per module, registered from each provider):** Properties `Handover\BuildingsMover` (10: buildings locked and checked; archived units too; slug kept when free else next free, on return from the name; taken `external_ref` cleared; `import_batch_id` cleared; agent assignments deleted; handover: landlord's owner profile = the one linked to their user, else one with their email, else a new company profile from the landlord organization's details, linked with `OwnerLinks::link()`; return: previous owner restored, `OwnerLinks::unlink()` unless they own other buildings there). Tenants `Handover\TenantsMover` (20: the tenants of the moved live leases through Leasing's new `Contracts\LiveLeases`; matched by phone there (an archived match restored, portal link filled when empty) or copied with contacts, next of kin, encrypted ID, KYC and `user_id`; the original stays with its history; copies audited `tenant.brought_over`). Leasing `Handover\LeasesMover` (30: draft and active leases, tenant re-pointed, taken `contract_number`/`external_ref` cleared, `import_batch_id` cleared, `next_billing_date` kept; their `lease_deposit_entries`; also implements `LiveLeases`). Billing `Handover\UtilitiesMover` (40: settings re-pointed to the receiving organization's type by code, else name, else a copy made there (audited `utility_type.brought_over`); taken `external_ref` cleared; every reading, removed ones too; billed readings keep `bill_id`).

**Decisions relied on (to confirm with the product owner):**
- **PO rules (handover):** live building-linked records move (buildings, units, tenants of live leases, draft/active leases with deposits, utility settings with their billing state, readings, files); history stays with its writer (bills, items, payments, allocations, receipts, credit entries/balances, lease contracts, verification codes, counters, expenses and their receipts, ended leases, opening balances, invitations, audit). **PO rule (evidence):** evidence files of moved records (meter photos) move with their records (copied, then the old copy removed after commit: no evidence is lost); issued documents and other evidence never move.
- **Decision 2 (lookup pattern):** the receiving side reads handovers only through `IncomingHandovers` (ids by `to_organization_id`, then load in the offering context); the link by token hash; no scope removed. The open-handover and history checks read ids only.
- **Decision 3 (no SMS/WhatsApp):** handover notices are mail + in-app only.
- **Decision 8 (gating):** nothing gated; handovers are in every plan as far as BILLING § 1.2 says.
- **Decision 11 (tenant credit, settled):** built as stated: lines refreshed at offer and acceptance; landlord's choice (default pass to the agency) changeable until settled; refund recorded by the landlord; pass-to-agency confirmed by the agency, closing and opening in one transaction for the exact amount; unsettled credit stays on the landlord's books, shown to both.
- **My choices:**
  - New module `Modules\Handovers`; SaasBilling keeps the quote behind a contract.
  - **`billing_transfer_requests` dropped** (replace, not rename): an accepted transfer moved nothing and maps to no handover state; the audit log keeps the history. Invitation kind value `org_billing_transfer` kept (ARCH § 6.1 "becomes the handover's link"), enum case renamed.
  - **New permission `handovers.manage`** (owner only, blocked while impersonating, not kept while suspended: a suspended organization does not see its handovers page). Alternative: keep viewing allowed while suspended with a second permission.
  - **Tenants are matched or copied, never moved**: their original records hold the landlord's history (bills, receipts, credit). The landlord's original is not archived (its unpaid bills are still collected against it).
  - Only `draft` and `active` leases move ("active and reserved"; a reservation is a draft). Ended leases stay as history (their pages at the landlord no longer name the moved unit).
  - A taken `contract_number` is cleared on the moved lease (the issued contract keeps it); `import_batch_id` is cleared on every moved row (the other organization's batch cannot roll it back).
  - Billed readings keep `bill_id` (so the agency never bills the same consumption again).
  - The landlord's user = the owner who offered (`initiated_by_user_id`); a new owner profile is a company named after the landlord organization, with that user's email and phone.
  - On a return the slug comes from the building's name (usually its old one) and the previous owner profile is restored.
  - Bill change "from your next billing day" = the 1st of next month (BILLING § 3); the amount checked on accept is the bill after.
  - Notice 0 days ends at the next hourly run; `ends_at` = notice time + days.
  - Both sides told on every change, including the offering side on its own offer, withdrawal and notice; offering-side notices go to every owner (not only the one who offered).
  - Console list with `customers.view`.

**Contradictions / findings:**
- BILLING § 7.1 "an agency returns buildings it holds without a handover to the landlord's own organization": its state machine does not say where such buildings go when that "handover" later ends (they would already be at the landlord). **Not built**; offers are landlord → agency only (an agency's `create` is refused). Product owner to define.
- DB § 1 planned `management_handover_buildings.unit_count` only; added name/address snapshots (each side can read the building only while it holds it), the previous owner profile, and a third table for decision 11.
- P03-04b's "open bills of moved leases": as built (decision): they stay with the landlord, which collects them; the tenant now owes two organizations for different periods. The landlord's old bill pages may show the moved building/unit as removed (names are on the bill details snapshot).
- ARCH § 6.1 said modules register movers "the way import targets are": done, but the contract lives in Core (`App\Tenancy\Moves`) so Core's media library can implement it.
- No organization deletion path exists yet; "the landlord's organization is kept while a handover is active or ending" (§ 7.6) must be enforced by whoever builds closing an organization.

**Follow-ups:**
- The return direction of § 7.1 (agency → landlord's own organization) once its end semantics are defined.
- Revoke moved tenants' pending portal invitations / sign-in links on the old side (P03-01b note); the agency re-invites.
- Credit the agency holds for returned tenants at the end of a management (decision 11 covers acceptance only).
- "Landlord collects rent" when a per-building collection account exists (P03-07 follow-up).
- Closing an organization (P06/NDPA tooling) must refuse while a handover from it is active or ending.
- An in-app notifications list (rows are stored; P02-04 follow-up still open); phone-only owners get no handover email.
- P06-01: quotes from the subscription/plans tables; proration when it ships (§ 1.4).

**P03 exit check (IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md § 3, P03 phase exit):**
- All P03 tasks complete: P03-01 … P03-13 and P03-15 Complete in `IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md` § 3 (2026-10-27/28); P03-14 this entry (all its ACs pass above).
- End-to-end journey: `tests/Feature/Journeys/RentLoopTest.php` (building → 3 units bulk → tenant → lease → scheduler issues the invoice on its billing day → proof from the portal → confirmation → numbered receipt, next period's receipt number increments → tenant sees it in the portal) re-run on this branch after the change: 3 tests, 3 passed, 366 assertions (alone), and again in the `APP_DEBUG=true` run for the annual, weekly and semi-annual cycles; also inside the full `composer ci` run.
- So P03's exit criteria pass locally on MariaDB 11.4; the orchestrator's GitHub CI run (MySQL 8.0/8.4, MariaDB 10.6/11.4) is the remaining gate before marking the phase complete.

The agent's `composer ci` on the final commit `2232200` stood as the local gate. **GitHub Actions** run [40647031847](https://github.com/thekingchild/Goproperty/actions/runs/40647031847): green on MySQL 8.0/8.4 and MariaDB 10.6/11.4 plus the Old PHP guard, 3,213 passed with **none skipped** on each, so P03's phase exit holds on all four databases.

### P03-15 — Complete (2026-10-28): owner portal (first cut) and owner invitations: owner profiles linked to users (`property-owner` only, never the workspace), owner invitations by email / WhatsApp stand-in / link + QR without account takeover, "Remove access", and the read-only `/owner` across organizations (buildings, units and occupancy, active leases with the tenant's name only, bills with their status), each organization in its current branding, 404 for anything not the owner's own, every Livewire call refused, 360 px browser pass

**Delivered** ([ARCHITECTURE.md § 3–5, § 6.1, § 8, § 10](../ARCHITECTURE.md); [ONBOARDING_COMPLIANCE.md § 1 "After login", § 2 "Property owners"](../ONBOARDING_COMPLIANCE.md); [BILLING_PAYMENTS.md § 7.4](../BILLING_PAYMENTS.md); [DATABASE_SCHEMA.md § 1](../DATABASE_SCHEMA.md); [PLATFORM_CONSOLE.md § 6](../PLATFORM_CONSOLE.md); [UX_DESIGN.md § 2](../UX_DESIGN.md); `docs/plans/P02-P03-subtasks.md` P03-15):

- **Properties module, `OwnerPortal\*`:**

One bind line each in the providers; the bill generator is untouched. Tenants' names through the existing `TenantDirectory::findMany` (name only).

With `APP_DEBUG=true` and the local Gotenberg stand-in: 61 tests, 61 passed, 749 assertions.

Ran locally against a private copy of P03-11b's Gotenberg stand-in (installed Chrome, port 3997, not committed) after `npm run build`: 2/2 passed; P03-11b's negative control passed against it too.

**Decisions relied on (to confirm with the product owner):**
- **Decision 2 (lookup pattern):** the owner portal's only unscoped read is `owner_profiles (organization_id, id) by user_id`; everything else inside `OrganizationContext::run` through the scope and the modules' contracts.
- **Decision 3 (no SMS/WhatsApp):** owner invitations by email, the `WhatsAppSender` stand-in (never offered in production) or link/QR; never SMS.
- **Decision 4 (people and roles), extended to owners:** unlike tenants, owners **do** get an `organization_user` row holding `property-owner` only — the docs ask for it (ARCH § 5 "a user whose only roles … are `property-owner`", BILLING § 7.4 "gets the `property-owner` role there", the task's `roles = [property-owner]`; spatie refuses a role to a non-member). The portal requires both the profile link and the role.
- **Decision 8 (gating):** nothing gated (owner portal in every plan per BILLING § 1.2 as far as stated; WhatsApp is Growth+ → P06-01 follow-up, as P03-01b).
- **Decision 9 (narrowing):** inviting is `owners.manage` (owners and administrators, never narrowed); agents only see the section.
- **PO rule (handover):** the landlord's view is `OwnerLinks::link()` in the receiving organization (see P03-14 notes).
- **PO rule (evidence):** the portal opens no file and archives nothing.
- **My choices:**
  - **Tenant detail for owners: the name only** (on leases and bills); never phone, email, ID, next of kin, KYC, payment reference, pay page, bank details, invoices or receipts (they print the tenant's contacts). Product owner: confirm, or say what more owners may see (e.g. payment history in P08 statements).
  - No new permission: `owners.manage` + `#[BlockedWhileImpersonating]` on the policy method and every action (P03-01b needed `tenants.portal_access` because agents hold `tenants.manage`; owners are managed by owners/administrators only).
  - **"Remove access"** added (not in the row): without it the only way to cut an owner off was archiving the owner record, which is refused while they own an active building.
  - WhatsApp and link invitations need the owner's phone number (the link is bound to it, so no address-less, number-less accounts are made).
  - One owner profile per user in an organization (unique index; a second invitation for the same person there is refused with its own page).
  - Archived buildings stay listed, marked "No longer managed"; draft leases are never shown; a building page lists the latest 50 bills with the count.
  - Landing: a workspace member lands in the workspace (owner portal one tap away in the sidebar / switcher / bottom bar); someone with no workspace lands in their only portal, or on the picker listing both when they are a tenant and an owner.
  - Owners hold the `property-owner` catalogue grants (`properties.view`, `bills.view`, …) from P01-01, but nothing uses them: the workspace refuses them and the portal checks `viewAsOwner`.

**Orchestrator review / CI fix:** after `main` was merged in (`de42833`), GitHub CI failed one test on all four databases against real Gotenberg: `OwnerPhoneScreenTest` › the new-account invitation page read `viewport 6723, overflow 15, small 14`, which is the page's own navy top bar (#0F1A43) at the probe pixel, not the probes. That page was the only owner page taller than the 360 × 900 capture (937 px) and the only one with `autofocus` (on the password field), so Chrome scrolled it, and Gotenberg's clip (document coordinates) missed the fixed probes; the local stand-in captures the viewport, so it did not show this. Fixed the page (`6027a08`): no autofocus (on a phone it also opened the keyboard and scrolled past who sent the invitation) and a shorter intro, so the form fits one screen like the tenant invitation (measured: 900 px, nothing focused, no scrollbar). `composer ci` afterwards: Pint passed; Larastan 0 errors; Pest 3,124 tests: 3,065 passed, 59 skipped (Gotenberg halves), 40,388 assertions, 0 failed; the owner tests with `APP_DEBUG=true` and the stand-in: 61 passed.

**Orchestrator review:** merged `main` into the branch twice (P03-06b, then P03-13b); resolved a BILLING_PAYMENTS.md § 7 conflict (utility billing state and the `OwnerLinks::link()` detail both kept) and a Billing provider import conflict (both contracts bound). GitHub CI run [40630118621](https://github.com/thekingchild/Goproperty/actions/runs/40630118621) then failed the 360 px check of the new-account invitation page in real Chrome (the local stand-in had passed): the page was taller than the screen and autofocused the password field. The agent removed the autofocus and shortened the intro; the page now fits one screen. `composer ci` locally: Pint passed, Larastan 0 errors, Pest 3,150 tests: 3,091 passed, 59 skipped (Gotenberg). **GitHub Actions** run [40635168840](https://github.com/thekingchild/Goproperty/actions/runs/40635168840): green on all four databases, 3,150 passed with **none skipped**.

**Contradictions / findings:**
- Owners as members vs tenants as non-members: the docs say owners get the role (so membership), decision 4 covered tenants only. Built as members; noted in ARCH § 4/§ 5 and DB § 1.
- `MemberInvitations` treated any pending invitation to an address (tenant or owner kinds too) as blocking a member invitation, and any membership as "already a member" — fixed for kinds and for owner-only members (covered by the "invited to work in the workspace too" test).
- The P03-15 IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN AC "a landlord whose buildings were handed over (P03-14) sees them there" can only be proven by P03-14; `OwnerLinks::link()` is what it should call.

**For P03-14 (management handover):**
- `owner_profiles.user_id` is a live link: when buildings move, the receiving organization's owner profile (matched or created) is linked to the landlord's user with `OwnerLinks::link()` (it also adds the membership with `property-owner`); mind the unique `(organization_id, user_id)` — match the landlord's existing profile there first. On return, `OwnerLinks::unlink()` in the agency's organization if the landlord owns nothing else there.
- `invitations` of kind `owner` are the issuing organization's history: revoke a moved owner's pending one on the old side; the receiving side re-invites if needed.
- No other table added.

**Follow-ups:**
- P08: monthly statements, charts, payments history and the statement PDF in `/owner`; owner notifications.
- P05-06: the real WhatsApp driver (event `owner_invitation`, variables above); owner password reset by WhatsApp; no one-time sign-in link exists for owners (owners with email reset themselves).
- P06-01: gate WhatsApp invitations to Growth+.
- A shared Core helper for the invitation takeover rules (tenant and owner acceptances are parallel copies today).
- "Add an email" for phone-only accounts (P03-11a follow-up) applies to owners too.
- Tickets for owners ("respond if no agent assigned", ARCH § 5) come with P04.

**Next:** P03-14 (management handover), then the phase-exit journey test.

### P03-13b — Complete (2026-10-28): import of balances, credits, utilities and history: the Opening balances sheet (one `opening_balance` bill per amount owed, due on the as-of date — payable and in arrears at once, never billed per period — or a tenant credit entry the next bill takes), the Utilities sheet (utility types added when missing, units' settings with overrides, the last meter reading as the starting reading, `next_billing_date` always set so nothing is back-billed), the optional Payment history sheet (confirmed history that settles nothing, no receipt unless the row asks, nobody told), rollback and "used since" for all three, the three sheets in the template; and the P03 phase-exit journey test (`RentLoopTest`, annual / weekly / semi-annual)

With it the parent **P03-13 is Complete** (P03-13a ongoing leases + P03-13b balances, credits, utilities, history: the P03-13 AC — "importing a lease that started in 2023 with paid until 31/12/2026 creates no past bills; the first generated bill covers the period from 01/01/2027; no reminders for past dates; no tenant contacted automatically" — passed in P03-13a and still passes; this task adds opening balances, tenant credits, utility settings and payment history to the same no-back-billing, no-contact rules).

**Delivered** ([DATA_IMPORT.md](../DATA_IMPORT.md) § 1 rows 6–8, § 2 "Undo", § 3 (as built); [ARCHITECTURE.md § 6.12](../ARCHITECTURE.md) "Balances, utilities and history (P03-13b)"; [DATABASE_SCHEMA.md § 4](../DATABASE_SCHEMA.md) `bills` (opening balances), `payments` (imported history), `tenant_credit_entries` (opening credit), new `opening_balances`, `utility_types.import_batch_id`, § 8A, integrity rule 3; [BRANDING_DOCUMENTS.md § 5](../BRANDING_DOCUMENTS.md) (receipts of imported history); AGENT.md § 3 rules 2, 3, 5, 6, 7 and § 4 "Imports"; `docs/plans/P02-P03-subtasks.md` P03-13b):
- **Schema (one change each, no TIMESTAMP and no JSON column added):** `Modules/Payments/database/migrations/2026_11_20_080201_create_opening_balances_table` (`opening_balances`: tenant, nullable building/unit/lease FKs, `as_of_date`, signed `amount_kobo`, `description`, `bill_id` / `credit_entry_id` FKs, provenance, soft deletes; unique `(organization_id, external_ref)`, org-leading indexes); `Modules/Billing/database/migrations/2026_11_20_080101_add_import_batch_id_to_utility_types_table` (nullable, indexed `(organization_id, import_batch_id)`). `Models\OpeningBalance` (organization-scoped, soft deletes, never force deleted) added to `tests/Support/OrganizationScopedModels.php`.
- **Billing — `Contracts\OpeningBalanceBills`** (new; `issue`, `withdraw`, `restore`; `Contracts\OpeningBalanceBill` value; implemented by `Bills\OpeningBalanceBillRecords`, bound in `BillingServiceProvider`): ONE `opening_balance` bill through `BillIssuer` (number, payment reference, verification code, branding and parties kept, audited, `BillIssued`), **due on the as-of date** (overdue at once when it has passed), no period, billable = the tenant's lease on the unit when there is one (else the unit; the lease's number and cycle printed), one line "Opening balance" (note = the row's description, meta `as_of`), `import_batch_id`. `withdraw()` soft deletes it for a rollback (refused once paid or void; audited `bill.import_rolled_back`), `restore()` brings it back with its number for a re-import (status worked out again; audited `bill.import_restored`). `BillDraft::$importBatchId` (new, optional); **`BillEvent::$imported`** (new, default false; `BillService::eventIds()` sets it from `import_batch_id`) so P05's notices can leave imported bills alone.
- **Billing — `Import\UtilitiesImportTarget`** (key `utilities`, sheet "Utilities", order 70; singleton, registered from `BillingServiceProvider`). Columns: Your ID, Building, Unit, Utility (name or code), Metered (Yes/No), Amount, Billing cycle, Unit of measure, Last meter reading, Paid until (dropdowns: Metered, Billing cycle, Unit of measure). Types found by name or code without case (archived included); a missing one is added by `UtilityTypes::addForImport()` (code made from the name, unique: `SECURITY-LEVY`; the row's amount and cycle as defaults; metered ones need a unit of measure) and audited; one archived by a rollback is brought back. Settings written by `UnitUtilitySettings::import()`: overrides only where they differ from the type, the last reading as `starting_reading`, `effective_from` = the day after "Paid until" (else the import day) and **`next_billing_date` always set** = the first period starting on or after the import day (calculator only), so `UtilityBillGenerator` never bills a past period. Matching: Your ID, then unit + utility (the unit by the id behind the lookup key, so a unit the file brings back is found); a re-import updates a setting with no bill and no reading, switches a switched-off one back on (Restore), and refuses new terms once it is billed or read. `rollBackImport()` on both writers: settings switched off (never deleted), types archived, audited. `BillUsage` also counts a unit named by a utility setting outside the batch.
- **Payments — `Import\OpeningBalancesImportTarget`** (key `opening_balances`, sheet "Opening balances", order 60; singleton). Columns: Your ID, Tenant, Building, Unit, Amount owed (negative = credit), As-of date, Description. Tenants through `Tenants\Contracts\ImportedTenants`, units through `Properties\Contracts\ImportedUnits`, the lease through `Leasing\Contracts\TenantLeases::forTenant()` (the active lease on the unit, else the latest signed one), bills through Billing's `OpeningBalanceBills` and `BillLedger` — no other module's table. An amount owed → the bill; a credit → `TenantCredit::addOpeningCredit()` (`+ opening_balance` entry under the tenant's credit row lock), taken by the next bill (`ApplyCreditToNewBill`). Matching: Your ID, then tenant + unit + as-of date (rolled-back rows included → Restore; a live match with the same amount → Update, nothing changes; another amount → error). Rollback withdraws the bills and reverses credits with `− import_rolled_back` entries (`TenantCredit::reverseOpeningCredit()`, refused if the credit is no longer all there), soft deletes the rows, audited (`opening_balance.imported` / `.import_restored` / `.import_rolled_back`, log `payments`).
- **Payments — `Import\PaymentHistoryImportTarget`** (key `payment_history`, sheet "Payment history", order 80; singleton). Columns: Your ID, Tenant, Date paid, Amount, Method (Bank transfer, Cash, POS, Paystack; "Transfer", "Card", "Online"… understood), Reference, Note, Issue receipt (Yes/No; dropdowns Method, Issue receipt). `PaymentService::importHistory()` (new): a confirmed payment, **source `import`** (new `PaymentSource::Import`, "Imported history"), confirmed by nobody, **no allocation, no credit**, audited `payment.imported`; `PaymentConfirmed` gains **`issueReceipt`** (default true; false unless the row says Yes): `IssueReceipt` issues nothing without it, `TellTenantAboutPayment` never tells the tenant about an imported payment. A receipt asked for prints "From the agency's earlier records" / "Imported from earlier records" and "sent via" nothing. Matching: Your ID, then tenant + date + amount + method + reference (rolled-back ones restored; a re-import issues a receipt asked for later). `rollBackImportedHistory()` soft deletes the batch's history while it has no receipt: the `Payment` model now allows exactly that (and the restore) for source `import` without a receipt — DATA_IMPORT § 2 "Undo" sanctions it; DATABASE_SCHEMA integrity rule 3 documents the exception.
- **Payments — `CreditReason::OpeningBalance`, `ImportRolledBack`**; `PaymentUsage` also counts a tenant, unit or lease named by another batch's opening balance.
- **Exact dry-run counts:** the Tenants module's `ImportedTenantLookup` now keys a tenant the same file adds by its Tenants-sheet row (`row:N`), whether a row names them by phone or by Your ID, so duplicates in a file are caught in the dry run and the counts match the commit (P03-13a's Leases sheet benefits too). Natural-key matches use the id behind the lookup keys, so a tenant or unit the file brings back after a rollback is matched the same way in the dry run and the commit.
- **Leasing:** `Contracts\BillingCycle::fromText()` (the spreadsheet cycle parser moved from `LeasesImportTarget::cycle()`, which now delegates; used by the Utilities sheet).
- **Template:** the three sheets follow Leases; their examples (the example tenant owes ₦250,000 as at 30/09/2026; Palm Court Flat 1 pays a ₦10,000 monthly security levy; a ₦1,500,000 transfer on 15/12/2025, no receipt) import with the other examples.
- **Journey test:** `tests/Feature/Journeys/RentLoopTest.php` (see Tests).
- **Docs:** DATA_IMPORT.md § 1 rows 6–8, § 2 "Undo", § 3; ARCHITECTURE.md § 6.12; DATABASE_SCHEMA.md § 4 (`utility_types`, `bills`, `payments`, `tenant_credit_entries`, `opening_balances`), § 8A, integrity rule 3; BRANDING_DOCUMENTS.md § 5.

**Orchestrator review:** the branch already contained `main` (`c4a4892`). **GitHub Actions** run [40632286186](https://github.com/thekingchild/Goproperty/actions/runs/40632286186): green on MySQL 8.0/8.4 and MariaDB 10.6/11.4, 3,089 passed with none skipped — including the phase-exit `tests/Feature/Journeys/RentLoopTest.php` for annual, weekly and semi-annual cycles.

**Decisions to confirm** (orchestrator decisions relied on, and choices made here):
- Relied on **decision 1 (cycle maths):** the utilities' `next_billing_date` and the unbilled days come only from `CycleCalculator` (`nextPeriodStart`, `daysAfter`); the interval-maths scan stays green. The journey's billing days are the calculator's (`billingDate`, lead 7).
- Relied on **decision 3 (no SMS):** nothing is sent by these imports at all; history receipts are never sent.
- Relied on **decision 7:** history receipts are numbered per organization through `DocumentNumberer` (unique `(organization_id, number)`).
- Relied on **decision 8 (gating):** nothing new to gate (import is every plan's).
- Relied on **decision 9 (narrowing):** imports are owner/admin only; opening-balance bills carry `property_id`, so narrowed agents see them like every bill.
- Relied on the **PO rules**: handover (below); evidence — no evidence file is created or removed by these imports (an issued invoice/receipt PDF is kept as for any bill/receipt).
- Relied on the brief's **MySQL JSON**, **fixed test data** (every tenant, owner and member in the new tests has a fixed name, phone and email) and **TIMESTAMP** rules (no timestamp column added).
- **Opening balance due date = the as-of date** (overdue at once when the date has passed; that is what "counts as arrears" means while there is no arrears report: the overdue list, the bill's OVERDUE badge, first in a payment's allocation). No `BillOverdue` event is sent for a bill overdue at issue (nothing "became" overdue on a past date). Alternative: due on the import day. Product owner: confirm.
- **One opening balance per tenant + unit + as-of date**; a unit is required for an amount owed (the bill is for a unit, and narrowing needs the building) and optional for a credit. The bill is for the tenant's lease on that unit when there is one (active first, else the latest signed). An issued opening-balance bill is never changed by a re-import (void it and import a corrected row with another as-of date or Your ID).
- **Credit on the same sheet as a negative amount** (DATA_IMPORT § 1 row 6 "amount owed (or credit)"): no separate Tenant credits sheet.
- **Payment history = history only:** confirmed, source `import`, no allocation and no credit — what the tenant still owed or had in credit is the Opening balances sheet's, so history never counts twice. Alternative (allocate to imported bills) was rejected as double counting. "Issue receipt" is a per-row Yes/No column (not a batch-wide checkbox); a receipt issued for history is numbered on the import day and **still never sent** (the tenant sees it in the portal once invited). Listeners of `PaymentConfirmed` (P05 notices, P06-09 compliance) must skip `source = import`.
- **History payments may be soft deleted by their import's rollback** while they have no receipt (DATA_IMPORT § 2 "Undo" sanctions rolling back the batch's records; DATABASE_SCHEMA integrity rule 3 now names this one exception). A batch whose history asked for receipts cannot be rolled back (issued receipts are evidence).
- **Utilities:** a "Paid until" column (not in DATA_IMPORT's list) so a levy paid ahead is not billed again; `effective_from` = the day after it (else the import day). Rollback **switches settings off** (settings are never deleted, P03-06a) and archives the types the batch added (`utility_types.import_batch_id`); a re-import switches them back on. A setting already billed or read is never changed by an import. "Metered" is a Yes/No column (DATA_IMPORT's "metered?"); empty = as the utility charges (No for a new one).
- **Opening balances kept in their own table** (`opening_balances`, not in DB § 4 before): it records each imported row with its bill or credit entry, so re-imports and rollbacks are exact (credit entries are never changed or deleted, so a rollback is a reversing entry).
- **Tenant lookup keys** for tenants the file adds changed from `phone:`/`ref:` to `row:N` (the Tenants sheet row), so two rows naming the same new tenant differently are seen as one (exact counts).

**Contradictions / findings:**
- DATABASE_SCHEMA integrity rule 3 ("a confirmed payment cannot be deleted") vs DATA_IMPORT § 2 "Undo" ("rollback soft-deletes the batch's records"): resolved for imported history only, documented in both.
- DATA_IMPORT § 1 row 7 lists no date column for utilities; without one an agency cannot say a levy is paid ahead — "Paid until" added (as on the Leases sheet).
- `TellTenantAboutPayment` still tells the tenant when a receipt of imported history is later voided (the void is the agency's action, not the import's). Left as is; P05 may decide.

**Management handover (P03-14) — what this task's tables need:**
- `opening_balances`: history of the organization that imported it; never moved (its `opening_balance` bill and credit entry stay, like every bill and credit entry). An unpaid opening balance of a moving tenant stays the landlord's receivable (P03 decision 11 governs credit).
- `utility_types.import_batch_id`: types do not move (P03-06a); the moved settings point to the receiving organization's type. Imported `unit_utility_settings` move like any setting (their `import_batch_id` stays the old organization's batch: the receiving organization cannot roll it back, and the old organization's rollback no longer sees the moved rows — safe).
- History `payments` (source `import`) and their receipts: history, never moved.

**Follow-ups:**
- P05: notices and reminders must skip bills with `BillEvent::$imported` (opening balances) and payments with `source = import`; reminders for an imported opening balance start from the commit date (an overdue-at-issue bill sends no `BillOverdue`).
- P06-09: compliance metering on `PaymentConfirmed` must skip `source = import` (history is not money GoProperty saw arrive).
- P08: arrears reports should count `opening_balance` bills (overdue) and exclude imported history from collections.
- A tenant statement page (DATA_IMPORT § 3 "appears in the tenant's statement") is not built yet.
- Restricted organizations (ONBOARDING § 5.5) are not checked on these sheets (they add no unit or lease); confirm.

**Next:** P03-14 (management handover), then the P03 phase review (all P03 tasks complete once P03-14 and P03-15 land).

### P03-06b — Complete (2026-10-28): utility bills: `UtilityBillGenerator` in the daily `bills-generate` job (each unit's utility on its own cycle through the calculator, flat amounts in the lead window, metered (current − previous) × rate in kobo after the period, never estimated), readings linked to their bill, the natural key `(unit_utility_setting_id, period_start)`, who pays (the unit's lease covering the period; none → skipped and flagged), the utility bill document (3 layouts with the readings table), bills on the bill pages and in the export

With it the parent **P03-06 is Complete** (P03-06a utility setup + P03-06b utility bills: both P03-06 ACs — "metered bill = (current − previous reading) × rate in kobo" and "flat utilities bill each cycle" — pass).

**Delivered** ([ARCHITECTURE.md § 6.4](../ARCHITECTURE.md) new "How utility bills are built (P03-06b)", § 6.5 utility bill row, § 7 `bill:generate` row; [DATABASE_SCHEMA.md § 4](../DATABASE_SCHEMA.md) `bills.unit_utility_setting_id` + natural key, `unit_utility_settings` billing state, `utility_readings.bill_id`, `bill_items.meta` of utility lines; [BRANDING_DOCUMENTS.md § 4, § 5](../BRANDING_DOCUMENTS.md) new "How it is built (P03-06b)"; [BILLING_PAYMENTS.md § 7.4](../BILLING_PAYMENTS.md); AGENT.md § 3 rules 2, 3, 6, 7; `docs/plans/P02-P03-subtasks.md` P03-06b):
- **Schema (Billing module migrations, one change each):** `2026_11_18_070101_add_billing_state_to_unit_utility_settings_table` — `next_billing_date` (date, nullable: the next period to bill; null = from `effective_from`), `billing_hold` (string 20, nullable: `no_tenant` / `no_reading`) and `billing_hold_since` (date, nullable), index `(organization_id, active, next_billing_date)`. `…070102_add_unit_utility_setting_id_to_bills_table` — `bills.unit_utility_setting_id` (nullable FK, restrict) and **unique `(unit_utility_setting_id, period_start)`** (`bills_utility_setting_period_unique`, the natural key; NULLs never collide, so other bills are unconstrained). No TIMESTAMP and no JSON columns added (`TimestampColumnsTest` green).
- **`Bills\UtilityBillGenerator`** (current organization), run by `Jobs\GenerateBills` after `RentBillGenerator` for each organization (each guarded: a failure in one is reported and does not stop the other; same 40 s budget and resume-from-organization). Pages through switched-on settings of active utility types whose next period is within reach (`coalesce(next_billing_date, effective_from) ≤ today + lead_days`), 100 at a time; archived units skipped (`UnitDirectory::findMany`). Per setting, one `DB::transaction(…, 3)` per period under the **setting's row lock** (the same meter lock `Utilities\MeterReadings` takes): re-read the setting, take the period from the cursor with `CycleCalculator::periodContaining(effective_from, cycle, cursor)` (a cursor that is not a period start after a cycle or `effective_from` change → moved to the next whole period, never re-billing days), check the natural key, issue through `BillIssuer`, link the readings, move `next_billing_date` on (`nextPeriodStart`), clear the hold. A duplicate-key error on the natural key is retried (max 3); a failing setting is reported and the others continue.
  - **Flat:** billed on `CycleCalculator::billingDate(period start, lead_days)`, due on the period start (`dueDate(start)`, offset 0); one line `{type name}`, note "Flat charge, monthly", the period, meta `{utility_setting_id, utility, charging: flat, cycle}`.
  - **Metered:** billed once the period has ended: the unbilled kept readings dated ≤ the period end (and ≥ the paying lease's start), one line per reading: `Utilities\MeteredCharge::of(consumption, rate_kobo)` — whole units × rate + remaining thousandths × rate ÷ 1,000 rounded to the nearest kobo (halves away from zero) through `Money` (exact, integer only; `OverflowException` past the largest amount); note "Read 31/01/2027: 1,000 to 1,234.567, 234.567 kWh at ₦250" (+ "(new meter)"), meta `{utility_setting_id, utility, charging: metered, reading_id, reading_date, previous, current, consumption, unit, rate_kobo, meter_replaced}`; due `lead_days` after the day it is made. The readings get `bill_id` in the same transaction (only where still empty; a count mismatch rolls the bill back).
  - **Never estimated:** no reading in an ended period → the period waits (`billing_hold = no_reading`) until the next period has ended too, then closes with no bill; a reading taken later is billed with the period it is dated in. Readings that show no consumption → no bill; they go on the next bill as ₦0 lines.
- **Who pays (`Bills\UnitTenancies`):** the tenant of the unit's lease covering the period's first day (flat) or last day (metered), read through Leasing's `Contracts\TenantLeases` (`tenantIdsQuery([building])` then `forTenant()`, once per building per run; leases `active`, `renewed` or `expired` with a contract number; never drafts or `terminated` ones; the latest start wins). No covering lease → the period waits while it can still be billed (flat: until it ends; metered: until the next ends), then closes without a bill; `billing_hold = no_tenant` + `billing_hold_since` flag the setting until a bill clears it.
- **Bills:** `type = utility`, `billable` = the unit, `lease_id` null, `unit_utility_setting_id`, the property/unit (narrowing as every bill), the lease's number and cycle in `details`, description "Electricity, 01/01/2027 to 31/01/2027"; numbers from the shared invoice sequence; audited `bill.issued` (now with `unit_utility_setting_id`), `BillIssued` (so tenant credit, the invoice PDF and P05 notices follow as for rent). `BillDraft` gains `unitUtilitySettingId`; `Models\Bill` its property.
- **Switch on/off and changes** (`Utilities\UnitUtilitySettings::update`): switched on again → `next_billing_date` = the first period start on or after that day (`nextPeriodStart(…, daysAfter(today, −1))`), hold cleared: the periods while off are never billed.
- **Utility bill document:** `Documents\UtilityBillDocument` (Core `Document`; wraps the bill's `InvoiceDocument` + utility name, charging label, unit of measure and `Documents\UtilityReadingLine`s from the lines' meta as issued). `Documents\Invoices::document()` returns it for `utility` bills (the invoice for others; `invoice()` builds the `InvoiceDocument`), so the kept PDF (`RenderInvoice`, key `invoice`/`invoice-void`), the bill page's Invoice/PDF, the tenant portal, `/verify` and `/pay` all show it. Views `billing::documents.utility.{modern,classic,compact}` + sections `header` (UTILITY BILL), `summary` (utility · charged · period), `readings` (Read on · Previous · Current · Used (unit) · Rate · Amount; previous/current under the date on phones), `styles`; the invoice's sections for parties, lines (flat), payment, totals, closing.
- **Lists:** the units' utilities list shows "next period from dd/mm/yyyy" and the hold badge ("Not billed: no tenant on a lease" / "Waiting for a meter reading" · since dd/mm/yyyy); a billed reading's "Billed" badge links to its bill. Bills list (type filter Utility), bill page and export need no change.
- **Docs:** ARCHITECTURE.md § 6.4 (new "How utility bills are built"; lead lines), § 6.5 (utility bill row), § 7 (`bill:generate` row); DATABASE_SCHEMA.md § 4 (`bills` utility paragraph, `bill_items.meta`, `unit_utility_settings` billing state, `utility_readings.bill_id`); BRANDING_DOCUMENTS.md § 4 row and § 5 "How it is built (P03-06b: utility bills)"; BILLING_PAYMENTS.md § 7.4 (settings move with their billing state); docs/runbooks/ci.md (the drill's utility part); Billing `module.json` description.

- `composer ci` on the final commit: Pint passed, Larastan 0 errors, Pest **3,047 tests: 2,990 passed, 57 skipped (the Chrome/Gotenberg halves, no Gotenberg locally: 48 before + this task's 6 visual diffs and 3 phone-width checks), 39,884 assertions**, 0 failed (`--parallel`, drills included).
- New tests with `APP_DEBUG=true`: UtilityBillGenerationTest 29/29 passed (325 assertions); UtilityBillDocumentTest 19 passed + 9 skipped (Chrome halves), and 28/28 with the local Chrome stand-in; BillGenerationDrillTest 4/4 (1,641 assertions).
- **AC: metered bill = (current − previous reading) × rate in kobo** — `Modules/Billing/tests/Feature/UtilityBillGenerationTest.php` › "acceptance: metered bill = (current − previous reading) × rate in kobo" › "bills the readings of the period after it ends: the consumption times the rate kept on each reading, exactly" (start 1,000, read 1,234.567 → 234.567 kWh × ₦250 = 5,864,175 kobo; not billed on the period's last day, billed the day after; bill fields, line note and meta (`toEqual`), reading linked; a rate change to ₦3.33 applies to February's readings only: 0.5 kWh → 167 kobo (166.5 rounded half away), 1.001 kWh → 333 kobo), › "bills large and fractional values exactly, in kobo" (dataset through the job: 0.5 L × ₦3.33 = 167, 1.001 × ₦3.33 = 333, 0.001 × ₦5 = 1, 987,654,321.987 × ₦1,234.56 = 121,931,851,975,227, 999,999,999,999.999 × ₦92,233.72 = 9,223,371,999,999,990,777 kobo — expected values worked out with exact fractions), › "works out consumption × rate in kobo with integers only…" (9 cases incl. 0.499 kobo → 0) and › "refuses an amount larger than money holds instead of overflowing".
- **AC: flat utilities bill each cycle (time travel across several cycles)** — › "acceptance: flat utilities bill each cycle: the calculator's periods from the date they apply, each on its billing day" (monthly from 31 Jan 2027 — 31 Jan, 28 Feb, 31 Mar, 30 Apr, 31 May, 30 Jun, the expected periods written out and equal to the calculator's — and a quarterly override ₦60,000 from 1 Jan; for each of the 9 periods the job runs the day before its billing day (nothing) and on it (exactly that period); amounts, due dates (= period start), tenants per unit, line meta, `lease_id` null, shared invoice sequence per year).
- **Sub: a rerun creates no duplicates** — › "bills each period once when the job runs again, the same day or later" (7 runs over 5 days: 3 bills, `BillIssued` and `bill.issued` once each), › "never bills a period twice, even for a setting put back a period, and the database refuses a second bill" (cursor put back → no new bill, moved to 1 Mar; a raw second row → `UniqueConstraintViolationException` on `bills_utility_setting_period_unique`), › "links each reading to one bill only, and leaves later readings for later bills". **Concurrent runs:** `tests/Drill/BillGenerationDrillTest.php` › "acceptance: bills every utility period once, and every meter reading on one bill, when several runs work on the same utilities at once" (4 processes, `tests/Fixtures/Processes/utility-bill-generation.php`, running `UtilityBillGenerator` straight without the job lock on 12 let units: flat daily/weekly/monthly utilities behind by 20–100 days and metered monthly ones with 3 readings each; processes overlapped, nothing reported, per-setting counts equal the calculator's, unique keys, each bill issued by one process, verification codes, every reading on exactly one bill of its own meter for the period it was read in, invoice sequence exactly 1..N, a second concurrent pair issues nothing). Ran 3× green. **Negative control** (temporary, reverted): with the setting's `lockForUpdate` removed the database's unique key refused the duplicates and the drill failed on "nothing reported" (`Duplicate entry … bills_utility_setting_period_unique`).
- **Sub: meter replacement** — › "acceptance: handles a replaced meter: the old meter's last reading and the new meter from its first value" (old meter 1,500 → 1,560 = 60, new meter from 0: 25, then 65 → lines 1,500,000 + 625,000 + 1,625,000 = 3,750,000 kobo = 150 kWh × ₦250; never 25 − 1,560; "(new meter)" in the note; all three readings on February's bill).
- **Sub: no reading → no estimated bill** — `describe('acceptance: no reading → no estimated bill')` › "waits for a reading, never estimates, and bills a late reading with a later period" (January without a reading: no bill, flagged "Waiting for a meter reading · since 01/01/2027" on the page; a reading dated 3 Feb is February's; January closes with no bill once February has ended; zero-consumption April: no bill, its reading goes on May's bill as a ₦0 line; bills only for 1 Feb and 1 May).
- **Sub: the utility bill PDF renders with both drivers** — `Modules/Billing/tests/Feature/UtilityBillDocumentTest.php` › "acceptance: the utility bill PDF renders with both drivers" › "prints on one A4 page with dompdf, in the organization's layout" (3 layouts × metered/flat; always runs: one page, UTILITY BILL, number, tenant, unit, account, reference, readings values and amounts, "Page 1 of 1") and › "prints the same with Chrome (Gotenberg) as with dompdf, within the threshold" (3 × 2; **skipped locally without Gotenberg, runs in CI**; verified locally against a scratch Chrome stand-in (P03-05c's DevTools server copied to my scratchpad, port 3996, not committed): 6/6 pass, scores modern 0.13/0.14 %, classic 0.23/0.24 %, compact 0.09/0.10 % (metered/flat; threshold 0.4 %), one page each). Also › web page readings table (values per row), › flat bill without a readings table, › rent bills still print as invoices, › kept PDF rendered once by `RenderInvoice` and downloaded through the signed link, › rendered CSS lint for 3 layouts × 3 targets, › 360 px without sideways scrolling in real Chrome (3 layouts; Chrome half, stand-in 3/3).

**Decisions to confirm** (orchestrator decisions relied on, and choices made here):
- Relied on **decision 1 (cycle maths and lead window):** every utility period, bill date, due date and catch-up step comes from `CycleCalculator` (`periodContaining`, `period`, `nextPeriodStart`, `billingDate`, `dueDate`, `daysAfter`; the interval-maths scan stays green), anchored to the setting's `effective_from` (month-end anchoring); flat utilities use the organization's `billing.lead_days` (default 7) like rent and are due on the period start (offset 0: settings have no due offset).
- Relied on **decision 6 (Gotenberg):** the Chrome halves (visual diff, 360 px) skip only when Gotenberg is unreachable and fail on CI otherwise; dompdf always runs. No `Http::preventStrayRequests()` in these tests.
- Relied on **decision 8 (gating):** utilities fall under BILLING § 1.2's "bills" row (every plan): nothing new for P06-01.
- Relied on **decision 9 (narrowing):** utility bills carry `property_id`, so `BillPolicy`, the bills list and the export narrow them like rent bills; no new page.
- Relied on the **product owner rules:** handover — bills are history and stay; the setting's `next_billing_date` / hold are live state and move with it; evidence — the utility bill's PDF is the invoice's kept PDF (`issued_documents`, never deleted).
- Relied on the brief's **MySQL JSON**, **fixed test data** (every tenant in these tests has a fixed name, phone and email) and **TIMESTAMP** rules (no timestamp columns added).
- **Metered utilities are billed in arrears**, the day after the period ends (consumption cannot be billed ahead), **due `lead_days` after the day the bill is made** (the same notice rent gets between bill and due date). Product owner: confirm, or prefer due on the next period's start.
- **Which readings make a period:** the unbilled readings dated on or before the period's last day (consumption from the reading billed before to the latest reading in the period); a reading dated later belongs to the next period. One bill line per reading, each at the rate kept on the reading (P03-06a's `rate_kobo`), so a rate change never re-prices a taken reading.
- **No reading:** the period waits until the next period has ended too, then closes with no bill (never estimated); a late reading is billed with the period it is dated in. **No consumption:** no bill; the readings go on the next bill as ₦0 lines.
- **Who pays:** the lease covering the period's first day (flat) / last day (metered), among active, renewed and expired leases; **terminated leases never pay** (`LeaseSummary` has no termination date, so a terminated lease's term is unknown): consumption read during a lease ended early is not billed automatically. A metered bill never includes readings dated before the paying lease's start (they stay unbilled: a manual bill is a follow-up). **No covering lease → skip and flag** (`billing_hold`), never billed to the next tenant or the owner.
- **Natural key on the setting, `lease_id` null on utility bills** (P03-04b's note): rent's `(lease_id, type, period_start)` stays untouched; the price is that a lease's "Bills" link (`/bills?lease=`) and the tenant portal's lease page list rent bills only (the tenant's bills list shows all). Follow-up below.
- **Catch-up:** a setting added with a past `effective_from` is billed for every period since (as rent catches up); switching a utility back on never back-bills the time it was off; a changed cycle/effective date continues from the next whole period of the new schedule (no double billing; the part period between is not billed).
- **Who pays is read through `TenantLeases`** (`tenantIdsQuery` + `forTenant`, once per building per run) because Leasing has no "lease of this unit" contract and this task must not touch `Modules/Leasing` (P03-13a runs alongside). A `UnitLeases` contract method in Leasing would be cleaner (follow-up).
- **The utility bill is the invoice with a readings table** (`UtilityBillDocument` wraps Core's `InvoiceDocument`), titled UTILITY BILL, same number sequence, `/verify` kind `invoice` ("Invoice").

**Management handover (P03-14) — what this task's tables need:**
- `bills` (utility bills included) and their `bill_items`: history, never moved (as P03-04b). Their `unit_utility_setting_id` keeps pointing at a setting that may move to the new organization (like `unit_id`/`property_id`).
- `unit_utility_settings.next_billing_date`, `billing_hold`, `billing_hold_since`: live state, move with the setting, so the receiving organization's next run bills the next period with its own numbers and branding.
- `utility_readings.bill_id` of billed readings: see P03-06a (a link into the old organization's history; keep or clear in P03-14). Unbilled readings move and are billed by the receiving organization.

**Follow-ups:**
- Leasing: a contract method for "the unit's lease covering a day" (replaces `UnitTenancies`' two-step read) and the termination date in `LeaseSummary` (so a lease ended early pays up to its end).
- Lease link for utility bills (`/bills?lease=` and the portal's lease page): e.g. a `payer_lease_id` column, or widening rent's key with a NOT NULL discriminator.
- Manual bills: consumption read between two leases or during a terminated lease; correcting a billed utility period (void + re-bill).
- The settings export could carry "next period" and the hold; a settings page for `billing.lead_days`.
- P05: reminders on utility bills (`BillIssued`/`BillOverdue` already carry them) and meter-reading reminders.
- P03-13b: imported utility settings should set `next_billing_date` so imports never back-bill (DATA_IMPORT rule).

**Delivered** ([DATA_IMPORT.md](../DATA_IMPORT.md) § 1 row 5, § 2.6, § 2 "Undo", § 3; [ARCHITECTURE.md § 6.3, § 6.12](../ARCHITECTURE.md); [DATABASE_SCHEMA.md § 3, § 8A](../DATABASE_SCHEMA.md); AGENT.md § 3 rules 3, 5, 8 and § 4 "Imports"; `docs/plans/P02-P03-subtasks.md` P03-13a):
- **No schema change, no new permission, no new package.** `leases` already had `import_batch_id` / `external_ref` (P03-03a).
- **Leasing — `Import\LeasesImportTarget`** (key `leases`, sheet "Leases", order 50: after tenants; registered from `LeasingServiceProvider` through Import's contract; a singleton that keeps only stateless collaborators and resolves the organization-reading ones when used). Columns: Your ID, Tenant (phone or Your ID), Building, Unit, Start date, End date, Billing cycle (dropdown; "Annual", "Per annum", "Bi-annual", "Every 3 months"… understood), Rent per period, Deposit held, Paid until, Due days after period start, Auto-renew (Yes/No), Contract number, Status (empty / Active / Expired / Month-to-month), Notes.
  - Tenants are named through the Tenants module's new `Contracts\ImportedTenants` (`Import\ImportedTenantLookup`: Your ID, then E.164 phone; a tenant the same file adds is known from the Tenants sheet's dry-run memory, which now also remembers `ref:` + Your ID; archived ones only when the file brings them back), units through the Properties module's new `Contracts\ImportedUnits` (`Import\ImportedUnitLookup`: the building by name or Your ID through `PropertiesImportTarget::resolve`, then the label without case, or the Units sheet's memory). No other module's table is read.
  - **Exact per-row errors** (row, column, message): tenant missing / unknown / archived; building unknown, ambiguous, archived or rolled back; unit unknown or rolled back; bad or missing dates; end not after start; unknown cycle; rent missing, not above ₦0, negative or ≥ ₦10bn; deposit negative; paid until before the start or after the end; due offset outside the cycle's period (0 daily, ≤ 6 weekly, ≤ 27 otherwise); Auto-renew not Yes/No; unknown status; Active on an ended lease, Expired on a running one, Month-to-month on a running one or with a cycle other than Monthly; Your ID twice in the file; a unit let on file, under maintenance or unavailable, or with a second ongoing lease in the file; a contract number already another lease's, twice in the file, or looking like the organization's own numbering (`LS-ADR-…`, which `DocumentNumberer` gives to new leases); markup / over-long text; a restricted organization's new rows (ONBOARDING § 5.5); a matched lease of another tenant or unit, in another state, with new terms once billed, or a different contract number.
  - **Warnings:** rent unlike the unit's yearly asking rent (cycles that fit a year: monthly, quarterly, six-monthly, yearly); the tenant already has an active lease on file or an ongoing one earlier in the file; the exact past days "paid until" leaves unbilled (they go on the Opening balances sheet); expired history; month-to-month with its end date; a rolled-back lease brought back; a deposit already recorded that the file would change.
  - Dry-run preview (tenant, unit, status, cycle, rent, paid until, "Billed from", end date) and expected yearly collections for ongoing leases.
  - **Matching:** Your ID, then tenant + unit + start date (active or expired leases), rolled-back leases included (restored). A re-import updates the terms while the lease has no bills; tenant, unit, status, contract number and the recorded deposit never change by import.
  - `usedSince`: a lease of the batch with a bill (Billing's answer, void bills included), a renewal, an issued contract, a deposit refunded or kept, or terminated/renewed on its page.
- **Leasing — `LeaseService::importOngoing()`** (the only writer, inside the import's chunk transaction; lock order tenant → unit → lease): an ongoing lease is `active` at once (`activated_at` = the import time, `import_batch_id`), refused while another lease is active on the unit (and by the `active_unit_id` unique index), for a unit under maintenance/unavailable, an archived building or tenant, or a restricted organization's new lease. `next_billing_date` from `Import\OngoingLeaseDates` (calculator only): the period after "paid until" (the start when empty) or the first period starting on or after the import day, whichever is later. Month-to-month: active, monthly, `auto_renew`, ending after twelve monthly periods from the current one (or the one paid up to), anchored to its start; kept on re-import. Past leases: `expired`, no `next_billing_date`, the unit untouched. The deposit is a `held` ledger entry ("Held when the lease was imported"; not added again on restore or update). Contract number kept from the file or `DocumentNumberer::next(LeaseContract)`. Audited `lease.imported` (log `leases`: batch, action create/restore/update, status, paid until, next billing date; an update lists the changed fields; an unchanged re-import writes nothing). Sends **`Events\LeaseImported`** (new; `active` flag; carries the unit status) — never `LeaseActivated`, so `IssueLeaseContract` issues nothing (the lease page issues the contract on its first download, as for leases activated before P03-03b). A refusal at commit (`LeaseRefused`) skips the row with its reason. `rollBackImport()` soft deletes the batch's leases one by one under each unit's lock, audited `lease.import_rolled_back`, and sends **`Events\LeaseImportRolledBack`** (the unit goes back to vacant when nothing else holds it); deposit entries stay.
- **Leasing — contracts:** `Contracts\ImportedLeases` (`heldUnitIdsQuery($batch)`, `batchTenantIdsQuery($batch)`; implemented by `LeaseRecords`), `Import\LeaseImportUsage` (a unit or tenant named by a lease the batch did not create).
- **Properties:** `FollowLeases` now also listens to `LeaseImported` and `LeaseImportRolledBack`; `UnitsImportTarget::usedSince` = held by a lease that is not the batch's own (the batch's own leases are rolled back first) or named by another module's record (`ImportUsages`). `Contracts\ImportedUnits` + `UnitReference`.
- **Tenants:** `TenantsImportTarget::usedSince` = portal access, or a lease / bill / payment the batch did not create (`ImportUsages`; replaces the direct `TenantLeases::tenantIdsQuery()` check). `Contracts\ImportedTenants` + `TenantReference`. **"Invite tenants now"** (`Livewire\ImportInvitations`, `tenants::import-invitations`): on a committed import's report, for the import's tenants (created/restored by it, and the tenants of its leases) with no portal account and no open invitation: "Invite by email (n)", "Invite by WhatsApp (n)" (when the agency's WhatsApp is available: the P03-01b stand-in), "Printable QR sheet (n)" (link invitations, the QR codes shown once in a printable sheet, kept only in a locked property until "Done"). Every invitation goes through P03-01b's `TenantInvitations::invite` (policy `managePortalAccess`, one live invitation per tenant, 14 days, single use, audited, rate limited); at most 30 per press; refusals counted and explained ("n tenants were not invited yet: …"). Each action `#[BlockedWhileImpersonating]`; a wire:confirm before sending.
- **Import:** `Contracts\ImportUsage` / `ImportUsages` (registry singleton, `Targets\UsageRegistry`: kinds `unit`, `tenant`, `lease`; records the batch created never count) and `Contracts\ImportReportPanels` (`Targets\ReportPanelRegistry`); the batch page renders the registered panels once committed.
- **Billing:** `Import\BillUsage` (bills naming a lease, tenant or unit); **Payments:** `Import\PaymentUsage` (payments naming a tenant, any status); each registered from its provider.
- **Template:** the Leases sheet joins the template (dropdowns: Billing cycle, Auto-renew, Status); its example lease (L-001: the example tenant, Palm Court Flat 1, 01/01/2025–31/12/2027, yearly ₦1,500,000, deposit ₦150,000, paid until 31/12/2026) imports with the other examples.

**Decisions to confirm** (orchestrator decisions relied on, and choices made here):
- **Decision 1 (cycle maths):** `next_billing_date`, the unbilled days and the month-to-month end come only from `CycleCalculator` (`nextPeriodStart`, `periodContaining`, `period`, `daysAfter`); the interval-maths scan covers the new Leasing code and stays green. The lead window is Billing's as built.
- **Decision 3 (no SMS):** invitations by email, the `WhatsAppSender` stand-in, or link/QR; never SMS.
- **Decision 5 (deposits):** a `held` lease ledger entry per imported deposit; no money flow.
- **Decision 8 (gating):** Excel import is every plan's; WhatsApp invitations are Growth+ (BILLING § 1.2) — not gated, P06-01 follow-up (as P03-01b).
- **Decision 9 (narrowing):** imports are owner/admin only (never narrowed); each invitation still goes through `managePortalAccess` (narrowing applies there).
- **PO rules:** handover notes below; no evidence file is created or removed by the import.
- **No back-billing, stricter reading:** billing never starts with a period that began before the import day, even the current one, so no bill is ever past due when made and nothing is sent about a past date; the unpaid part of the current period is arrears for the Opening balances sheet (P03-13b), named in a warning. Alternative: bill the current period (due in the past, overdue at once). Product owner to confirm.
- **Empty "paid until"** = nothing paid since the start (same rule applies; no error).
- **Status per row** (empty / Active / Expired / Month-to-month) instead of a file-wide "Treat as month-to-month" switch; a contradiction with the dates is an error.
- **Month-to-month** (not defined in the docs): active, monthly (Billing cycle must be Monthly, rent = one month), `auto_renew` on, end = twelve monthly periods from the current one (or the one paid up to); the daily job then flags it before that end and keeps it active (P03-03a's auto-renew rule). Product owner to confirm the twelve months.
- **No contract issued by the import** (`LeaseImported`, not `LeaseActivated`): an imported lease is often years old and may already have a signed paper contract; the agency prints GoProperty's from the lease page when it wants (issued on first download).
- **Kept contract numbers** refuse ones shaped like the organization's own `{prefix}-YYYY-nnnnnn` (a later activation would collide on the unique index).
- **Service charge** (DATA_IMPORT § 1 row 5 lists it) is not imported on leases: bills carry rent only; `leases.other_charges` is unused. Docs say so; P03-06 (utilities) or a later task decides.
- **Per-lease audit** `lease.imported` (unlike tenants/units, which leave creates to `import.committed`): leases are state-machine records.
- **Re-import** changes terms only while the lease has no bills (void ones included); the deposit already recorded and the contract number stay.
- **Restricted organizations** add no lease by import (ONBOARDING § 5.5, as `LeasePolicy::create`); updates continue.
- **"Used" for rollback** (DATA_IMPORT § 2 "Undo", as built): see Delivered; a payment makes the tenant used, and a lease through its bill.
- **New Import contracts** `ImportUsages` (modules answer whether imported records are used, so targets never read other modules' tables) and `ImportReportPanels` (a module's step on the report).
- **Invitation batch** of 30 per press under P03-01b's unchanged rate limits (30 per member and 50 per organization an hour): a 200-tenant import is invited over a few hours. The import's tenants = created/restored by it + the tenants of its leases.

**Contradictions / findings:**
- DATA_IMPORT § 1 row 5 lists "service charge" on leases, but bills carry rent only and leases have no service-charge column (only the unused `other_charges` json): not imported; docs updated.
- DATA_IMPORT § 3 "unless 'Treat as month-to-month' is chosen" had no semantics anywhere; defined above.
- The Units and Buildings import sheets (P02-06c) do not apply the restricted-organization rule (ONBOARDING § 5.5 "no new … properties or units"); only the Leases sheet does. Follow-up.
- Singleton import targets must not keep the scoped `OrganizationContext` (a queue worker resets it between jobs): found when a test made a request between two imports; `LeasesImportTarget` resolves such collaborators when used. The Properties targets hold no scoped service (checked).

**Management handover (P03-14) — what this task's tables need:** no new table. Imported leases are ordinary `leases` rows (live while active, with `lease_deposit_entries`) and move as P03-03a describes; `import_batch_id` stays the landlord organization's batch id — P03-14 should clear or keep it knowing that the receiving organization cannot roll that batch back (its batches are scoped to the old organization) and that the old organization's rollback must refuse once the leases have moved (their rows are no longer visible to it: a rollback would archive nothing of them, which is safe).

**Follow-ups:**
- P03-13b: opening balances / credits / utilities / history targets; their records should answer `ImportUsages` (e.g. a bill of the same batch never counts; Billing's answer already excludes it) and the phase-exit journey test.
- P05-01: re-check "no reminders for past dates" with real reminders (subtask plan item 14); reminders must start from the commit date.
- P06-01: gate WhatsApp invitations (Growth+).
- Restricted rule for the Units/Buildings import sheets.
- Bulk invitations for large imports (rate limits) and an invitation export; send the contract to the tenant on request.
- Service charge on leases and bills (DATA_IMPORT § 1 row 5).

**Next:** P03-13b (wave 12, alone).

- **Module:** `Modules/Expenses` (`module:make`, trimmed: no controller stub, seeder, event provider, config, factories), in `modules_statuses.json`. `ExpensesServiceProvider` registers `ExpensePolicy`, the Livewire namespace `expenses::`, the sidebar item "Expenses" (order 490, after Bank statements; `wallet`), the list export, and the module's own **evidence** media collection `expense_receipts` (JPEG, PNG, WebP, PDF; 10 MB; `thumb` for images), merged into `config('media.collections')` from `register()` as P03-09 did for `payment_proofs`.
- **Schema (module migrations, one table each):** `2026_11_17_120101_create_expenses_table` — `organization_id`, `property_id`/`unit_id` (nullable FKs, restrict), `property_name`/`unit_label` (names as recorded), `category`, `description` (500), `amount_kobo`, `spent_on` (date), `vendor_name`, `payment_method`, `reference`, `note`, `ticket_id` and `bill_id` (nullable, **no FK**, unwritten until P04 / a recharge flow), `recorded_by_user_id`, `updated_by_user_id`, `archived_by_user_id`, `archive_reason`, timestamps, soft deletes; indexes `(org, spent_on)`, `(org, property, spent_on)`, `(org, category, spent_on)`, `(org, unit)`, `(org, ticket)`. `…120102_create_expense_receipts_table` — `organization_id`, `expense_id` (FK restrict), `media_id` (unique), `file_sha256`, `uploaded_by_user_id`, `uploaded_at` (`useCurrent()`), `archived_at`/`_by_user_id`/`archive_reason`, timestamps. No bare NOT NULL TIMESTAMP (`TimestampColumnsTest` green); no JSON columns.
- **Models and enums:** `Models\Expense` (organization-scoped, soft deletes, `HasMedia`/`HasOrganizationMedia`; `forceDelete()` throws), `Models\ExpenseReceipt` (organization-scoped; refuses delete and every change but archiving once). `Enums\ExpenseCategory` (the six DB § 4 categories, labels and Nigerian hints: diesel, prepaid power, tenement rate, Land Use Charge, security guards…), `Enums\ExpensePaymentMethod` (bank transfer, cash, POS, cheque, other). Both models in `tests/Support/OrganizationScopedModels.php`.
- **Permissions** (classed in both guard methods; roles in `OrganizationRole`): `expenses.view` (owner, admin, agent; kept while suspended), `expenses.view_organization` (owner, admin; kept while suspended: the organization's own expenses, no building), `expenses.record` (owner, admin, agent; dropped while suspended), `expenses.manage` (owner, admin; dropped while suspended: archive/restore, archive receipts). **None blocked while impersonating.**
- **`Policies\ExpensePolicy`:** `viewAny`/`export`/`view`, `viewOrganizationExpenses`, `viewReceiptFile` (`#[BlockedWhileImpersonating]`), `create`, `createUnlinked`, `update`, `attachReceipt` (`#[BlockedWhileImpersonating]`), `archiveReceipt` (`#[BlockedWhileImpersonating]`), `delete` (archive), `restore`, `forceDelete` never. An expense with no building answers 404 to members without `expenses.view_organization`; outside a narrowed agent's buildings 404 (`BuildingAccess`).
- **`Recording\ExpenseBook`** (the only writer): `record` (with optional receipt files), `update`, `archive` (reason), `restore`, `attachReceipts`, `archiveReceipt` (reason). Policy checked again; building through `PropertyDirectory` (this organization's, active for a new link; one kept on edit even if archived or handed over), `BuildingAccess` (404 for another building to a narrowed agent), unit through `UnitDirectory` (of that building; an archived unit kept on edit); amounts typed in naira → `Money` (no floats, ≤ ₦10bn, > 0); dates Y-m-d, not in the future (Lagos), from 2000. Files: every file of an upload checked by `UploadValidator` before anything is stored, then `MediaUploads` into `expense_receipts` with one `expense_receipts` row each; at most 5 a time, 10 kept per expense. Audited under log `expenses` (`expense.recorded`, `.updated` with changed fields and amount/building before → after, `.archived`/`.restored` with reason, `.receipt_added`, `.receipt_archived` with sha256 and reason); never the description or note.
- **Pages (`expenses::`):** `/o/{slug}/expenses` (`ExpenseIndex`: 25 a page, latest date paid first, debounced search over description, payee and reference, filters category, building or "no building", date range, Active/Archived, all in the URL, the total of the filtered rows, "No receipt" badge, export `expenses`), `/expenses/create` (`?building=`) and `/expenses/{id}/edit` (`ExpenseForm`; receipts on create), `/expenses/{id}` (`ExpenseShow`, archived ones too: details, who recorded/changed, receipts with thumbnails, add receipts, archive a receipt with a reason, archive/restore the expense), `/expenses/{id}/receipts/{receipt}` and `/thumb` (`ExpenseReceiptFileController`, throttled 60/min: policy then a signed `MediaUrls` redirect; a receipt of another expense 404).
- **Export:** `Lists\ExpenseList` (key `expenses`, built like `LeaseList`, rows from `Lists\ExpenseRows`: live building/unit names else as recorded, recorder name, receipt count per chunk): Date paid, Category, Description, Amount, Building, Unit, Paid to, Payment method, Reference, Note, Receipt files, Recorded by, Recorded on, Archived on, Reason archived.
- **Docs:** DATABASE_SCHEMA.md § 4 (`expenses` as built, `expense_receipts`, media collections incl. the missing `payment_proofs`), ARCHITECTURE.md § 5 (new "Expenses" paragraph), § 6.13, § 8, § 9; BILLING_PAYMENTS.md § 7.4 (expenses stay); PLATFORM_CONSOLE.md § 6 (receipt file/receipts/archive blocked, record/edit allowed in write mode, list exports); `config/media.php` comment.

**Decisions to confirm** (orchestrator decisions relied on, and choices made here):
- Relied on **decision 8 (plan gating):** built ungated. Note: BILLING_PAYMENTS.md § 1.2's table does not list expenses at all; the `expenses` feature key is only in DATABASE_SCHEMA.md `plan_feature`'s registry → P06-01 must decide which plans include expenses.
- Relied on **decision 9 (narrowing):** `BuildingAccess` in the policy, the list query (page and export), the form's building picker and the writer; built against the contract (unassigned agents see every building's expenses, as the contract answers today).
- Relied on the **PO rules:** receipts are evidence in their own collection `expense_receipts` (never `photos`), never deleted, "remove" archives with the file kept; expenses archive (soft delete), never hard-deleted.
- **Handover (PO rule: history stays with the organization that wrote it):** expenses are the recording organization's books → **they stay**, with their receipts, even when their building moves; the row keeps the building's and unit's names as recorded so it reads right afterwards; the FK to `properties` still holds (a moved building keeps its id). P03-14 calls nothing in Expenses.
- **The organization's own expenses (no building) are owners' and administrators' only** (`expenses.view_organization`): salaries and office costs are not agents' business; agents, narrowed or not, record and see only building expenses.
- **Who records / who archives:** agents record and edit their buildings' expenses and add receipts; archiving and restoring an expense, and archiving a receipt, are owners' and administrators' (`expenses.manage`). No approval workflow (none in the docs).
- **Impersonation:** recording an expense is not a tenant-money action (no funds move, no tenant balance changes) → no expense permission is blocked; write mode may record/edit (audited with both identities); archiving, adding/archiving receipts and opening a receipt file are refused in both modes (evidence; a receipt may show a payee's bank details or phone). The list exports while impersonating (no contact details).
- **Several receipts per expense:** DB § 4's `receipt_media_id` became an `expense_receipts` row per file (invoice + receipt, multi-page photos; per-file archive with who/why). Documented in DB § 4.
- **Schema names:** DB § 4's `spent_at` built as `spent_on` (a date); added `payment_method`, `reference`, `note`, `property_name`, `unit_label`, `updated_by_user_id`, archive columns; `ticket_id` and `bill_id` built nullable with no FK and no writer yet.
- **Categories:** exactly DB § 4's six, with Nigerian examples as hints (not new categories).
- **An archived building** can't be chosen for a new expense; an expense already on it keeps it on edit (also a building no longer in the organization).
- **Suspended:** both view permissions kept (owners still see the organization's own expenses); record/manage dropped.
- **Property owners** get no expense permission yet (owner portal P03-15 / reports P08 decide what owners see).

**Management handover (P03-14) — what this task's tables need:** nothing moves. `expenses` and `expense_receipts` (with their `expense_receipts` media files) are history of the organization that recorded them. Note for P03-14: if Properties ever hard-deletes or re-keys a moved building, `expenses.property_id`/`unit_id` (FK restrict) would block it — a move keeps ids, so this holds.

**Follow-ups:**
- P06-01: gate the `expenses` feature key per plan (BILLING § 1.2 to say which plans).
- P08: expenses by category and per building in reports and owner statements (indexes in place); property owners' view of their buildings' expenses (P03-15/P08).
- P04: link an expense to a maintenance ticket (`ticket_id`, add the FK then); a "charge this to the tenant" flow would write `bill_id`.
- Import rollback (P02-06c): a building or unit imported in a batch that is rolled back after an expense was recorded on it is soft-deleted under the expense (the expense keeps it, shown by the name recorded); Properties' `usedSince` does not know about expenses (it cannot read Expenses without a contract). Consider an Expenses contract Properties asks, so such a batch counts as used.
- Strip EXIF from receipt photos / serve conversions only (NDPA), as noted for proofs.

No NOT NULL timestamp without a default (TimestampColumnsTest green).
- **Models and enums:** `Models\UtilityType`, `UnitUtilitySetting`, `UtilityReading` (organization-scoped, nothing mass assignable; types and settings refuse delete; readings refuse change once billed and force delete; `UtilityReading` has the media relation), `Enums\ChargingMode`, `Enums\MeterUnit`; cycles are `Modules\Leasing\Contracts\BillingCycle`. `Utilities\MeterValue` (+ `MeterValueCast`): meter values as exact whole thousandths, never floats. All three models in `tests/Support/OrganizationScopedModels.php` (every `CrossTenantAccessTest` probe).

None blocked while impersonating; archiving a type and removing a reading are `delete` abilities (refused in both modes).
- **Policies:** `UtilityTypePolicy` (organization-wide), `UnitUtilitySettingPolicy` (incl. `recordReading`: switched-on metered setting of an active type) and `UtilityReadingPolicy` (update/delete only the latest kept, unbilled reading; `viewPhoto`); 404 outside a narrowed agent's buildings through `BuildingAccess` on `unit_utility_settings.property_id`.

Names through `UnitDirectory`/`PropertyDirectory` once per page (`Lists\UtilityRows`; query count fixed for 1 vs 21 rows).
