**Tests and evidence (local, MariaDB 11.4):**
- `composer ci` on the final commit: Pint passed, Larastan 0 errors, Pest **2,889 tests: 2,845 passed, 44 skipped (the Chrome/Gotenberg halves of the document tests, no Gotenberg locally), 35,226 assertions**, 0 failed (`--parallel`, drills included). The first full run caught `EvidenceFilesTest` (the list of media collections now includes `meter_photos`); the test was extended, not weakened.
- New and touched tests with `APP_DEBUG=true` (UtilitySetupTest, UtilityAccessTest, UtilityPagesTest, CrossTenantAccessTest, ListExportCoverageTest, WorkspaceNavigationTest, SuspendedOrganizationTest, EvidenceFilesTest, TimestampColumnsTest): 258 tests, 258 passed, 1,602 assertions.
- **Sub: unique `(unit_id, utility_type_id)`:** `UtilitySetupTest` › "acceptance: keeps one setting per unit and utility: adding it again skips the unit, and the database refuses a second row" (re-adding skips and names "Flat 1"; a raw second row throws `UniqueConstraintViolationException`; another building's or organization's unit refused).
- **Sub: a reading cannot go backwards:** › "acceptance: a reading cannot go backwards: it counts from the last reading, or the starting reading" (1,099.999 after 1,100.5 refused with the message; equal value = 0 consumption; below the starting reading refused; previous/consumption exact) and › "acceptance: counts from a replaced meter's first value when \"meter replaced\" is ticked, and never below it"; through the page: `UtilityPagesTest` › "records a meter reading through the page…".
- **Readings ordered by date, none on the same day:** › "acceptance: keeps readings in date order, never two on the same day, none in the future or before the utility applies" (same day and earlier refused, future (Lagos) refused, before `effective_from` refused, raw same-day insert refused by the unique key); removal frees the day: › "corrects or removes the latest reading before it is billed, audited, and frees the removed reading's day".
- **Sub: cross-org probes:** `CrossTenantAccessTest` (datasets `UtilityType`, `UnitUtilitySetting`, `UtilityReading`); `UtilityAccessTest` › "answers 404 for another organization's utility, setting or reading guessed by ID, on pages, lists and actions" (edit pages, photo, filters, record page with a foreign meter → 404, `archive(foreign)` → 404, tampered snapshot → 419 with debug off, writers refuse foreign records, a member of both sees each one's own).
- **Narrowed agents:** › "shows an agent assigned to buildings only those buildings' utilities and meters, and 404 for the others" (lists, filters, record picker, record/remove on another building → 404 from the page and the writer, photo 404, exports narrowed, own building recorded; unassigned agent sees all).
- **Suspended org read-only:** › "shows utilities read-only while the organization is suspended, and still exports them" (no buttons, archive/record/edit → 403, photo and the three exports work).
- **Impersonation:** › "lets impersonating staff look at and export utilities in a read-only session, and change nothing" and › "…in write mode record and correct readings, audited with both identities, but never archive a utility or remove a reading" (`impersonator_staff_id` on the audit entry; `impersonation.blocked` audited).
- **Roles:** › "enforces the ARCHITECTURE.md § 5 role rules in the utility policies" (5 roles × 12 abilities) and › "opens the utility pages for owners, administrators and agents, and 404 for property owners, tenants and strangers" (agents: no type/setting management, 403 on those forms; they record readings).
- **List exports:** `UtilityPagesTest` › exact rows in CSV and Excel for all three lists (formula-injection neutralised in a note, Lagos times, kobo as naira), filters carried into the export, audit `list.exported`; `ListExportCoverageTest` (a utility, setting and reading in the shared fixture; `/utilities/units` and `/utilities/readings` walked as list pages; the three keys in the expected registry).
- Others: types (validation, uniqueness incl. archived, charging locked once used, archive/restore, never deleted), overrides and defaults following the type, starting reading locked once read, readings refused for flat/switched-off/archived utilities and archived units, rate kept per reading, billed reading never changes (bill from a real lease), meter photo as evidence (cannot be deleted, kept on removal, PDF refused) and its signed link, `MeterValue` parsing, fixed query count.
- Existing tests changed (none weakened): `WorkspaceNavigationTest` (+`utilities` for owner, admin, agent), `SuspendedOrganizationTest` (+`utilities.view` in the kept list), `ListExportCoverageTest` (fixture + pages + keys), `EvidenceFilesTest` (+`meter_photos` as evidence), `OrganizationScopedModels` (+3 models).
- MySQL JSON key order: the only JSON compared is audit `before`/`after`, with `toEqual`; `changed` lists with `toEqualCanonicalizing`.
- Not run: MySQL 8 and MariaDB 10.6 (the unique key with a NULL `live` relies on standard NULL semantics in unique indexes on both; watch it on CI); no browser smoke (HTTP/Livewire tests only).

**Orchestrator review:** merged `main` (with P03-11b) into the branch; resolved one PLATFORM_CONSOLE.md § 6 conflict (the exportable lists gain the utility lists; the delete/void rule gains the utility examples). GitHub CI then failed one exact query-count assertion (off by one either way on MySQL 8.0 and MariaDB 11.4); it now allows a margin of two, which still fails on any per-row growth. `composer ci` locally: Pint passed, Larastan 0 errors, Pest 2,922 tests: 2,874 passed, 48 skipped (Gotenberg). **GitHub Actions** run [40609985592](https://github.com/thekingchild/Goproperty/actions/runs/40609985592): green on MySQL 8.0/8.4 and MariaDB 10.6/11.4 (including the `live` NULL-unique reading key).

**Decisions to confirm** (orchestrator decisions relied on, and choices made here):
- Relied on **decision 8 (gating):** built ungated. Utilities fall under BILLING § 1.2's "bills" row (every plan), so nothing new for P06-01; the agent narrowing used here is the Scale+ "agent ↔ building assignment" feature, already a P02-03 follow-up for P06-01.
- Relied on **decision 9 (narrowing):** settings carry `property_id`; policies, lists, exports, the record picker and the writers narrow through `BuildingAccess`; readings follow their setting.
- Relied on the **product owner rule on handovers:** settings and readings are live, building-linked records with `organization_id` + building/unit ids, no other organization's ids cached except `utility_type_id` (see below) and a billed reading's `bill_id` (history link).
- Relied on the **evidence rule:** meter photos go in their own evidence collection `meter_photos`, never `photos`; removing a reading is a soft delete keeping the photo.
- **Who:** owners and administrators keep utility types and units' settings (pricing, like the building portfolio); agents see them and read meters in their buildings. Three new permissions rather than reusing `bills.manage` (which is blocked while impersonating as a money action; changing a rate is like editing a lease's rent, allowed in write mode).
- **A unit pays a utility only with an active setting** (opt-in per unit, with "add to every unit of a building" in one step), not "every unit pays every type unless switched off" — nothing is billed that nobody chose. Product owner: confirm.
- **Charging mode lives on the type** (`charging_mode` enum replacing the documented `metered` bool; no per-unit `metered`): a unit without a meter gets a flat utility type. Changing charging/unit of measure is refused once units use the type.
- **Meter replacement:** an explicit "Meter replaced" flag on the reading with the new meter's first value (default 0); the reading counts from it. The old meter's last reading should be recorded first (the form says so). Never inferred from a lower value.
- **Readings strictly after the latest kept reading** (no back-dated insert between two readings: it would change the next reading's consumption); not in the future (Lagos), not before `effective_from`.
- **Corrections:** only the latest kept reading of a meter, only before it is billed (correct: audited with before/after; remove: soft delete with reason, frees its day). A billed reading never changes; fixing a billed one is P03-06b's (credit/void the bill).
- **Meter values:** decimal(15,3) (three places for water litres), handled as whole thousandths (`MeterValue`); P03-06b multiplies thousandths × `rate_kobo` and divides by 1,000 through `Money` with its rounding.
- **`rate_kobo` on the reading** is the rate when it was read (override, else default): later rate changes do not re-price taken readings. P03-06b may decide to bill at the period's rate instead.
- **Archived units:** their settings stay listed ("Unit archived") and editable (to switch off); they take no readings.

**Management handover (P03-14) — what this task's tables need:**
- `unit_utility_settings`: move with the unit (rewrite `organization_id`; `property_id`/`unit_id` keep their ids); **re-point `utility_type_id`** to the receiving organization's type with the same code (else name), creating one from the moved type when missing; clear a clashing `external_ref`.
- `utility_readings`: every reading of a moved setting moves (rewrite `organization_id`), with its `meter_photos` media (move files to the new organization's folder, like building photos). Removed readings move too (meter history). `bill_id` of billed readings points to a bill that stays with the old organization (history): decide in P03-14 whether to keep the link or clear it on the moved row.
- `utility_types`: organization-wide configuration, never moved.

**Follow-ups:**
- P03-06b: `UtilityBillGenerator` (active settings of active types, each setting's cycle from `effective_from` through the calculator; flat = amount per period; metered = sum of unbilled readings' consumption × `rate_kobo`, linking each reading's `bill_id` once); its own natural key (P03-04b note: `(lease_id, type, period_start)` would collide for two utilities of one lease — e.g. a key on `(unit_utility_setting_id, period_start)`); who pays (the unit's active lease's tenant).
- P03-13b: utility settings import target (types created if missing by code/name, settings with `external_ref`, last meter reading → `starting_reading`); settings have no soft deletes, so import rollback switches a setting off (or a migration adds `deleted_at`).
- P03-14: the moves above.
- P05: reminders for meter reading days, if wanted.

**Next:** P03-13a runs alongside; then wave 11 (P03-06b ‖ P03-12).

### P03-11b — Complete (2026-10-27): tenant portal: proofs, receipts, documents — "Send a proof" for the tenant's own bill through Payments' `PaymentProofs` (also while the agency is suspended, never while staff impersonate, limits as plain messages), my payments (receipts and proofs with where each stands), a receipt's page with the verify link, my documents (lease contract, invoices, receipts and VOID copies) as the kept PDFs through signed, expiring links after a tenant policy check, and a real-browser 360 px pass on every portal page

With it the parent **P03-11 is complete** (P03-11a units, bills, pay info + P03-11b proofs, receipts, documents; the parent AC "tenant sees only their own leases/bills; portal usable at 360px width" passes: the first in P03-11a and again here for proofs, receipts, invoices and contracts, the second by the browser pass below).

**Decisions relied on (to confirm):**
- **Decision 2 (lookup pattern):** every new read goes through `PortalTenancies` (the one unscoped `tenant_profiles` read of P03-11a), then the modules' contracts inside `OrganizationContext::run`.
- **Decision 4 (people and roles):** tenants hold no workspace role, so the portal identity is the permission (`viewAsTenant` / new `downloadAsTenant`); no new `OrganizationPermission`.
- **Decision 6 (Gotenberg):** the 360 px pass skips only when Gotenberg does not answer, and fails on CI if it does not.
- **Decision 8 (gating):** nothing gated — the portal, proofs, receipts and documents are every plan's.
- **Decision 3:** no SMS/WhatsApp added (the portal only reads what the P03-10b notices already send).
- **PO rules (evidence):** every file the tenant opens is the kept evidence file (proof originals, PDFs rendered once and never re-rendered; VOID copies are separate files); nothing in the portal archives or deletes. **Handover (P03-14):** no tables added, nothing to move; receipts, proofs and documents stay with the organization that issued them and stay visible through the old, still-linked tenant profile until it is archived (as P03-11a's bills).
- **ONBOARDING § 3 / § 5.5:** the read-only portal of a suspended (or cancelled) agency still takes proofs and still opens documents (downloads are reads).
- **My choices:** the receipt shows "Valid" while a void waits for its second approver (the request is the agency's business); a void bill's invoice download is its VOID copy (as members get); the proof page fills in the amount owed, today and the account's name; a member's upload for the tenant is listed as "Added by the agency"; archived proofs are listed (status "Archived") without the archive reason; the tenant may open their own proof files (not while staff impersonate); lists are not paginated; the portal's sign-in link pages (P03-01b, on the shared sign-in layout) were included in "every portal page", which meant small CSS changes to the approved sign-in layout (44 px eye button, brand link, footer and top-bar links; mobile top-bar padding) — product owner: confirm, as they touch /login too.

**Contradictions / findings:**
- The local `public/build` was stale (built before P03-11a), so `min-h-11` and other new classes were missing from the CSS locally; CI builds fresh. Run `npm run build` before the browser pass (noted in docs/runbooks/ci.md).
- UX § 1's 44 px rule is not met by Flux's default 40 px buttons and inputs anywhere else in the app (workspace pages); the portal pages set `min-h-11` themselves. A Flux-wide fix (e.g. a theme override for touch screens) is a follow-up.

- **Schema (Collections migrations, after both tables exist):** `2026_11_15_070201_add_matching_columns_to_bank_statement_lines_table` (`tenant_profile_id` nullable FK restrict, `match_attempts` default 0, `matched_at` nullable, `matched_by_user_id` nullable FK null on delete), `…070202_add_payment_keys_to_bank_statement_lines_table` (**unique `payment_id`** + FK to `payments`, restrict), `…070203_add_bank_statement_line_foreign_key_to_payments_table` (the P03-10a follow-up: FK `payments.bank_statement_line_id` → `bank_statement_lines`, restrict; not unique — a rejected match and the later one both name the line; in Collections, so no Payments migration was touched). All reversible; no TIMESTAMP without a default.
- **Enums/model:** `MatchStatus` — `suggested` now means "a payment recorded from the line waits for confirmation" (label "Waiting for confirmation"), only `unmatched` is open (`isOpen()`, `offeredForMatching()` = credits `unmatched`); `MatchConfidence` gains `manual` (labels "Reference in the narration", "Same amount, similar name", "Matched by hand"). `BankStatementLine::paymentKey($attempt)` = `statement-line:{id}` (attempt 1, decision 10) or `statement-line:{id}:{n}` after a rejection.
- **Matching (`Modules\Collections\Matching`):** `NarrationReferences` (upper-case, spaces and dashes — `\p{Pd}` too — removed, every "GP" + 7 letters/digits, at most 5 candidates); `NameSimilarity` (documented, testable: words without accents/case/titles/one-letter words; two of the tenant's words among the payer's words — sender column and narration —, equal or a cut-short word of ≥ 4 letters beginning the other; a one-word name needs that word of ≥ 4 letters); `MatchFinder` (reads only: references → bills via `BillLedger::findByReference`, one tenant → high (first non-void bill); two tenants → nothing; else `BillLedger::openBillsOwing(amount)` + similar name for exactly one non-archived tenant → medium; else nothing); `LineMatches` (the only writer of a line's match: `matchAutomatically`, `confirmHighConfidence`, `matchByHand`, `requestMatching`, `paymentConfirmed`, `paymentRejected`; audited under log `statements`, never narrations).
- **Payments only through `Contracts\PaymentRecorder`:** a match → `record()` as the system (`by` null): bank transfer, `statement_match`, the key, `bank_statement_line_id`, the bill, **only the line's date (`paid_at`), amount and narration** (reference = narration cut to 100, note = whole narration when longer; no payer, account or balance). Bulk confirmation → `recordConfirmed()` with the line's key as the member; a hand match → `recordConfirmed()` with an explicit split (or none: open bills oldest first), recorded and confirmed at once (decided: one payment with explicit allocations — what `PaymentService` supports; one tenant per credit).
- **`Listeners\FollowStatementPayments`** (sync, inside the payment's transaction) on Payments' public `PaymentConfirmed`/`PaymentRejected` (`statement_match` only): confirmed → line `matched` (+1 `lines_matched` on the statement); rejected → line `unmatched`, payment/bill/tenant/confidence cleared, attempts kept (decided: back to `unmatched`, not `dismissed` — the money arrived and is someone's; the matcher never re-suggests it; an owner/admin matches it by hand).
- **`Jobs\MatchStatementLines`** (queue `billing`, 3 tries, backoff 30/120, timeout 50; 250 lines or 35 s a run, then queues itself after the last line): dispatched after commit by `StatementReader` when a statement with credits is read, and by "Look for matches again".
- **A line is matched once:** the line's row lock + re-read (only `unmatched` credits), the per-line idempotency key (unique per organization in `payments`) and unique `bank_statement_lines.payment_id`. Lock order (found by the drill): a hand match locks the tenant's open bills (`BillLedger::lock`, id order) **before** the recorder's first audit entry — otherwise a hand match holding the audit chain waits for a bill whose foreign-key S-lock a matcher (waiting for the audit chain) holds: deadlock; and the listener finds a payment's line with a plain read, then locks it by primary key (a locking read by `payment_id` scanned and locked other lines of a small table).
- **Billing contract (small addition):** `BillLedger::openBillsOwing(Money $outstanding, int $limit = 20)` (open bills owing exactly that, oldest due first; `amount_kobo = paid_kobo + ?`, so no unsigned underflow on MySQL), implemented in `Bills\Ledger`. Needed for "same amount" without reading `bills`.
- **Permission:** new `statements.match` (owner, admin; `allowedWhileSuspended` false; `blockedWhileImpersonating` true; admin role map in `OrganizationRole`). `BankStatementImportPolicy::match` (`#[BlockedWhileImpersonating]`); page actions `findMatches`, `confirmHighConfidence`, `LineMatch::save` also `#[BlockedWhileImpersonating]`; the recorder checks `payments.confirm` too.
- **Pages:** statement page (`StatementShow`): a "Matching" section (matched / waiting for confirmation, with how many by reference / still to match), "Confirm N matches with the reference" (with `wire:confirm`) and "Look for matches again" for `statements.match`; under each matched line its tenant, how it was matched and "Open the payment"; under an unmatched credit "Match by hand" and, after a rejection, "Its matched payment was rejected". New `/o/{slug}/statements/{statement}/lines/{line}/match` (`Livewire\LineMatch`): the credit (date, amount, narration), tenant picker (`TenantDirectory::activeOptions`), the tenant's open bills with an amount each (typed naira read by `StatementAmount` into exact kobo), "Record and confirm"; a matched or debit line shows it is closed / 404.
- **Docs:** as listed above.

**Decisions relied on / to confirm:**
- **Decision 10 (product owner, settled)** — followed as written: matches (high and suggested) become payments waiting for confirmation (`statement_match`, `PaymentRecorder` key `statement-line:{id}`) in the existing narrowed queue, showing only the line's date, amount and narration; agents confirm/reject with `payments.confirm`; owners/admins bulk-confirm high-confidence ones; unmatched lines and splits stay owner/admin-only on the statement page (new permission `statements.match`).
- **Decision 9 (narrowing):** agents meet matched credits through the payments queue (Tenants' narrowing on `BuildingAccess`); a narrowed agent's confirmation settles only their buildings' bills (PaymentService, unchanged).
- **Decision 8 (gating):** "bank transfer with statement matching" is on every plan (BILLING § 1.2) → nothing for P06-01.
- **PO rule on handovers:** lines and their payments are history; nothing moves (see below).
- Choices made here (to confirm): rejecting a match → line back to `unmatched` (not `dismissed`), never re-suggested; a later attempt keyed `statement-line:{id}:{n}`; a hand match is recorded **and confirmed** at once (owners/admins look at the money on the statement; ARCH's recorder doc already names "a statement line a member matched" for `recordConfirmed`); a split is one payment with explicit allocations, **one tenant per credit** (splitting across tenants not built); the matcher keeps running for a suspended organization (system work; members cannot confirm until reactivated); an ambiguous credit (two tenants' references, or amount+name fitting two tenants) stays to match; reference-matched credits of any amount are high confidence (a part payment or an overpayment is still that tenant's money).
- **Contradictions / notes:** ONBOARDING § 4.1 says "the agent confirms matches in one tap (bulk confirm for high-confidence ones)"; decision 10 keeps bulk confirm with owners/admins and agents confirm one by one in the queue — decision 10 followed, ONBOARDING § 4.1 "as built" says so. DB § 4's `match_status` values kept; `suggested` re-read as "waiting for confirmation" and `manual` added to `match_confidence` (documented).

**Open question for the product owner:** a matched credit recorded through `PaymentRecorder::record` fires Payments' `PaymentRecorded`, so the tenant is told "payment received, waiting for confirmation" (P03-10b notices) — also for a *suggested* (name + amount) match that may turn out to be someone else's; rejecting it then tells the tenant "payment rejected". Options: skip the tenant notices for `statement_match` until confirmed, or only for medium confidence. Not changed here (Payments internals were out of scope).

**Management handover (P03-14):** nothing moves. `bank_statement_lines` (with the new `tenant_profile_id`, `suggested_bill_id`, `payment_id`) and the payments recorded from them are the organization's history; if P03-14 moves a tenant's row, the old organization's matched lines keep pointing at it (the page then shows "A tenant") — P03-14 must not rewrite them.

- "Not a tenant payment" (dismiss/ignore) for credits that are no rent (interest, loans, transfers between own accounts), owner/admin, audited; today they stay "To match".
- Splitting one credit across several tenants (several payments per line) if wanted.

**Delivered** ([ARCHITECTURE.md § 4, § 5 "Tenant portal", § 8](../ARCHITECTURE.md); [PLATFORM_CONSOLE.md § 6](../PLATFORM_CONSOLE.md) "Scope" and the invoice rule; [UX_DESIGN.md § 2](../UX_DESIGN.md); [ONBOARDING_COMPLIANCE.md § 4.1](../ONBOARDING_COMPLIANCE.md); `docs/plans/P02-P03-subtasks.md` P03-11a):
- **No schema change, no new permission, no new package.**
- **The access path (`Modules\Tenants\Portal\PortalTenancies`, documented in ARCH § 4):**
  1. one unscoped read of `tenant_profiles` (`organization_id`, `id`) by `user_id`, archived profiles left out (`LinkedTenantProfiles::byOrganization`, the only raw read, in the file `TenantPagesTest` already allows) — decision 2's lookup pattern, no scope removed;
  2. per organization, `OrganizationContext::run`: the profiles are loaded through the scope and kept only when `TenantProfilePolicy::viewAsTenant` allows them;
  3. leases, units and bills are read there through contracts only (`Leasing\Contracts\TenantLeases`, `Properties\Contracts\UnitDirectory`/`PropertyDirectory`, the new `Billing\Contracts\TenantBills`), limited to those profile ids.
  - Record pages use `PortalTenancies::first()`: the record must be found among the user's own profiles, so another tenant's or organization's id gets the same 404 as a missing one.
  - While staff impersonate the user, `opens()` keeps only the support session's organization (PLATFORM_CONSOLE § 6 "Scope").
- **Policy (`TenantProfilePolicy`):** `viewAsTenant` (in context, not archived, `user_id` = the user, organization opened by the path; otherwise `denyAsNotFound`) and `viewInvoiceAsTenant` (`#[BlockedWhileImpersonating]`: the invoice prints the tenant's phone and email, as `BillPolicy::viewInvoice`). Both are reads, so the central suspended and read-only guards let them through.
- **Billing contract `Contracts\TenantBills`** (`forTenants`, `find`, `payment`, `invoicePage`; value objects `TenantBill` with the bill's `DocumentBranding` from its snapshot, and `TenantBillPayment` with lines, the primary usable bank account, the `PaymentOptions` HTML and the `/pay` URL). Implemented by `Bills\TenantBillRecords`, bound in `BillingServiceProvider`. `PayPages` and `PaymentOptions` are untouched (P03-08 plugs into the slot; the portal shows whatever it returns).
- **Pages (Tenants module routes; `auth` + `verified`; Livewire `tenants::portal.*`; Core's `Route::view('portal')` and `surfaces/portal.blade.php` removed):**
  - `/portal` (`PortalHome`): one card per agency in its current colours with the amount to pay, "Pay this bill" for the next bill due, my homes (unit · building, address, status, dates, rent per cycle; draft leases hidden) and the latest bills. Empty state "No rentals yet" for someone who rents nothing. Shows the session `status` flash (sign-in link follow-up from P03-01b).
  - `/portal/bills` (`PortalBills`): per agency, under the logo of its newest bill, bills to pay (due first first, with the total) and paid/cancelled ones. Each card has a stripe in the colour the bill was issued with.
  - `/portal/bills/{id}` (`PortalBillShow`): branding as issued (logo, name, contacts), status, amount to pay and due date, lines, bank transfer details with the reference for the narration, "Pay online" options, "Open the pay page" to share, invoice link when allowed. A void bill says "do not pay it" with the reason and no bank details; a paid bill says so.
  - `/portal/bills/{id}/invoice` (`PortalInvoiceController::show`, throttle 60/min, `#[BlockedWhileImpersonating]`): the invoice web page (P03-05c). The PDF is P03-11b's.
  - `/portal/leases/{id}` (`PortalLeaseShow`): the home, agency, status, contract number, dates, rent, cycle and the lease's bills.
  - Home / My bills tabs (44 px targets) on every portal page; cards, wrapping text, no tables; existing sidebar "My rentals" and bottom nav kept.
  - Suspended or cancelled organization: everything stays visible with a "Read-only for now" notice; the pages have no actions.

**Decisions relied on (to confirm):**
- **Decision 2 (lookup pattern):** the portal's only unscoped read is `tenant_profiles (organization_id, id) by user_id`; everything else inside `OrganizationContext::run`.
- **Decision 4:** tenants have no `organization_user` rows, so the portal identity (`tenant_profiles.user_id`) is the permission (`viewAsTenant`); no new `OrganizationPermission`.
- **Decision 8 (gating):** nothing gated (the portal is every plan's).
- **P01-08 / PLATFORM_CONSOLE § 6:** the portal lists only the session's organization; the invoice is blocked in a session like the workspace invoice.
- **PO rules:** handover — no new tables; nothing for P03-14 to move here (it already plans to copy `tenant_profiles.user_id` to the receiving organization's tenant, so the portal follows automatically; old bills stay with the issuer and stay visible through the old, still-linked profile until it is archived).
- **My choices:** draft leases hidden from tenants; archived tenant profiles give no portal access (as P03-01b's `LinkedTenantProfiles` and P03-09's proof policy); bill URLs carry no organization slug (ids are global; the path finds the organization); the agency frame uses the organization's current branding, each bill its snapshot; the invoice web page is in 11a (AC names invoices), its PDF in 11b.

**Contradictions / findings:**
- `ImpersonationGuard::ensureRequestAllowed` ignores `#[BlockedWhileImpersonating]` on **invokable** controllers (`Route::getActionMethod()` returns the class name, so `marked()` checks a method that does not exist). I used a named method (`show`); the guard gap itself is not fixed here (Core; no invokable controller is marked today). Follow-up: treat a class-name action method as `__invoke`.

**Follow-ups:**
- **"Add an email" for phone-only accounts: not built** (deferred again). Settings → Profile now opens, but saving needs an email, and adding one sets it unverified, which locks the `verified` portal until confirmed. Needs a pending-email + verify-link flow (blocked while impersonating, rate-limited, refused if another account holds the address).
- P03-11b: proof upload on the bill page (`Payments\Contracts\PaymentProofs`, `maySubmit`), receipts and documents (invoice PDF, contract, receipts via signed `MediaUrls`), the 360 px browser test on every portal page.
- Archived tenants (moved out) might want read-only history later; today they lose portal access.
- `ImpersonationGuard` invokable-controller gap (above).

### P03-08 — Complete (2026-10-27): agency-owned Paystack: the OnlinePayments module with the organization's own Paystack connection (keys encrypted and masked, test, rotate, disconnect, owner-only, hidden from impersonating staff), the per-organization webhook `/webhooks/paystack/{webhook_token}` (HMAC-SHA512 with that organization's key, raw body kept in `webhook_log`, 2xx then the `billing` queue), verify-by-fetch, auto-confirmation through `PaymentRecorder` once per Paystack reference, amount mismatches held for review, "Pay online" on the pay page within the online-payment cap, and the Paystack option in the signup wizard

**Delivered** ([ONBOARDING_COMPLIANCE.md § 4.2](../ONBOARDING_COMPLIANCE.md) "How it is built (P03-08)", § 2 wizard; [ARCHITECTURE.md § 5 "Settings", § 6.4 "How online payments are built", § 7, § 8, § 10](../ARCHITECTURE.md); [DATABASE_SCHEMA.md](../DATABASE_SCHEMA.md) `webhook_log` / `organization_paystack_connections` as built, new `paystack_transactions`, `organizations.online_payment_cap_kobo`; [BILLING_PAYMENTS.md § 4.2](../BILLING_PAYMENTS.md); [BRANDING_DOCUMENTS.md § 5](../BRANDING_DOCUMENTS.md) payment options slot; [PLATFORM_CONSOLE.md § 6](../PLATFORM_CONSOLE.md); AGENT.md § 3 rules 1, 5, 6, 8, 9; `docs/plans/P02-P03-subtasks.md` P03-08):
- **Module:** `Modules/OnlinePayments` (`module:make`, trimmed like Collections: no controller stub, seeder, event provider, config or factories), in `modules_statuses.json`. `OnlinePaymentsServiceProvider` registers `PaystackConnectionPolicy`, the Livewire namespace `onlinepayments::`, the Settings tab "Online payments" (order 25, `SettingsTabs`) and the Paystack option into Billing's `Contracts\PaymentOptions`. `RouteServiceProvider` loads `routes/web.php` in the `web` group and `routes/webhooks.php` **outside it** (no session, no cookies).
- **Schema (one change each, explicit TIMESTAMP defaults):** module `2026_11_14_080101_create_organization_paystack_connections_table` (unique `organization_id`; `public_key`; `secret_key` text, encrypted cast; `secret_key_last4`; **unique `webhook_token`**; `mode`; `status` pending_test/active/failing/disconnected; `last_tested_at`, `last_test_error`, `last_event_at`, `connected_by_user_id`, `connected_at`, `disconnected_at`); **Core** `2026_11_14_080102_create_webhook_log_table` (shared with platform Paystack P06-03: **nullable `organization_id`** (decision 2), `provider`, `event`, `reference`, `payload` **longText raw body** (not json: MySQL reorders json keys and the HMAC is over the raw bytes), `signature_valid`, `ip_address`, `received_at` useCurrent, `processed_at`, `outcome`, `processing_error`); module `2026_11_14_080103_create_paystack_transactions_table` (bill, tenant, globally unique `GPO-…` reference, amount asked, currency, mode, status initialized/confirmed/held/failed, hold reason, what Paystack verified, `payment_id` FK).
- **Models** (all organization-scoped, all three added to `tests/Support/OrganizationScopedModels.php`): `PaystackConnection` (keys and token `#[Hidden]`, `maskedPublicKey()`/`maskedSecretKey()`, `active()`), `PaystackWebhookLog` (table `webhook_log`; the delivery never changes, never deleted; the platform's null-organization rows are never visible through it), `PaystackTransaction` (bill/amount/reference frozen; a settled one never changes; never deleted). Enums `ConnectionStatus`, `ConnectionMode`, `TransactionStatus`, `WebhookOutcome`.
- **`Paystack\PaystackClient`** — the organization's key only (never `services.paystack.secret_key`): `test()` (`GET /transaction?perPage=1`; 401 → refused), `initialize()` (`POST /transaction/initialize`; only `checkout.paystack.com`-type https addresses accepted back), `verify()` (`GET /transaction/verify/{reference}`; 400/404 "not found" → null; anything else odd → `PaystackUnavailable`).
- **`Connections\PaystackConnections`** — the only writer of the connection and of `organizations.online_payment_cap_kobo`; checks `manage` itself: `connect` (shape `pk_/sk_` + live/test, same mode, **test keys refused in production**; then Paystack tries the secret: refused → nothing saved; accepted → `active`; unreachable → `pending_test`), `test` (active/failing/unchanged), `rotateWebhookToken` (48 lowercase letters/digits), `disconnect` (both keys erased, status `disconnected`, token kept), `setCap` (₦1,000–₦100,000,000 or null = standard ₦1,000,000; one audit entry, the organization model's own entry suppressed). Audited under `payment_settings` with masked keys (`paystack.connected`, `.keys_replaced`, `.tested`, `.webhook_rotated`, `.disconnected`, `.online_cap_changed`); an `active` connection completes the checklist's collections item (`paystack: true`).
- **Settings → Online payments** (`/o/{slug}/settings/paystack`, `Livewire\PaystackSettingsPage`, `#[BlockedWhileImpersonating]`; route `workspace.settings.payments.paystack`, and Core's sidebar "Settings" now also lights for `workspace.settings.payments.*`): status, mode, masked keys, webhook address with where to paste it, last test and last call; Test connection, New webhook address, Disconnect, Connect / Replace keys (the secret field is emptied after every attempt, so it never goes back to the browser), the online payment limit, and the latest ten held payments. **Permissions reused, none new:** `payment_settings.view` (see) and `organization.payment_settings` (change), both org-owner only, both already blocked while impersonating and classed for suspension; `PaystackConnectionPolicy::viewAny/manage`, both `#[BlockedWhileImpersonating]`.
- **Webhook** `POST /webhooks/paystack/{token}` (`Http\Controllers\PaystackWebhookController`, `Webhooks\PaystackWebhooks`; `throttle:300,1` per IP; also excepted from CSRF in `bootstrap/app.php`): token → organization by one unscoped read of `organization_paystack_connections.organization_id` (disconnected excluded), then `OrganizationContext::run` (decision 2 / P01-04 pattern); unknown/malformed/rotated/disconnected token → the plain 404 of any unknown address, nothing kept; > 64 KB → 413; `X-Paystack-Signature` = HMAC-SHA512 of the raw body with **that** organization's secret, `hash_equals`; every delivery kept in `webhook_log` first; wrong signature → kept as invalid (`rejected`), 401, never processed; right → `last_event_at`, `Jobs\ProcessPaystackWebhook` queued on `billing` (afterCommit, 5 tries, backoff 30/120/600/1800 s, timeout 45 s), 200 `{"received":true}` at once.
- **Processing / settlement** (`Transactions\PaystackTransactions::settle`): only `charge.success` for a reference GoProperty started (else `ignored`, no Paystack call); a settled transaction → `duplicate`; **verify by fetch** with the organization's key outside any lock (unreachable → exception, job retried, nothing recorded; not `success` → `not_paid`, `failed`/`reversed` close it); then `DB::transaction($cb, 3)` with the transaction row locked: paid as asked (Paystack's kobo = asked, NGN, `domain` = connection mode, bill not void and still owing ≥ it) → `PaymentRecorder::recordConfirmed` (method `paystack`, source `paystack_webhook`, key `paystack:{reference}`, `paystackReference`, `billId`, `by` null) → receipt and tenant notice through Payments as for any confirmation; otherwise **held**: `PaymentRecorder::record` (pending in the confirmation queue, reason in the note, the bill linked unless void) or, for another currency/mode, no payment; audit `paystack.payment_held` (log `online_payments`).
- **Pay online** (`PayOnline\PaystackOption` in Billing's slot; `Http\Controllers\PayOnlineController`): offered when the connection is `active` and the bill's outstanding is > 0 and **≤ the cap**; renders a "Pay ₦… online" link to the signed `/pay/{reference}/online?organization=…` (12 h). That page (own CSP: no scripts, `form-action 'self' https://checkout.paystack.com`; `no-store`, `noindex`; agency name, bill number, amount — no tenant details) asks for the payer's email (Paystack requires one; sent to Paystack only), POST (same signed address, CSRF, `throttle:10,1`) → `PaystackTransactions::start()` keeps the transaction row then initializes it with the organization's key (amount = outstanding, `GPO-` + 20 hex, callback `/pay/{reference}/online/return`, metadata bill number/reference) → 303 to Paystack's checkout; Paystack refusing → error shown, the row `failed`. The bill is found through Billing's `BillLedger::findByReference` inside the signed organization (OnlinePayments never reads `bills`). **Return** `/pay/{reference}/online/return?reference=GPO-…` (`throttle:30,1`): finds the transaction's organization by its unique reference (own table), queues `Jobs\SettlePaystackTransaction` (`billing`, `ShouldBeUnique` per transaction) when it belongs to that bill and is not settled, and redirects (303) to `/pay/{reference}`.
- **Signup wizard (P01-02) collections step:** links "Add a bank account" and "Connect your Paystack account" (only for members who may see payment settings, only while the route exists), "Continue" once the collections checklist item is done ("Set up later" otherwise); the Paystack page shows "Back to setting up your organization" while the organization is `onboarding`.
- **Docs:** ONBOARDING_COMPLIANCE.md § 2 (wizard), § 4.2 (URL with `{webhook_token}`, "How it is built"); ARCHITECTURE.md § 5 (Settings → Online payments), § 6.4 (`{webhook_token}`, "How online payments are built"), § 7 (the two jobs), § 8 (module list), § 10 (webhook as built); DATABASE_SCHEMA.md (domain map, `organizations.online_payment_cap_kobo`, `webhook_log` and `organization_paystack_connections` as built, new `paystack_transactions`); BILLING_PAYMENTS.md § 4.2 (organizations' own endpoint); BRANDING_DOCUMENTS.md § 5 (the option); PLATFORM_CONSOLE.md § 6 (the page blocked). `App\Navigation\SettingsTabs` doc comment.

- All Paystack HTTP is faked: `FakePaystackServer` (in `Modules/OnlinePayments/tests/Feature/helpers.php`) behind `Http::fake()` with `Http::preventStrayRequests()` — it knows keys per organization, keeps transactions per key (a reference is unknown to another account's key) and records every request; none of these tests renders a PDF (receipt and invoice PDF jobs held back), so no Gotenberg call is blocked.
- **AC: a webhook signed with the wrong organization's key is rejected (and logged)** — `Modules/OnlinePayments/tests/Feature/PaystackWebhookTest.php` › "acceptance: a webhook signed with the wrong organization's key is rejected" › "rejects and keeps a delivery to our address signed with another organization's secret key, and records nothing" (401; one `webhook_log` row under our organization: `signature_valid` false, outcome `rejected`, raw body byte-equal, event/reference; nothing in theirs; no payment, transaction still initialized, bill unpaid; **no verify call made**), › "rejects GoProperty's own key, a forger's key, a missing or garbled signature, and a body changed after signing" (6 rejected rows incl. pretty-printed JSON and a changed amount under a valid signature; then the right signature, uppercase hex, is accepted), › "keeps each organization's deliveries apart…".
- **AC: a replayed webhook creates one payment** — › "acceptance: a replayed webhook creates one payment" › "confirms the payment once, however often Paystack delivers the event, and the return from checkout changes nothing" (3 deliveries + the return + a direct settle: one payment, confirmed, method/source/key/paystack reference/bill/`confirmed_by` null, allocation, bill paid, one receipt, transaction confirmed with `payment_id`, log outcomes confirmed/duplicate/duplicate, one `payment.confirmed` audit), › "creates one payment even when a replay finds the transaction not yet marked settled" (transaction row reset by hand: the recorder's key and the unique reference still give one payment and one receipt).
- **AC: keys hidden during impersonation (settings page and any API/JSON)** — `PaystackConnectionTest.php` › "acceptance: keys hidden during impersonation" › "never shows the keys or the webhook address to impersonating staff and refuses every change, in both modes" (read-only and write: page 403 without any key part or token, every Livewire action 403 (test/rotate/disconnect/saveCap/connect), Settings tabs hide "Online payments", stored keys/token/status/cap unchanged, `impersonation.blocked` audited), › "keeps the keys out of every array and JSON form of the connection"; connecting › "…never sends the secret back to the browser" (Livewire reply HTML + snapshot, page, `toArray`/`toJson`, audit log: no key, no token).
- **Sub: a bill over the cap shows bank details only** — `PayOnlineTest.php` › "the pay page" › "acceptance: shows a bill over the cap with bank details only" (cap ₦500,000 on an ₦850,000 bill: bank details and reference, no "Pay online", no online link; the online page and its POST send the bill back to the pay page and start nothing; exactly at the cap offered; null cap = standard ₦1,000,000; a ₦1,500,000 bill over the standard cap shows bank details only until a part payment brings it to ₦800,000).
- **Sub: an amount mismatch is held for review** — `PaystackWebhookTest` › "acceptance: holds an amount mismatch for review: a payment waiting for a member, never confirmed" (₦500,000 paid for ₦850,000 asked: pending payment with the reason in the note, no allocation, bill unpaid, no receipt, transaction `held` with `payment_id`, log `held`, audit `paystack.payment_held` with asked/paid kobo; a replay changes nothing; the payment is in the confirmation queue), › "holds a payment the bill no longer needs (paid meanwhile, or voided), and money in another currency" (USD: held, no payment; both listed on the settings page).
- **Sub: verify-by-fetch failure records nothing** — › "acceptance: records nothing when Paystack cannot be asked, and processes the delivery once it can" (Paystack 503: the queued job throws, nothing recorded, log keeps the error, tries 5 / backoff; then it succeeds), › "acceptance: records nothing when Paystack says the charge failed, was abandoned, or knows no such transaction"; › "records what Paystack says when asked, never what the webhook claims" (a signed event claiming ₦9,999,999 records Paystack's ₦850,000; the verify call used the organization's key; GoProperty's key never sent).
- **Sub: unknown webhook token → 404 without leaking** — › "answers the same plain 404 as any unknown address for an unknown, malformed, rotated or disconnected token, keeping nothing" (bodies identical to an unrelated unknown address; no organization or provider name; `webhook_log` empty; rotation kills the old address at once; a disconnected organization's address 404).
- **Sub: fast 2xx then queued** — › "keeps the raw body and answers 200 at once, with no session or cookies, then processes on the billing queue" (`Queue::fake`: 200, no cookies, nothing recorded, raw body kept, `last_event_at`, job on `billing`; the worker runs it outside any context → confirmed), › "needs no CSRF token and starts no session…" (route has no `web` middleware), › 413 for > 64 KB, › events/charges GoProperty did not start are `ignored` without a Paystack call.

- Not run: MySQL 8 (no MySQL here); real Paystack (faked; PRE-03 provides test accounts); no browser smoke of the pages (HTTP/Livewire tests only).

**Orchestrator review:** read `Webhooks\PaystackWebhooks`: the token is shape-checked before the lookup (decision 2 pattern), the signature is HMAC-SHA512 of the raw body with that organization's own secret, compared with `hash_equals`, and an invalid delivery is logged as rejected. Merged `main` (with P03-09) into the branch; resolved two conflicts by keeping both sides (ARCHITECTURE.md § 6.4 webhook URL and the P03-09 cash/POS wording, plus both new sections; the cross-tenant probes for Paystack and proof models with their shared closing lines). `composer ci` locally: Pint passed, Larastan 0 errors, Pest 2,767 tests: 2,723 passed, 44 skipped (Gotenberg). **GitHub Actions** run [40585994297](https://github.com/thekingchild/Goproperty/actions/runs/40585994297): green on MySQL 8.0/8.4 and MariaDB 10.6/11.4.

**Decisions to confirm** (orchestrator decisions relied on, and choices made here):
- Relied on **decision 2:** `/webhooks/paystack/{webhook_token}`, random (48 lowercase letters/digits) and rotatable on the connection; `webhook_log.organization_id` nullable (the table did not exist yet, so it is created here, in Core, with that column; P06-03's platform rows will have it null); lookup = token → organization id by one unscoped column read, then `OrganizationContext::run`.
- Relied on **decision 8 (plan gating):** built ungated; **own Paystack is Growth+ (BILLING § 1.2) → P06-01 follow-up** (gate `PaystackOption::offers()` / `PaystackTransactions::offers()` and the settings page's connect).
- Relied on **P03-10a's suspended-organization decision:** verified Paystack payments still record (system recorder) while the organization's members cannot change the connection; tenants of a suspended organization can still pay online (money to the agency's account; tenants not punished, ONBOARDING § 3).
- Relied on the **PO rules:** history stays — `paystack_transactions` and `webhook_log` rows are never changed in substance or deleted; payments are recorded through Payments' contract only (`Modules/Payments` not edited).
- **Online-payment cap default ₦1,000,000** when `online_payment_cap_kobo` is null (ONBOARDING § 4.2 says "default from Paystack's limits for that account", but Paystack's API does not report per-account limits); owners set ₦1,000–₦100,000,000. Product owner: confirm the default.
- **"Held for review"** = a payment waiting in the existing confirmation queue (`PaymentRecorder::record`, reason in the note) — so the agency's member decides — not platform staff (BILLING § 4.3 rule 2 is about GoProperty's own invoices). Held when: amount ≠ asked, the bill void or owing less than paid (e.g. paid by transfer meanwhile). Money in another currency (or a test/live mismatch) cannot be a kobo payment: no payment, listed on the settings page for the owner to refund or record by hand.
- **Only transactions GoProperty started are recorded** (`paystack_transactions`): a `charge.success` for any other reference on the agency's account (e.g. their shop) is `ignored`, so a webhook can never create a payment for a bill it merely names in its metadata.
- **Pay online is a link to a module page, not a form on the pay page:** the pay page's CSP (`form-action 'self'`) would block the redirect to Paystack; the module page has its own CSP allowing `checkout.paystack.com`. Redirect flow, no Paystack Inline script anywhere.
- **The payer's email** is asked on that page (Paystack requires one; the pay page never knows or shows the tenant's contacts); it is sent to Paystack only, not stored.
- **Return from checkout** settles too (queued, unique per transaction) — the fallback ONBOARDING § 4.2 asks for when the webhook is missing; a scheduled sweep of still-`initialized` transactions is a follow-up.
- **Test keys are refused in production**; elsewhere test or live are accepted. Keys are tried with `GET /transaction?perPage=1`. "Not tested yet" (Paystack unreachable) saves the keys but offers no online payment until a test passes.
- **Disconnect erases both keys** (risk R10) but keeps the row and token, so reconnecting needs no new address; deliveries to a disconnected organization's address get 404 (Paystack retries for a while).
- **Unknown tokens are not logged** in `webhook_log` (there is no organization to file them under, and keeping anonymous bodies invites flooding); BILLING § 4.2's "unverified payloads logged" holds for every delivery to a real organization's address.
- **Webhook answers:** 401 for a wrong signature (Paystack retries non-2xx, harmless), 200 `{"received":true}` otherwise; 300 a minute per IP.
- Settings route named `workspace.settings.payments.paystack` (address `/settings/paystack`); Core's sidebar "Settings" item now also matches `workspace.settings.payments.*` (a name pattern, no module named in Core). The wizard's view names the route string only when `Route::has()` (like `ChecklistItem::routeName()`).
- **No new permission:** the existing owner-only `payment_settings.view` / `organization.payment_settings` (already classed for suspension and impersonation).
- Held payment notes are stored in plain English (not translated at write time), like other stored reasons.

**Management handover (P03-14) — what this task's tables need:** nothing moves. `organization_paystack_connections` are the organization's settings (the receiving agency collects with its own Paystack); `paystack_transactions` and the organization's `webhook_log` rows are history of the organization that started/received them. An initialized online payment for a bill that later moves stays the old organization's (its money settles in the old organization's account).

- P06-01: gate own Paystack to Growth+ (BILLING § 1.2) in `PaystackTransactions::offers()` and on connecting; the wizard text already says "Available from the Growth plan".
- Scheduled re-verification of `initialized` transactions older than ~1 h (ONBOARDING § 4.2 "periodic verification of pending references"; R3) — `PaystackTransactions::settle()` is ready for it.
- P03-11a: "Pay online" in the tenant portal (reuse `PaystackOption::link()` / `PaystackTransactions::offers()`).
- P06-07 console webhooks view (PLATFORM_CONSOLE.md § 2 "per connected agency account; replay button"): `webhook_log` with `organization_id`; replay = re-queue `ProcessPaystackWebhook` (idempotent).
- Refunds of held foreign-currency payments are done by the agency in Paystack; a "record by hand" shortcut from the held list is not built.
- Key rotation runbook: `APP_KEY` rotation must re-encrypt `organization_paystack_connections.secret_key`.
- ONBOARDING § 5 tier limits / P06-09 compliance flags on online payments (not in this task).

### P03-09 — Complete (2026-10-27): proofs of payment (tenant from the portal through `Contracts\PaymentProofs`, member upload page), SHA-256 duplicate flag shown to the reviewer, proofs as evidence (archive only), proof review in the confirmation queue, upload rate limits; cash/POS confirmed as recorded with the receipt PDF sent straight to the tenant

**Delivered** ([ONBOARDING_COMPLIANCE.md § 4.1, § 4.3](../ONBOARDING_COMPLIANCE.md); [ARCHITECTURE.md § 4, § 5 "Proofs of payment", § 6.4 "How proofs of payment and cash receipts are built", § 9, § 10](../ARCHITECTURE.md); [DATABASE_SCHEMA.md § 4](../DATABASE_SCHEMA.md) `payment_proofs` as built; [PLATFORM_CONSOLE.md § 6](../PLATFORM_CONSOLE.md); AGENT.md § 3 rules 5–7, 9; `docs/plans/P02-P03-subtasks.md` P03-09):
- **Schema (module migration, one table):** `Modules/Payments/database/migrations/2026_11_14_090901_create_payment_proofs_table` — `payment_id` (FK, **unique**), `tenant_profile_id`, `bill_id` (nullable), `amount_kobo`, `paid_on`, `sender_name`, `bank_name`, `reference`, `note`, `media_id`, `file_sha256`, `first_upload_sha256` (**unique per organization**, set only on the first proof with a hash), `duplicate_of_proof_id` (self FK), `submitted_by` (`tenant`/`member`), `submitted_by_user_id`, `submitted_at` (`useCurrent()`, written by the service), `archived_at`/`_by_user_id`/`archive_reason`, timestamps; indexes `(org, file_sha256)`, `(org, tenant, submitted_at)`, `(org, bill_id)`.
- **Model** `Models\PaymentProof` (organization-scoped, `HasMedia`): refuses delete and every change except `media_id` once and the archive once. `Enums\ProofSubmitter`, `Enums\ProofStatus` (waiting / accepted / rejected from the payment, or archived; worked out). Added to `tests/Support/OrganizationScopedModels.php`.
- **Media:** the module's own evidence collection `payment_proofs` (JPEG, PNG, WebP, PDF; 10 MB; `thumb` for images), merged into `config('media.collections')` from `PaymentsServiceProvider::register()` (config/media.php allows it; avoids touching the shared config list). `config/media.php`'s `documents` comment no longer claims payment proofs.
- **`Proofs\ProofSubmissions`** (the only writer; implements **`Contracts\PaymentProofs`** for the P03-11b portal: `maySubmit`, `submitForBill` → `SubmittedProof`): input checked (amount naira → kobo, date paid ≤ today Lagos, sender, bank, narration, note), file checked by `UploadValidator` before anything is written, rate limits, then one transaction: **`PaymentService::recordProof`** (new: bank transfer, `pending_confirmation`, source `proof_upload`, `recorded_by` = submitter, audited `payment.recorded`, `PaymentRecorded` → tenant told it was received) + proof row + file via `MediaUploads`; audited `payment_proof.submitted` (log `payments`). `submitForMember` (member, `tenantId`/`billId`, narrowed through `PaymentService`). `archive` (reason 3–500, locked, audited `payment_proof.archived`; the payment unchanged). `sameFile`.
- **Duplicate detection:** SHA-256 of the bytes within the organization; first proof claims `first_upload_sha256` (unique), a later insert hits the key (waiting for a racing upload to commit), re-reads the first with a locking read and stores `duplicate_of_proof_id`. **Flagged, never auto-rejected.** Queue badges "Proof" and "Same file as another proof" (`Lists\PaymentRows`: two queries per page, both directions), the payment page lists the other uploads (linked only where the member may see the payment; "A proof of a tenant you cannot open" otherwise; never another organization's).
- **Rate limits:** `PaymentProofs::PER_BILL_PER_HOUR` = 5, `PER_TENANT_PER_DAY` = 20, whoever submits; a stored proof counts; refusal = `proof` validation error with minutes to wait.
- **Policy `PaymentProofPolicy`:** `viewAny`/`view` (`payments.view`, narrowed: a proof follows its tenant → 404), `viewFile` (`#[BlockedWhileImpersonating]`), `create` (member upload, `payments.confirm`, blocked while impersonating), **`submitAsTenant`** (bill in this organization, issued to the tenant record linked to the user's portal account, not archived → else 404; void / paid bill → 403; `#[AllowedWhileSuspended]` + `#[BlockedWhileImpersonating]`), `archive` (`payments.confirm`, not yet archived, blocked while impersonating), `update`/`delete`/`forceDelete` always false.
- **Core (small):** `OrganizationWriteGuard::inspect` honours `#[AllowedWhileSuspended]` on a **policy method** (mirrors `ImpersonationGuard`'s policy-attribute check), so the tenant's proof passes the suspended-organization Gate check while every other write stays refused; `AllowedWhileSuspended` docblock updated. No new permission (`payments.submit_proof` existed and was classed; tenants hold no workspace role, so the portal identity is the check).
- **Pages:** `/o/{slug}/payments/proofs/create` (`Livewire\ProofForm`: tenant picker, the tenant's open bills, amount, date paid, sender, bank (datalist of `config/banks.php`), narration, note, file; `?tenant=`/`?bill=`), `/payments/proofs/{proof}/file` and `/thumb` (`Http\Controllers\ProofFileController`, throttle 60/min, policy then a signed `MediaUrls` redirect). Payment page (`PaymentShow`): "Proof of payment" section (fields, sent by, status, thumbnail and open link or "not shown in this session", duplicate warning, archive with reason). Payments list: "Upload a proof" button; record form: "Upload the tenant's proof instead".
- **Cash/POS:** the record form confirms cash and POS as they are recorded (`save` → `recordAndConfirm`; the only button for those methods is "Record and send the receipt"; note required as before). `Notices\TenantNotices` passes the receipt for a confirmed cash/POS payment; `Notifications\TenantPaymentNotice` attaches **the receipt PDF as issued** (`Receipts::pdf()`, the kept evidence file, rendered once then if the `pdf` queue has not) with "Your receipt is attached."; if the PDF cannot be read the email still goes (number + verify link) and the error is reported. Phone-only tenants: the agency's WhatsApp (stand-in sender), never SMS. Told once (existing `payment_notices`).
- **Docs:** ARCHITECTURE.md § 4 (write guard + portal proof), § 5 ("Proofs of payment" paragraph), § 6.4 (cash line, new "How proofs of payment and cash receipts are built"), § 9 (`payment_proofs` collection), § 10 (limits); DATABASE_SCHEMA.md § 4 `payment_proofs` as built; ONBOARDING_COMPLIANCE.md § 4.1 and § 4.3 as built; PLATFORM_CONSOLE.md § 6 (proof file, upload, archive, portal proof blocked while impersonating); `config/media.php` comment.

**Orchestrator review:** merged `main` (with P03-07a) into the branch; resolved two doc conflicts by keeping both sides (ARCHITECTURE.md: the statement-import and proof sections, and `statements` plus `payment_proofs` in the evidence collections; PLATFORM_CONSOLE.md § 6: both file-download rules and both blocked actions). `composer ci` locally: Pint passed, Larastan 0 errors, Pest 2,708 tests: 2,664 passed, 44 skipped (Gotenberg). **GitHub Actions** run [40578867559](https://github.com/thekingchild/Goproperty/actions/runs/40578867559): green on MySQL 8.0/8.4 and MariaDB 10.6/11.4.

**Decisions to confirm** (orchestrator decisions relied on, and choices made here):
- Relied on **decision 3 (no SMS/WhatsApp in P03):** a phone-only tenant gets the cash receipt through the `WhatsAppSender` stand-in; never SMS.
- Relied on **decision 4 (people and roles):** tenants have no `organization_user` rows, so a tenant's right to submit is their portal identity (`tenant_profiles.user_id`), not `payments.submit_proof`.
- Relied on **decision 9 (narrowing):** proofs follow their tenant like payments (TenantDirectory narrowing on `BuildingAccess`).
- Relied on **decision 8 (gating):** proof upload and cash receipts are in every plan — nothing for P06-01.
- Relied on the **PO rules:** proofs are evidence (own collection `payment_proofs`, never `photos`; never deleted; "remove" archives with the file kept); handover: nothing moves (below).
- **Suspended organizations (ONBOARDING § 3, § 5.5; P01-06):** tenants keep submitting proofs from the portal (Core guard change: `#[AllowedWhileSuspended]` honoured on policy methods); members only read, as P03-10a decided.
- **Duplicates are flagged, not refused**, both ways (the earlier upload shows the warning too); same file across organizations is not compared (privacy: an organization never learns about another's proofs).
- **The proof's payment is always a bank transfer** recorded by the submitter (`recorded_by_user_id` = the tenant's account for portal proofs).
- **Tenant proofs need a bill** that still asks for money (void → refused, paid → refused); members may upload without a bill.
- **Rate limits** 5 a bill an hour, 20 a tenant a day, counted on stored proofs, shared by tenant and member submissions.
- **The proof file is blocked while impersonating** (payer's bank details), like the printed receipt; typed details stay visible.
- **Cash/POS are confirmed when recorded from the form** (also when the form is submitted with Enter); `PaymentService::record()` still accepts a pending cash payment (existing tests and possible future callers). Product owner: confirm, or forbid pending cash at the service.
- **"Receipt sent straight to the tenant"** = the receipt PDF attached to the confirmation email (cash and POS only); bank transfers keep the link only (P03-10b). The in-app copy links the verify page until the portal's receipts (P03-11a/b).
- **Media collection registered from the module provider** instead of `config/media.php` (allowed by its header; keeps the module's rules in the module).
- **Contradiction noted:** P02-05's follow-up said payment proofs go into `documents`; the P03 product-owner rule (evidence in its own collection) wins → `payment_proofs`. DB § 4's `uploaded_by_user_id`/`uploaded_at` are `submitted_by_user_id`/`submitted_at` (documented).

**Management handover (P03-14) — what this task's tables need:** nothing moves. `payment_proofs` (with their `payment_proofs` media files) are history of the organization that received them, like their payments.

**Follow-ups:**
- P03-11b: the portal proof page on `Contracts\PaymentProofs` (`maySubmit`, `submitForBill`; errors keyed by input name, `file`, `proof`); per-IP HTTP throttle on its route; a tenant-side "remove" (archive) if wanted; link from the `/pay` page ("Paid already? Upload your proof in the portal").
- Notify members (in-app, "Today" inbox) when a proof arrives.
- P05: WhatsApp template with the receipt PDF; SMS copy for cash receipts (ONBOARDING § 4.3).
- A concurrency drill for racing duplicate uploads on MySQL 8 (the unique-key wait is the design; tested here only by the key refusing a second "first").
- Strip EXIF from proof images or serve only conversions to tenants (NDPA), as noted in P02-05.

### P03-07a — Complete (2026-10-27): bank statement import: the Collections module with uploaded statements kept as evidence, column mapping saved per bank account, CSV/XLSX parsing of Nigerian bank exports into exact kobo, de-duplicated statement lines (`line_hash` unique per organization), the import report, statement pages with export, owner/admin permissions

The parent **P03-07 stays In progress** until P03-07b (matching and confirm) is done.

**Delivered** ([ONBOARDING_COMPLIANCE.md § 4.1](../ONBOARDING_COMPLIANCE.md) incl. the new "Statement import as built (P03-07a)"; [DATABASE_SCHEMA.md § 4](../DATABASE_SCHEMA.md) `bank_statement_imports`, `bank_statement_lines`, new `bank_statement_column_mappings`; [ARCHITECTURE.md § 5](../ARCHITECTURE.md) "Bank statements", § 6.4 "How bank statement import is built", § 6.12, § 6.13, § 8, § 9; [PLATFORM_CONSOLE.md § 6](../PLATFORM_CONSOLE.md); AGENT.md § 3 rules 2, 7; `docs/plans/P02-P03-subtasks.md` P03-07a):
- **Module:** `Modules/Collections` (`module:make`, trimmed like Payments: no controller stub, seeder, event provider, config or factories), in `modules_statuses.json`. `CollectionsServiceProvider` registers `BankStatementImportPolicy`, the Livewire namespace `collections::`, the sidebar item "Bank statements" (order 480, after Payments; `building-library`) and the two list exports.
- **Schema (module migrations, one table each):** `2026_11_13_070101_create_bank_statement_imports_table` (DB § 4 columns plus `file_name`, the report's counts `lines_added`/`lines_credit`/`lines_debit`/`lines_duplicate`/`lines_unreadable`, `report` json (first 100 unreadable rows, rows that were not transactions), `failure_reason`, `processed_at`; `media_id` without FK), `…070102_create_bank_statement_lines_table` (DB § 4 columns plus `organization_bank_account_id`, `row_number`, `reference`, `occurrence`; **unique `(organization_id, line_hash)`**; `suggested_bill_id`/`payment_id` without FKs for 07b), `…070103_create_bank_statement_column_mappings_table` (one row per bank account: field => header text, date order; unique `(organization_id, organization_bank_account_id)`). No TIMESTAMP column without a default (`processed_at` nullable; `TimestampColumnsTest` green).
- **Models and enums:** `Models\BankStatementImport` (organization-scoped, `HasMedia`/`HasOrganizationMedia`, collection `statements`; refuses delete, before the media library's own listener), `BankStatementLine` (refuses delete; refuses any match state but `ignored` for a debit; `offeredForMatching()` scope = credits `unmatched`/`suggested`), `BankStatementColumnMapping`. Enums `StatementStatus`, `StatementFormat`, `LineDirection`, `MatchStatus`, `MatchConfidence`, `DateOrder`, `StatementField` (fields and Nigerian header aliases). All three models in `tests/Support/OrganizationScopedModels.php`.
- **Media:** new **evidence** collection `statements` in `config/media.php` (`.xlsx`/`.csv` with P02-06a's content checks, 10 MB).
- **Upload** (`Statements\StatementImports`, the only writer; policy `create` checked again; audited `statement.uploaded` under log `statements`, ids and counts only): account = any of the organization's accounts not removed (deactivated ones too); file through `UploadValidator` (collection `statements`); header found in the first 30 rows (`Statements\StatementLayout`: a row naming a date column and two other statement columns, else the first row with three cells, mapped by hand); **a statement whose heading names another 10-digit account number is refused** (compared with `BankAccounts::fingerprint`, never decrypting; a number cell that lost its leading zero is padded); mapping = the account's saved one when all its headers are in the file, else worked out from the headers, else the page's choices (`Statements\StatementMapping::problems()`: date, narration, credit/debit columns or one amount column (not both), Dr/Cr only with an amount, no column twice); the mapping is saved per account; the file stored as evidence; `Jobs\ProcessStatementImport` queued on `imports` after the commit (3 tries, backoff 30/120 s, timeout 50 s; `failed()` marks the statement failed).
- **Reading** (`Statements\StatementReader`): rows stream through the Import module's new **`Modules\Import\Contracts\SpreadsheetReader`** (bound to `Reading\Spreadsheets` on the engine's `SpreadsheetFile` and `LocalCopy` — `LocalCopy::temporaryFile()` added; reader failures as `Contracts\UnreadableSpreadsheet`), so Collections never touches Import's internals; `Statements\StatementRowParser` → transaction, nothing (blank, titles, opening/closing balance, totals) or `UnreadableRow` with the reason. Money: separate debit/credit columns (a minus in the debit column ignored; a negative credit or both on one row unreadable) or one signed amount column (negative, brackets or DR = debit) or amount + Dr/Cr column. `Statements\StatementAmount`: text straight into `Money::naira` (commas, ₦/NGN/N, spaces, brackets, trailing minus, CR/DR marks); an Excel number only when its `%.2F` text is exactly the same double; more than two decimals refused, never rounded. `Statements\StatementDate`: Excel date cells and serials, d/m/Y, d-m-y, d.m.Y, 31-Jan-2026, 31 January 2026, 31JAN26, Jan 31, 2026, 2026-01-31 (times ignored), 20260131; numbers-only dates day first unless the mapping says month or year first; not after tomorrow (Lagos), not before 2000. Lines inserted 500 at a time with `insertOrIgnore` on the unique hash; debits stored `ignored`, credits `unmatched`; at most 20,000 transactions a statement; report counts taken from the table at the end (a retried job counts right); audited `statement.processed` (counts only) or `statement.failed`.
- **`line_hash`** (`Statements\LineHash`) = SHA-256 of `v1`, the bank account id, the date, the direction, the amount in kobo, the narration normalised (lower case, letters and digits, single spaces), the running balance in kobo (when the statement has a balance column), the bank reference normalised (when it has one) and the **occurrence** (1 for the first line with all of that in the file, 2 for the next identical one, …). So the same statement uploaded twice (CSV or Excel), or an overlapping statement, adds only new lines, also when two uploads run at once (the unique key decides); two genuinely identical credits of one day stay two lines (balance or reference, else occurrence). Limitation: occurrence counts within one file, so it assumes statements cover whole days; a statement cut mid-day, of an account whose export has neither balance nor reference, could take the second of two identical lines of that day for the first.
- **Permissions:** new `statements.view` (owner, admin; kept while suspended; not blocked while impersonating) and `statements.import` (owner, admin; dropped while suspended; **blocked while impersonating**), both classed in `allowedWhileSuspended()` and `blockedWhileImpersonating()`; `OrganizationRole::Admin` gets both (owners get every permission). `Policies\BankStatementImportPolicy`: viewAny/view/export (`statements.view`), create (`statements.import`, `#[BlockedWhileImpersonating]`), downloadFile (view + `#[BlockedWhileImpersonating]`: the file prints the full account number), update/delete always false.
- **Pages (`collections::`, routes in the module):** `/o/{slug}/statements` (`StatementIndex`: newest first, 25 a page, filters account and status in the URL, polls while one is being read, export `bank_statements`), `/statements/upload` (`StatementUpload`: account + file → "Check the columns" reads the header and offers the mapping with the first 3 rows, "Filled in from this account's last statement" when the saved one fits → "Import the statement"; nothing stored before Import; actions `#[BlockedWhileImpersonating]`; "No bank account yet" without accounts), `/statements/{id}` (`StatementShow`: report — period, transactions read, new lines, credits to match, debits kept never matched, duplicates skipped, unreadable rows with reasons — then the lines in file order, 50 a page, "Credits to match" / "All lines", filters direction, match status, search over narration/other party/reference; credits marked "To match", debits "Not matched (debit)"; polls while reading; export `bank_statement_lines`), `/statements/{id}/file` (`StatementFileController`, throttled: signed `MediaUrls` link after `downloadFile`).
- **Exports:** `Lists\StatementList` (key `bank_statements`: "Bank account (masked)", file, format, period, counts, status, uploader, time) and `Lists\StatementLineList` (key `bank_statement_lines`: date, narration, other party, reference, credit, debit, balance, match status, account masked, statement, row); registered from the provider.
- **Docs:** DATABASE_SCHEMA.md § 4 (as built for both tables, new `bank_statement_column_mappings`, handover note; `media` collections), ARCHITECTURE.md § 5 ("Bank statements"), § 6.4 ("How bank statement import is built"), § 6.12 (the `SpreadsheetReader` contract), § 6.13, § 8, § 9; ONBOARDING_COMPLIANCE.md § 4.1 ("Statement import as built"); PLATFORM_CONSOLE.md § 6 (statements export allowed; upload and file download blocked).
