- **Queue / idempotency / failure / no deletes:** › "reads the lines on the imports queue, once, and a retried job adds nothing twice" (`Queue::assertPushedOn('imports')`, timeout 50; processing page polls; re-run after a reset counts 5 added, 0 duplicates), › "marks a statement failed, with the reason, when its file cannot be read, and keeps the file", › "never deletes a statement, its file or its lines".
- **Cross-org probes:** `CrossTenantAccessTest` (datasets `BankStatementImport`, `BankStatementLine`, `BankStatementColumnMapping`); `StatementAccessTest` › "answers 404 for another organization's statement guessed by ID, and never shows its lines" (page and file 404, unknown/malformed ids, never listed, its lines never in our search or export, `StatementLineList` with their statement id finds nothing, tampered snapshot 419 with debug off, their owner 404 on ours, a member of both sees each one's own).
- **Narrowed agents (decision: agents see no statement):** › "shows agents no statement, whether or not they are assigned to buildings" (unassigned and narrowed agents: no sidebar item, list/page/upload/file 403, service refuses the upload, export authorisation refused, an export call on an owner's snapshot 403); role matrix › "lets only org-owners and org-admins see and upload statements" (5 roles × 7 abilities), › pages (owner/admin 200 + file redirect to `/media/`; property owner/tenant/stranger 404).
- **Suspended organization:** › "keeps statements read-only while the organization is suspended: read and export, no upload" (list/page/export work, no upload button, upload page 403, `read` call 403, service refuses).
- **Impersonation:** › "lets impersonating staff look at statements and export them, but never upload one or download its file, in either mode" (read-only and write: pages open, no upload/download buttons, upload page and file 403, `read`/`import` calls on an owner's snapshot 403, export works, `impersonation.blocked` audited in write mode).
- **List exports:** › "exports the statements list and a statement's lines, in Excel and CSV, with the page's filters" (exact headers and rows, naira text, Lagos time, masked account, labels; credits-to-match and search filters); `ListExportCoverageTest` (a statement in `beforeEach`; the statement page's lines added to the walked pages; registry gains `bank_statement_lines`, `bank_statements`).
- Existing tests changed (none weakened): `ImpersonationGuardTest` blocked list (+`statements.import`), `SuspendedOrganizationTest` kept list (+`statements.view`), `EvidenceFilesTest` (+`statements` evidence), `WorkspaceNavigationTest` (owner and admin see `statements`), `OrganizationScopedModels` (+3 models), `ListExportCoverageTest` (statement fixture and page, expected registry; **clears the export rate limit before each export**: with 15 lists the walk now makes more than `ListExportRequests::PER_HOUR` = 30 exports as one member, and the 31st was refused — a test-only change, the limit is unchanged).
- MySQL JSON key order: maps read from JSON (`columns`, `report.unreadable`) compared with `toEqual` or read by key; no `toBe` on JSON maps.
- Not run: MySQL 8 (`INSERT IGNORE` and the unique key behave the same there; watch the first CI run); no browser smoke (HTTP/Livewire tests only).

**Orchestrator review:** merged `main` (with P03-10b receipts) into the branch; resolved eight conflicts by keeping both sides (permissions, the Admin role, sidebar and export-coverage expectations, the impersonation list, the cross-tenant probes, ARCHITECTURE.md and PLATFORM_CONSOLE.md § 6), then restored one closing line the probe merge had dropped. `composer ci` locally: Pint passed, Larastan 0 errors, Pest 2,666 tests: one load-only timeout in `MediaConversionsTest` (passes alone, 7/7; another agent's suite was running on the machine), 44 skipped (Gotenberg). **GitHub Actions** run [40572449543](https://github.com/thekingchild/Goproperty/actions/runs/40572449543): green on MySQL 8.0/8.4 and MariaDB 10.6/11.4.

**Decisions to confirm** (orchestrator decisions relied on, and choices made here):
- Relied on the **product owner rules**: uploaded statements are evidence (media collection `statements`, kind `evidence`; the import row and its lines refuse deletion); statement lines are the organization's history (never deleted; stay in a handover).
- Relied on **decision 8 (gating):** BILLING § 1.2 puts "bank transfer with statement matching" on every plan → nothing gated, nothing for P06-01.
- Relied on **decision 9 (narrowing)** by deciding **agents see no statement at all** (the task asked to decide): a statement is the whole bank account's history (every building's rent and the agency's own outgoings), and its lines carry no building to narrow by through `BuildingAccess`; owners and administrators upload and see statements. Payments made from matched lines reach agents through the payments pages (narrowed by tenant). ONBOARDING § 4.1 says "the agent confirms matches": P03-07b must decide whether agents get a narrowed "matches to confirm" view (e.g. through suggested bills of their buildings) or confirming stays owner/admin.
- **Impersonation:** staff may view statements and export both lists (dates, narrations, amounts; no contact details, like payments), but never upload (`statements.import` blocked) nor download the uploaded file (it prints the full account number, which `payment_settings.view` never shows them).
- **Suspended:** `statements.view` kept, `statements.import` dropped (an upload creates records); P03-07b decides whether matching continues for a suspended organization.
- **Wrong bank account** = an account id that is not one of this organization's accounts (another organization's, removed, unknown) or a statement whose heading names another account number; a statement without an account number in its heading cannot be checked and is taken for the chosen account.
- **Debits are stored** (as `ignored`), not dropped: DB § 4 has `direction` debit and `match_status` ignored, the statement page shows the whole statement, and the running balance keeps the hash stable; they are never offered for matching. The report's "debits ignored" = new debit lines kept but never matched.
- **Line hash** extends ONBOARDING § 4.1's "date + amount + narration + balance" with the bank account, direction, bank reference and occurrence (documented in DB § 4 and ONBOARDING § 4.1).
- **Mapping per bank account** in a new table `bank_statement_column_mappings` (DB § 4 has only the import's own `column_mapping`, which is kept as the mapping used for that file).
- **Deactivated accounts take statements** (late payments into an old account); removed ones do not.
- **Size:** 10 MB and 20,000 transactions a statement (one job, well within 50 s); `.xls` and PDF not read (as P02-06a: save `.xls` as `.xlsx`; PDF statements later per ONBOARDING § 4.1).
- **No remove/archive of a wrongly uploaded statement** in this task: its file is evidence and its lines history (see follow-ups).
- **Import module touched** (not its behaviour): a public `SpreadsheetReader` contract (+ `UnreadableSpreadsheet`) and `LocalCopy::temporaryFile()`, so Collections reuses the engine's reader through a contract instead of its internals.

**Management handover (P03-14):** nothing in this task moves. `bank_statement_imports`, `bank_statement_lines` and `bank_statement_column_mappings` belong to the organization's bank accounts (no building link); uploaded files are evidence; payments matched from lines (07b) stay with the organization that recorded them.

**Follow-ups:**
- P03-07b: matcher on `BankStatementLine::offeredForMatching()` (reference in narration = high; amount + similar name = medium), `suggested_bill_id`/`match_confidence`, `payment_id` and `lines_matched`; payments through `PaymentRecorder` (`statement_match`, key `statement-line:{id}`, `bankStatementLineId`); add the FK on `payments.bank_statement_line_id` (P03-10a follow-up); who confirms (agents? see decisions); a line matched once only (unique `payment_id` or status check under a row lock).
- An "archive this statement" action for a statement uploaded to the wrong account (no account number in its heading to catch it): mark its unmatched lines `ignored` (audited), keep the file and the lines (evidence/history). Product owner to confirm.
- PDF statements (ONBOARDING § 4.1 "PDF later").
- Home "Today" inbox: statement matches to confirm (UX § 1).
- The file's sha256 is stored (`custom_properties`) but not re-checked when read (P02-05 follow-up).
- Upload rate limiting (owners/admins only, 10 MB cap; not limited per hour).
- MySQL 8.0/8.4 not run locally: watch the first CI run.

**Next:** P03-10b runs alongside; then P03-09, and P03-07b (matching and confirm) once P03-10b is in.

### P03-10b — Complete (2026-10-27): receipts: a numbered receipt issued in the confirming transaction (per organization, QR/verification code, branding and parties kept, "confirmed by"), its PDF rendered once on the `pdf` queue and kept as evidence, receipt voids with a second approver (single-approver path flagged), VOID copy as a new file, `receipt` on `/verify`, the tenant told once about every payment transition, receipt pages with export

With it the parent **P03-10 is complete** (P03-10a `PaymentService` + P03-10b receipts; the parent AC "overpayment goes to tenant credit and is applied to the next bill; voided bills accept nothing; concurrent confirmations of the same bill cannot over-settle (locked transaction test); void needs a second approver" all pass).

**Delivered** ([ARCHITECTURE.md § 5 "Receipts", § 6.4 "How receipts are built", § 6.5, § 6.13](../ARCHITECTURE.md); [DATABASE_SCHEMA.md § 4](../DATABASE_SCHEMA.md) `receipts` as built, new `payment_notices`, integrity rules 5 and 8; [BRANDING_DOCUMENTS.md § 5](../BRANDING_DOCUMENTS.md) "How it is built (P03-10b: receipts)"; [PLATFORM_CONSOLE.md § 6](../PLATFORM_CONSOLE.md); [ONBOARDING_COMPLIANCE.md § 4.3](../ONBOARDING_COMPLIANCE.md); AGENT.md § 3 rules 5–7, 9, § 4 Documents; `docs/plans/P02-P03-subtasks.md` P03-10b):
- **Schema (module migrations, one change each, explicit TIMESTAMP defaults):** `Modules/Payments/database/migrations/2026_11_12_100201_create_receipts_table` (DB § 4 columns plus `tenant_profile_id`, `amount_kobo`, `branding_snapshot`/`details` json, `void_requested_at`/`_by_user_id`/`void_request_reason`, `void_single_approver`, `void_document_media_id`; `payment_id` **unique**; **unique `(organization_id, number)`** (decision 7); `verification_code` unique; `issued_at` `useCurrent()`; indexes for issue date, tenant, void requests, voids), `…100202_add_receipt_foreign_key_to_payments_table` (`payments.receipt_id` → `receipts`, restrict), `…100203_create_payment_notices_table` (unique `(payment_id, event)`, `channels` json).
- **Models:** `Models\Receipt` (organization-scoped, `HasMedia`; refuses delete and any change to what was issued — only the two media ids once each, the void request while valid, and the void once), `Models\PaymentNotice` (never changed or deleted), `Enums\ReceiptStatus` (valid / void requested / voided, worked out). Both added to `tests/Support/OrganizationScopedModels.php`.
- **Issue (`Receipts\Receipts`, `Receipts\ReceiptDetails`, `Listeners\IssueReceipt`):** on `PaymentConfirmed`, **inside the confirming transaction**: under the payment's row lock, once per payment — number from `DocumentNumberer::next(receipt)` with the snapshot's prefix (per organization and year, gap-free; a rolled-back confirmation gives it back), `VerificationCodes::issue('receipt')`, `BrandingSnapshots::take()`, the receipt's parties/payment/allocations/balances/"confirmed by"/channels kept in `details` through the Tenants, Properties, Leasing and Billing contracts; `payments.receipt_id` filled; audit `receipt.issued` (log `payments`). `PaymentService` unchanged in money terms; it now also sends `Events\PaymentRecorded` (only for a payment left waiting; not for `recordAndConfirm` or `recordConfirmed`).
- **PDF:** `Jobs\RenderReceipt` on `pdf` after the commit (3 tries, backoff 30/120 s, timeout 50 s) → `DocumentRenderer::stored($receipt, 'receipt', …)` into the receipt's `issued_documents` media (evidence), `document_media_id`; the download renders it then, once, if the queue has not.
- **Void (`Receipts\ReceiptVoids`):** `request` (reason 3–500; `payments.confirm`: owners, admins, agents; the other approvers get `Notifications\ReceiptVoidRequested`, app + email), `approve` (new permission **`receipts.approve_void`**: org-owner, org-admin; never the requester — policy denial and a re-check under the receipt lock), `dismiss` (withdraw / decline), `voidAlone` (only when exactly one member holds `receipts.approve_void`, i.e. a single owner and no administrator: `void_single_approver` = true, audited "Receipt voided by the single approver"). Audits `receipt.void_requested`, `receipt.void_dismissed`, `receipt.voided` (requester, approver, single_approver, reason); `Events\ReceiptVoided`; the **VOID copy is a new kept file** (`RenderReceipt` with `void`, key `receipt-void`, `void_document_media_id`), the original PDF untouched. The payment is not changed.
- **Verify:** `Receipts\ReceiptVerifier` registered as kind `receipt`: "Official receipt", issuer as printed, number, date, amount, paid on, method; **valid** / **voided** ("Voided on dd/mm/yyyy"). No tenant, property, reason or bank detail.
- **Tenant notices (`Notices\TenantNotices`, `Notices\NoticeChannels`, `Notices\PaymentNoticeEvent`, `Notifications\TenantPaymentNotice`, `Listeners\TellTenantAboutPayment` after commit):** recorded / confirmed (receipt number + verify link) / rejected (reason) / receipt voided (reason). A `payment_notices` row is written first (unique per payment and event), so each transition is told **once** even when handled twice. Channels: `database` with a portal account (`TenantSummary::$userId`, new), `mail` to the account's or else the record's address, and only without any address `whatsapp` through `App\WhatsApp\WhatsAppSender` (stand-in sender; decision 3). **Never SMS.** The receipt prints where its copy went.
- **Policy `ReceiptPolicy`:** viewAny/view/export (`payments.view`), `viewDocument` (web page, PDF, VOID copy; `#[BlockedWhileImpersonating]`: it prints the tenant's phone and email), `voidRequest`, `voidApprove`, `voidAlone`, `voidDismiss` (all `void…` + `#[BlockedWhileImpersonating]`), update/delete never; 404 outside a narrowed agent's tenants (a receipt follows its tenant, as payments).
- **Pages (`payments::`):** `/o/{slug}/receipts` (`ReceiptIndex`: 25 a page, newest first, search number/tenant, status filter with the "Voids to approve" queue and count, tenant filter, URL state; export `Lists\ReceiptList` + `ReceiptRows`, one lookup per page), `/receipts/{id}` (`ReceiptShow`: details, applied to, balances, links, void request / approve / decline / withdraw / void alone), `/receipts/{id}/document`, `/pdf`, `/void.pdf` (`Http\Controllers\ReceiptDocumentController`, throttle 60/min, signed `MediaUrls` links, `no-store`). Sidebar item "Receipts" (order 475). The payment page links its receipt.
- **Core (minimal):** `OrganizationPermission::ApproveReceiptVoids` (classed: dropped while suspended, blocked while impersonating; granted to org-admin, org-owner has all), `App\Members\Members::holdersOf($permission)`. Nothing added to `app/Documents`. **Tenants:** `TenantSummary` gains `userId` (portal account; default null).
- **Docs:** ARCHITECTURE.md § 5 (Receipts paragraph), § 6.4 ("How receipts are built"), § 6.5 (receipt row as built), § 6.13; DATABASE_SCHEMA.md § 4 (`receipts` as built, `payment_notices`, `payments.receipt_id` FK), integrity rules 5 and 8; BRANDING_DOCUMENTS.md § 5 (P03-10b); PLATFORM_CONSOLE.md § 6 (receipts export allowed, printed receipt blocked, `receipts.approve_void`, void abilities); ONBOARDING_COMPLIANCE.md § 4.3 (as built).

The payment drill ran green three times (twice alone, once in `composer ci`).

- **Sub: each transition notifies the tenant exactly once (mail + database; no SMS)** — `PaymentNoticesTest` › "tells the tenant once each when a payment is recorded, confirmed, another rejected, and the receipt voided" (5 notices, each `database` + `mail`, contents checked; every event replayed and the listener called directly → still 5; `payment_notices` 5; channels only database/mail), › "keeps the in-app copies and sends the emails for real" (notifications rows + array mailer), › "tells the tenant once when a payment is recorded and confirmed in one step: the confirmation" (cash `recordAndConfirm`, Paystack `recordConfirmed`, statement `record` replayed), › "tells nobody about a step that is refused or rolled back", › "emails a tenant without a portal account at the address on record, with no in-app copy", › "sends a phone-only tenant the agency's WhatsApp (the stand-in sender), never an SMS or an email" (and nothing outside the app where WhatsApp is unavailable).
- **A confirmed payment issues exactly one receipt even if the event is handled twice** — `ReceiptIssueTest` › "acceptance: a confirmed payment issues exactly one receipt even if the confirmation is handled twice" (event re-sent outside and inside a transaction, listener called, `issue()` called: one receipt, one code, counter at 1, one audit, one PDF job; a raw second row refused by the unique key).

Suspended: › "voids nothing in a suspended organization, while its receipts stay readable and printable".

**Orchestrator review:** the branch already contained `main` (`36a2478`). **GitHub Actions** run [40560855677](https://github.com/thekingchild/Goproperty/actions/runs/40560855677): green on MySQL 8.0/8.4 and MariaDB 10.6/11.4, 2,591 passed with **none skipped** — the receipt Chrome parity checks ran against real Gotenberg.

**Decisions to confirm** (orchestrator decisions relied on, and choices made here):
- Relied on **decision 4 (people and roles):** request by `payments.confirm` (owner, admin, agent) with a reason; approval by a different member holding the new `receipts.approve_void` (org-owner, org-admin); single-approver = exactly one member holds `receipts.approve_void` (one owner, no admin): that member voids alone with a typed reason, `void_single_approver` + audit. Tenants never get workspace rows (they hold none of these).
- Relied on **decision 3 (no SMS/WhatsApp in P03):** tenants with no email get the agency's WhatsApp through the `WhatsAppSender` stand-in (sends nothing; never "available" in production, so in production a phone-only tenant gets in-app copies only, when they have a portal account); never SMS.
- Relied on **decision 7:** `receipts.number` unique per `(organization_id, number)`; numbers per organization and year from `document_counters`.
- Relied on **decision 9 (narrowing):** a receipt follows its tenant (TenantDirectory narrowing), like payments.
- Relied on **decision 6 (Gotenberg):** the Chrome half skips only when Gotenberg does not answer; fails on CI if unreachable.
- Relied on **decision 8 (gating):** receipts are in every plan — nothing for P06-01.
- Relied on the **PO rules:** receipts and their PDFs are evidence/history (never deleted; the VOID copy is a second file; the issued columns are frozen); handover: nothing moves (below).
- **Receipt issued inside the confirming transaction** (not after commit as lease contracts are): a confirmed payment always has its receipt and the number is gap-free with the confirmation (AGENT.md § 4 "numbering inside the issuing transaction"); the price is that a receipt failure refuses the confirmation. The PDF is rendered after the commit.
- **Voiding a receipt does not touch the payment** (it stays confirmed and allocated); undoing money is the not-yet-built reversal. No replacement receipt is issued automatically (the payment keeps `receipt_id` of the voided one). Product owner: confirm, or ask for "void and re-issue" / "void and reverse".
- **Approvers are told of a request** (app + email); requesters can withdraw, approvers decline; both leave the receipt valid (audited).
- **Notices:** a `payment_notices` table (not in DB § 4) makes "exactly once" durable; `PaymentRecorded` is a new event, not sent when a payment is recorded and confirmed in one step (the tenant hears once: confirmed). Rejection reasons are shown to the tenant. The PDF is **linked** (receipt number + verify address), not attached: it renders on its own queue; the portal's receipt download is P03-11a.
- **Receipt prints no "paid into" account** (a payment does not record which account received it); "how it was confirmed" labels per source/method (see BRANDING § 5).
- **Printed receipt (web/PDF) blocked while impersonating** (tenant phone/email), like contracts and invoices; the list exports while impersonating (names, amounts, void reasons; no contact details).
- **`TenantSummary::$userId`** added to the Tenants contract (default null) so notices reach the portal account.

**Management handover (P03-14) — what this task's tables need:** nothing moves. `receipts` (with their `issued_documents` PDFs and `receipt` verification codes) and `payment_notices` are history of the organization that confirmed the payment, like the payments themselves.

- P03-11a: receipts in the tenant portal (list, web view, PDF download through `ReceiptPolicy`-like portal rules); link the in-app notices there.
- P05: WhatsApp template mapping for `payment_recorded`, `receipt_issued`, `payment_rejected`, `receipt_voided`; SMS copy for cash receipts (ONBOARDING § 4.3); attach the receipt PDF; `notification_deliveries`.
- P03-13b: imported payment history must confirm **without** a receipt by default ("history issues no receipts unless asked"): `IssueReceipt` currently issues on every `PaymentConfirmed` — add a flag on the event/draft (e.g. `issueReceipt: false`) for imports.
- P03-09: proof-confirmed payments print "Proof reviewed" already (source `proof_upload`).
- Payment reversal / "void and re-issue" (product owner decision above).
- Home "Today" inbox: count of voids to approve.

### P03-05c — Complete (2026-10-27): invoices issued from bills (branding and parties kept on the bill at issue, PDF rendered once on the `pdf` queue and kept as evidence, VOID copy on void), invoice web page and PDF on the bill page, `invoice` on `/verify` with the current status, and the branded public pay page `/pay/{reference}` with the organization's bank details and a payment-options slot

With it the parent **P03-05 is complete** (P03-05a document kit + P03-05b templates and `/verify` + P03-05c invoice issue and `/pay`; the parent AC "invoice and receipt render identically (visual diff) with dompdf and Chrome, on one A4 page for up to 8 line items" passed in P03-05b and is re-proven here on a real issued invoice, and "changing branding later does not change issued documents" passes here).

**Delivered** ([BRANDING_DOCUMENTS.md § 1, § 5](../BRANDING_DOCUMENTS.md) incl. the new "How it is built (P03-05c)"; [ARCHITECTURE.md § 6.4, § 6.5](../ARCHITECTURE.md); [DATABASE_SCHEMA.md § 4](../DATABASE_SCHEMA.md) `bills.details`; [ONBOARDING_COMPLIANCE.md § 4.1](../ONBOARDING_COMPLIANCE.md); [PLATFORM_CONSOLE.md § 6](../PLATFORM_CONSOLE.md); AGENT.md § 4 Documents, § 7; `docs/plans/P02-P03-subtasks.md` P03-05c):
- **Schema (module migration, one change):** `Modules/Billing/database/migrations/2026_11_12_050501_add_details_to_bills_table` — `bills.details` json nullable: the parties as at issue (`tenant` {name, phone, email}, `building` {name, address}, `unit`, `lease` {number, cycle}). Null on bills issued before (they print their parties as they are now).
- **At issue** (`Bills\BillIssuer`): the parties through the Tenants and Properties contracts (`Bills\BillDetails::take()` → `Bills\IssuedParties`), with the lease's number and cycle from the draft (`BillDraft` gains optional `leaseNumber`, `cycle`; `RentBillGenerator` passes them). `Models\Bill` is now `HasMedia` (`HasOrganizationMedia`) and refuses any change to `number`, `payment_reference`, `verification_code`, `issued_at`, `branding_snapshot`, `details`, and a second `document_media_id` (`LogicException`). `BillLedger`, `paid_kobo` and `BillService` are unchanged.
- **Invoice** (`Documents\Invoices`): Core `InvoiceDocument` from a bill — snapshot branding/layout, kept parties, lines with their periods, reference, verification code — with money and status now (paid to date, badge, VOID + reason); bank = the organization's primary usable account (`BankAccounts::primary()`, P01-05); `OnlinePayment` (link to the pay page) when a payment option offers the bill. `pdf()` = `DocumentRenderer::stored($bill, key, …)`: key `invoice` (as issued) or `invoice-void` (VOID copy of a void bill; the original is kept); records `bills.document_media_id` once. `kept()`, `payable()` (→ `Contracts\PayableBill`).
- **Rendered once on `pdf` after commit:** `Listeners\QueueInvoicePdf` (`ShouldHandleEventsAfterCommit`, on `BillIssued` and `BillVoided`) → `Jobs\RenderInvoice` (`pdf` queue, afterCommit, 3 tries, backoff 30/120 s, timeout 50 s; idempotent). A rolled-back issue queues nothing; a download before the queue got there renders it then, once.
- **Bill page:** "Invoice" (`GET /o/{slug}/bills/{id}/invoice`, the web page, `no-store`) and "Invoice (PDF)" (`…/invoice.pdf` → signed `MediaUrls` link to the kept file), `Http\Controllers\InvoiceController`, `throttle:60,1`, behind the new **`BillPolicy::viewInvoice`** (reachable + `bills.view`; `BuildingAccess` narrowing → 404; a `view…` ability, so allowed while suspended; `#[BlockedWhileImpersonating]` — it prints the tenant's phone and email, as P03-03b's contract download). No new permission.
- **Verify:** `Documents\InvoiceVerifier` registered as kind `invoice` in `DocumentVerifiers`: "Invoice", issuer as printed (snapshot), number, issue date, amount; **valid** with "Payment status" (Unpaid / Partly paid / Paid / Overdue), "Due" and "Period"; a void bill → **voided** "Voided on dd/mm/yyyy" (no reason). Never tenant, unit, address, bank or reference. `tests/Feature/Documents/VerifyPageTest.php`'s fake resolver now registers kind `sample_invoice` (as P03-04b asked).
- **Pay page** `/pay/{reference}` (`Http\Controllers\PayPageController`, `Pay\PayPages`, `Pay\PayPage`, view `billing::pay.show`; route moved from Core's 404 stub into `Modules/Billing/routes/web.php`, same name `pay.show`): no sign-in; `throttle:30,1,pay` per IP; `no-store`, `noindex`, the sign-in pages' CSP (`SignInPageHeaders`, no scripts); typed references (case, spaces, dashes, O/I/L) → 301 to the canonical address; unknown/malformed/rolled-back → plain 404. Lookup = decision 2's pattern: one unscoped read of `bills.organization_id` by the globally unique reference, then the bill inside `OrganizationContext::run`. Shows the agency's branding **as on the invoice** (snapshot logo/monogram, colour, name, phone, email), number, issue/due dates, period, lines, building — unit, total, paid so far, **amount to pay**, the organization's **primary usable bank account now** with the reference for the narration, and the payment options. Paid → "This bill is paid", void → "This bill is void … Do not pay it", neither with bank details; no usable account → "contact the agency". No tenant name or contacts, no address.
- **Payment options slot** (`Contracts\PaymentOptions` singleton registry, `Contracts\PaymentOption` {`offers`, `render`}, `Contracts\PayableBill`): for OnlinePayments (P03-08) to register its Paystack button; asked inside the bill's organization, only while the bill asks for money.
- **Lists/pages keep issued names:** `Lists\BillParties` falls back to the names kept in `details` when the contracts no longer find the tenant, unit or building (e.g. after a handover).
- **Docs:** BRANDING_DOCUMENTS.md § 1 (pay page built) and § 5 "How it is built (P03-05c)"; ARCHITECTURE.md § 6.4 (issue keeps parties; new "Invoices" bullet) and § 6.5 (rent invoice row as built); DATABASE_SCHEMA.md § 4 `bills` (`details`, `document_media_id`, frozen columns, handover note); ONBOARDING_COMPLIANCE.md § 4.1 (pay page as built); PLATFORM_CONSOLE.md § 6 (invoice view blocked while impersonating); AGENT.md § 4 (parties snapshotted too); `routes/web.php` comment.
- **Tests support:** `holdInvoicePdfs()` in the Billing test helpers (fakes only `RenderInvoice`); existing bill tests, `ListExportCoverageTest`, the bill-generation drill and its fixture process hold the invoice PDF back (it is not their business; dompdf takes 1–3 s a bill). New `Modules/Billing/tests/Feature/invoice-helpers.php`.

**Tests and evidence (local, MariaDB 11.4):**
- `composer ci` on the final commit: Pint passed, Larastan 0 errors, Pest **2,479 tests: 2,438 passed, 41 skipped (the Chrome halves with no Gotenberg locally: 34 from P03-03b/05a/05b + this task's 3 visual diffs and 4 phone-width checks), 27,687 assertions**, 0 failed (`--parallel`, drills included). An earlier run caught `VerificationCodesTest` registering a fake `invoice` resolver (now kind `sample_invoice`).
- **AC: changing branding later does not change issued documents (HTML and stored PDF bytes)** — `Modules/Billing/tests/Feature/InvoiceIssueTest.php` › "acceptance: changing branding later does not change issued documents" › "prints the invoice as issued, web page and kept PDF bytes alike, after the branding, names and layout change" (PDF held back; after issue the trading name, colour, layout, prefix, tenant name, building name/address and unit label all change; then the job renders: web page byte-identical to the one at issue, old names/colour/layout/number in HTML and PDF text, none of the new; a third branding change → download returns the same bytes, no new render, same web page). Negative control (temporary, reverted): with `Invoices::brand()` reading the live branding instead of the snapshot, this test fails. › "keeps the parties on the bill at issue, and the bills list and page keep showing them once they are gone" (`details` compared with `toEqual`; unit soft-deleted and tenant moved out of the organization → bill page still shows "Flat 3B" and "Kemi Adebayo"). `PayPageTest` › "shows the branding the invoice was issued with, not branding changed later".
- **Render once on `pdf` after commit; stored PDF reused, not re-rendered** — `InvoiceIssueTest` › "queues the invoice PDF on the pdf queue once the bill is issued, and nothing for an issue that rolls back" (`Queue::assertPushedOn('pdf', RenderInvoice…)` once; a `BillIssuer::issue` in a transaction that throws queues nothing and leaves one bill), › "renders the PDF once and serves that same kept file on every download, even when the job runs again" (counting driver: renders only at issue; two downloads → signed `/media` link, identical bytes; job re-run renders nothing; one `issued_documents` media owned by the bill, key `invoice`, `document_media_id`; PDF text has INVOICE, number, tenant, unit, amount, reference, account number, "Page 1 of 1"), › "renders the PDF on the first download when the queue has not got to it yet".
- **Evidence** — › "keeps the PDF as evidence and never changes what the bill was issued with" (`EvidenceFileProtected` on media delete; `LogicException` for each frozen column and a second `document_media_id`; file still on disk), › "prints a VOID copy once the bill is voided, and keeps the invoice as issued" (void queues the job; `invoice-void` media; original bytes unchanged; web page and PDF show VOID + reason).
- **Sub: verify page shows the invoice's current status (unpaid → partly paid → paid → void)** — › "shows unpaid, then partly paid, then paid, and nothing about the tenant, the unit or the bank" (payments applied through `BillLedger`; asserts no tenant name/phone, building, unit, address, account number or reference), › "shows a void bill's invoice as voided, without the reason", › "answers 404 for a code nobody issued, and shows another organization's invoice under its own issuer only".
- **Sub: unknown reference gets 404** — `Modules/Billing/tests/Feature/PayPageTest.php` › "answers the same plain 404 for every reference that finds no bill" (never issued, not a reference, too short, letters outside the alphabet), › "answers 404 for the reference of a bill removed by an import rollback"; `tests/Feature/Console/SurfacesTest.php` (unchanged) still passes for `/pay/PAY-2026-0001` and `/pay/x`.
- **Sub: `/pay` is rate-limited** — › "allows 30 pages a minute per address, then 429, unknown references included" (29 OK + one 404 = 30; 31st → 429 for any reference; after 61 s OK again).
- **Cross-org: a reference never shows another organization's bill** — › "shows each reference's own organization's bill only, whoever is signed in" (their reference: their name, number, amount, account; none of ours; signed in as the other organization's owner, our reference shows only ours). Bill pages: `InvoiceIssueTest` › "answers 404 for another organization's bill, by URL, by guessed ID and by a signed link of the other organization" (their bill id under our slug, our bill under theirs, their owner on ours, a stranger, a forged media link → 404).
- **Chrome/dompdf parity on a real issued invoice** — `InvoiceIssueTest` › "acceptance: a real issued invoice prints alike with dompdf and Chrome" › "prints on one A4 page with dompdf, in the organization's layout" (3 layouts; always runs) and "prints the same with Chrome (Gotenberg) as with dompdf, within the threshold" (3 layouts; **skipped without Gotenberg, runs in CI**; with `CI` set an unreachable Gotenberg fails). **Verified locally** against a scratch Gotenberg stand-in (P03-05b's Node server driving the installed Chrome over DevTools, copied to my scratchpad on port 3997, not committed): 3/3 pass, scores modern 0.13 %, classic 0.19 %, compact 0.10 % (threshold 0.4 %), one page each.
- **Pay page content and privacy** — `PayPageTest` › "shows the agency's branding, the bill, the amount to pay and the bank account, without signing in" (headers, CSP without scripts, no `<script>`), › "never shows the tenant's name or contact details, nor the address", › part payment / paid (no bank details), › void (no amounts, no bank, no reason), › primary account now / none usable, › typed references 301, › payment options slot (offered option shown on the pay page and "Pay online" link on the invoice; above the option's limit or once paid, nothing), › "lays out on a 360 px screen without sideways scrolling, in real Chrome" (4 states; Chrome half, skipped without Gotenberg). Stand-in: 4/4 (viewport 360, overflow 0); screenshots at 360 and 1200 px looked right.
- **Who may open the invoice** — › role matrix (owner/admin/agent 200 + PDF redirect; property-owner/tenant 404), › narrowed agent 404, › "still shows the invoice while the organization is suspended (a read), but never to impersonating staff" (read-only and write sessions: no button on the bill page, web page and PDF 403).
- Existing tests changed (none weakened): Billing feature tests + `ListExportCoverageTest` + `BillGenerationDrillTest` and `tests/Fixtures/Processes/bill-generation.php` hold back `RenderInvoice`; `VerifyPageTest` and `VerificationCodesTest` fake kind `invoice` → `sample_invoice` (and the unknown-kind check uses `unknown_kind`); `VerificationCodesTest` now also expects Billing's `invoice` and Leasing's `lease_contract` among the registered kinds.
- MySQL JSON key order: `details` compared with `toEqual`; no `toBe` on JSON maps.
- New tests with `APP_DEBUG=true`: InvoiceIssueTest + PayPageTest + VerifyPageTest + VerificationCodesTest: 78 tests, 71 passed, 7 skipped (Chrome halves); with `APP_DEBUG=true` and the local Chrome stand-in, InvoiceIssueTest + PayPageTest 46/46 passed.

**Orchestrator review:** merged `main` (with P03-10a) into the branch; resolved a PLATFORM_CONSOLE.md § 6 conflict by combining both sides (the invoice download and pay-page rules beside the payments rules). Added `holdInvoicePdfs()` to the four Payments feature test files and faked `RenderInvoice` in the payment drill, so bills issued there no longer render real PDFs. `composer ci` locally: Pint passed, Larastan 0 errors, Pest 2,537 tests: 2,496 passed, 41 skipped (Gotenberg). **GitHub Actions** run [40551964156](https://github.com/thekingchild/Goproperty/actions/runs/40551964156): green on all four databases, 2,537 passed with **none skipped** — the invoice Chrome parity and pay-page 360 px checks ran against real Gotenberg.

**Decisions to confirm** (orchestrator decisions relied on, and choices made here):
- Relied on **decision 2** (lookup pattern) for `/pay/{reference}`: reference → `bills.organization_id` by one unscoped read, then `OrganizationContext::run`; and through P03-05a's `DocumentVerifiers` for `/verify`.
- Relied on **decision 6** (Gotenberg): the Chrome halves (visual diff on a real issued invoice, pay page at 360 px) skip only when Gotenberg does not answer; fail on CI if unreachable; dompdf always tested.
- Relied on **decision 8** (plan gating): built ungated; "Pay online" depends on own Paystack (Growth+, BILLING § 1.2) — the P06-01 gate belongs in P03-08's `PaymentOption::offers()`.
- Relied on **decision 9** (narrowing): `BillPolicy::viewInvoice` narrows through `BuildingAccess` (404).
- Relied on the **product owner rules**: issued invoices are evidence (PDF in `issued_documents`, never deleted; VOID copy is a second file, the original kept; issue-time columns frozen); handover: bills are history (see below).
- **The kept PDF is the invoice as rendered just after issue** (status and payments at that moment — normally UNPAID/OVERDUE, or with tenant credit if P03-10a applied it first); the **web page shows the current status and payments** (BRANDING § 5: "built from its record, the stored BrandingSnapshot and the current status") with the issue-time branding and parties. A paid bill's PDF is not re-issued (receipts are the proof of payment); a void bill gets a VOID copy (`invoice-void`, like receipts' `receipt-void`).
- **Bank details are not snapshotted:** the invoice and pay page print the organization's primary usable account at the time they are shown/rendered (the kept PDF: just after issue). Storing account numbers in the bill's JSON would bypass P01-05's encrypted cast; and an account the bank disputes must stop being shown to tenants (ONBOARDING § 4.1).
- **Pay page branding = the invoice's snapshot** (not the live branding): the tenant sees the brand on the invoice they hold, images are embedded (no signed links on a public page).
- **Pay page shows building — unit but no tenant name, contacts or address** (BRANDING § 1 lists only "bank details and the online-payment button"; anyone holding the reference opens it). Reference + amount together are shown because the feature requires them (AGENT.md § 7).
- **Void reference → a clear "This bill is void … Do not pay it" page (200)**, not 404 (a tenant holding the invoice should be told); paid → "This bill is paid"; neither shows bank details. Unknown → plain 404.
- **Invoice web page and PDF blocked while impersonating** (tenant phone/email printed; same as the lease contract download); staff still see the bill page.
- **Invoice notification to the tenant: out of scope** (ARCH § 6.4: "P05 notifies" on `BillIssued`).
- **Pay page at its own minimal layout** (inline styles, no Vite assets, no scripts) under the sign-in pages' CSP; P03-08 will need to widen the CSP for Paystack's inline script/popup if it uses one.

**Management handover (P03-14):** nothing in this task moves. `bills.details`, the invoice PDFs (`issued_documents` media of the bill) and the `invoice` verification codes are history of the issuing organization. The pay page of an old organization's open bill keeps showing that organization's branding and its current bank account (its receivable), whatever happens to the building; P03-14 decides whether such bills are cancelled.

**Follow-ups:**
- P03-08: register the Paystack `PaymentOption` (offers = own Paystack connected, live, amount ≤ the online cap; plan gate P06-01); the pay page's CSP may need Paystack's hosts.
- P03-10a/b: apply tenant credit on `BillIssued` before the PDF job runs if the kept PDF should show it (both after commit; the job is queued); receipts for `/verify` kind `receipt` (`VerifyPageTest` still registers a fake `receipt`; P03-10b must rename it like this task did for `invoice`).
- P05: invoice email/SMS/WhatsApp on `BillIssued` with the pay link (`route('pay.show', $reference)`).
- P03-06b: utility bills issue through `BillIssuer` and get the same invoice/PDF/pay page; the utility-bill template (readings) may need its own document instead of `InvoiceDocument`.
- Pay page for a cancelled organization's bills: still shown as built (the receivable exists); product owner to confirm.
- The kept PDF does not change when a bill is paid; if agencies want a "PAID" copy, add a key like `invoice-paid` (not built).

**Next:** P03-05 is complete; with P03-10b (wave 6 partner) the next wave is P03-09 ‖ P03-07a.

### P03-10a — Complete (2026-10-27): `PaymentService` core: the Payments module with payments, allocations across bills under the bill ledger's locks, tenant credit applied to the next bill once, confirm/reject with a reason, `PaymentRecorder` for Collections and OnlinePayments, the record form, the confirmation queue and the payment page with export

The parent **P03-10 stays In progress** until P03-10b (receipts, void with a second approver, notifications) is done.

**Delivered** ([ARCHITECTURE.md § 5 "Payments", § 6.4 "How payments are built", § 6.13, § 8](../ARCHITECTURE.md); [DATABASE_SCHEMA.md § 4](../DATABASE_SCHEMA.md) `payments`, `payment_allocations`, `tenant_credit_entries`, new `tenant_credit_balances`; AGENT.md § 3 rules 2, 5, 6, 8, 9; `docs/plans/P02-P03-subtasks.md` P03-10a):
- **Module:** `Modules/Payments` (`module:make`, trimmed like Billing: no controller, seeder, event provider, config or factories), in `modules_statuses.json`. `PaymentsServiceProvider` binds `Contracts\PaymentRecorder`, registers `PaymentPolicy`, the Livewire namespace `payments::`, the sidebar item "Payments" (order 470, after Bills), the list export and the `BillIssued` listener.
- **Schema (module migrations, one table each):** `2026_11_10_100101_create_payments_table` (DB § 4 columns plus `bill_id` (the bill the payment was for), `note`, `idempotency_key`, `rejected_at`/`rejected_by_user_id`, provenance; `paid_at` is a date (Lagos); `bank_statement_line_id` and `receipt_id` without FKs until P03-07a/P03-10b; unique `(organization_id, idempotency_key)`, `(organization_id, paystack_reference)`, `(organization_id, external_ref)`; indexes for the queue, paid date, tenant, statement line, import batch), `…100102_create_payment_allocations_table` (unique `(payment_id, bill_id)`, FKs restrict), `…100103_create_tenant_credit_entries_table` (signed amount, reason, payment/bill FKs, **unique `applied_bill_id`**, note, provenance), `…100104_create_tenant_credit_balances_table` (one locked row per tenant, unique `(organization_id, tenant_profile_id)`). No TIMESTAMP column without a default (`TimestampColumnsTest` green).
- **Models and enums:** `Models\Payment` (organization-scoped, soft deletes, nothing mass assignable; refuses to delete — soft or hard — or change a confirmed payment, only `receipt_id` and a later move to `refunded` may change; never force-deleted), `PaymentAllocation` and `TenantCreditEntry` (refuse update and delete), `TenantCreditBalance` (refuses delete). `Enums\PaymentStatus` (pending_confirmation, confirmed, rejected, refunded — reserved), `Enums\CreditReason`; public `Contracts\PaymentMethod` (bank_transfer, cash, pos, paystack; members record the first three), `Contracts\PaymentSource` (manual, proof_upload, statement_match, paystack_webhook), `Contracts\PayerType`. All four models added to `tests/Support/OrganizationScopedModels.php`.
- **`Payments\PaymentService` — the only writer** (`DB::transaction($cb, 3)`, audited under log `payments`): `record` (member, policy `create`, checked input: tenant visible to the member and not archived, optional bill of that tenant, not void, in the member's buildings; amount in naira as typed → kobo, max ₦10bn; method bank transfer/cash/POS; paid on ≤ today (Lagos); note required for cash and POS, ONBOARDING § 4.3), `confirm` (payment row locked and re-read → refused unless still waiting; plan under the bills' locks through Billing's `BillLedger::lock` (id order): the bill it was for first, then the tenant's open bills oldest due first, each up to what it owes — or an explicit split, refused whole unless every share fits; each share `apply()`d and written as an allocation; the rest to tenant credit; `PaymentConfirmed`), `reject` (reason 3–500, nothing allocated; `PaymentRejected`), `recordAndConfirm` (one transaction), `plan` (preview for the page), `recordDraft` (the recorder's work, idempotent by key). A narrowed agent's confirmation settles only their buildings' bills (the rest to credit).
- **`Credit\TenantCredit`:** balance row locked after the bills (lock order everywhere: payment → bills in id order → credit), entry written beside it; `addOverpayment` (inside the confirming transaction; refused outside one), `applyToBill` (own transaction: bill locked first, skipped if void/settled/already credited, takes min(credit, owed) through `BillLedger::apply`, entry `applied_to_bill` with `applied_bill_id`, audit `credit.applied`). **`Listeners\ApplyCreditToNewBill`** handles `BillIssued` queued on `billing` after the issuing transaction commits (`ShouldQueueAfterCommit`, 5 tries, backoff), in the bill's organization.
- **`Contracts\PaymentRecorder`** (+ `PaymentDraft`, `RecordedPayment`; implemented by `Payments\Recorder` through the service): `record` and `recordConfirmed`, idempotent by `idempotencyKey` (and by Paystack reference): a replay returns the first payment unchanged (`created` false), `recordConfirmed` confirms an earlier pending one; `by` = the member acting (permissions and narrowing apply) or null for the system. Plain insert in a savepoint, then a locking read on a duplicate (no gap-lock deadlocks between racing replays).
- **Events** (`Modules\Payments\Events`, plain values, inside the transaction): `PaymentConfirmed` (payment, organization, tenant, amount, method, source, confirmed by (null = system), allocations `[{billId, amountKobo}]`, credit), `PaymentRejected` (… reason).
- **`Policies\PaymentPolicy`:** viewAny/view/export (`payments.view`), create/confirm/reject (`payments.confirm`, confirm and reject only while waiting; all three `#[BlockedWhileImpersonating]`), update/delete always false; 404 for a payment whose tenant is outside a narrowed agent's view. No new permission (`payments.view`/`payments.confirm` existed and were classed: confirm dropped while suspended and blocked while impersonating).
- **Pages (`payments::`, routes in the module):** `/o/{slug}/payments` (`PaymentIndex`: 25 a page, "To confirm" (the queue, oldest paid first, with a count) / "All payments" (newest first), debounced search over tenant name/phone/email, payer and reference, filters status/method/source/tenant/bill in the URL, tenant names in one lookup per page — no N+1; Record button), `/payments/create` (`PaymentForm`: tenant picker (narrowed), the tenant's open bills (narrowed), amount, paid on, method, reference, payer and type, note; "Record for confirmation" / "Record and confirm"; `?tenant=` / `?bill=` prefill), `/payments/{id}` (`PaymentShow`: details, what confirming settles, Confirm with a note, Reject with a reason; afterwards the allocations (bill links only where the member may see the bill) and the credit; the tenant's credit now). **Export:** `Lists\PaymentList` (rows via `PaymentRows`), registered from the provider.
- **Tenants (through its contract):** `TenantDirectory::narrowingAllows($member, $tenantId)` and `narrowedIdsQuery($member)` added (narrowing alone, no permission check, for records that follow a tenant), implemented in `TenantDirectoryService` on `TenantNarrowing`.
- **Docs:** ARCHITECTURE.md § 5 (new "Payments" paragraph, the suspended-organization decision; Tenants contract methods), § 6.4 ("How payments are built"), § 6.13, § 8; DATABASE_SCHEMA.md § 4 "as built" for `payments`, `payment_allocations`, `tenant_credit_entries`, new `tenant_credit_balances` (+ handover note), § 8A; PLATFORM_CONSOLE.md § 6 (payments export allowed, record/confirm/reject blocked); docs/runbooks/ci.md (the payment confirmation drill).

**Tests and evidence (local, MariaDB 11.4):**
- `composer ci` on the final commit: Pint passed, Larastan 0 errors, Pest **2,430 tests: 2,402 passed, 28 skipped (the Chrome halves of the P03-05 visual diffs and 360 px checks, no Gotenberg locally), 29,672 assertions**, 0 failed (`--parallel`, drills included).
- New and touched tests with `APP_DEBUG=true`: `Modules/Payments`, `PaymentConfirmationDrillTest`, `Modules/Billing`, `Modules/Tenants`, `BillGenerationDrillTest`, `ListExportCoverageTest`, `WorkspaceNavigationTest`, `ModuleSkeletonTest`, `CrossTenantAccessTest`: 364 tests, 364 passed, 5,782 assertions.
- **AC: overpayment goes to tenant credit and is applied to the next bill:** `Modules/Payments/tests/Feature/PaymentConfirmationTest.php` › "acceptance: puts an overpayment to the tenant's credit and applies it to the next bills as they are issued" (monthly ₦100,000 lease; ₦250,000 confirmed on 25 Dec → January paid, ₦150,000 credit; time travel through the scheduled `bills-generate`: February (25 Jan) takes ₦100,000 → paid; March (22 Feb) takes ₦50,000 → partly paid; April (25 Mar) finds none; entries +150,000 / −100,000 / −50,000 with bill ids; `credit.applied` audits).
- **AC: voided bills accept nothing:** › "acceptance: a void bill accepts nothing, whichever way a payment is pointed at it" (record for a void bill refused; explicit split to it refused; a payment recorded for it before the void skips it and pays the next bill; with nothing else open all goes to credit; credit never applied to it; paid stays 0).
- **AC: concurrent confirmations of the same bill cannot over-settle (locked transaction test):** `tests/Drill/PaymentConfirmationDrillTest.php` (fixture `tests/Fixtures/Processes/payment-confirmations.php`, 6 real PHP processes on their own connections, file barrier) › "acceptance: never settles a bill beyond its amount when payments to it are confirmed at once, the rest goes to credit" (30 × ₦7,000 for one ₦100,000 bill: all 30 confirmed, overlapped; bill paid exactly ₦100,000, 15 allocations summing to it; credit ₦110,000 in 16 entries = balance row; each payment's allocation + credit = ₦7,000; 30 audits) and › "acceptance: refuses explicit splits beyond what the bill still owes when they are confirmed at once" (30 × "₦30,000 to the bill": exactly 3 confirmed, 27 refused "is more than that", paid ₦90,000). Ran 4× green. **Negative controls (temporary, reverted):** without the payment row lock the double-confirm drill confirmed 13 of 10 (with errors); planning without the bills' locks made 10 of 30 confirmations fail — both caught.
- **Sub: double-confirming the same payment from two processes confirms it once:** drill › "sub: confirms a payment once when several processes confirm the same payments at once" (4 processes × the same 10 payments in different orders: exactly 10 confirmed, 30 refused "already confirmed", one allocation per payment, bill paid exactly, 10 audits); feature › "confirms a payment once…" (sequential second confirm/reject refused past the policy; audit and event payload).
- **Sub: impersonating staff and tenants cannot confirm or reject:** `PaymentAccessTest` › "sub: impersonating staff look at and export payments, but never record, confirm or reject one, in either mode" (read-only and write: no buttons, create page 403, confirm/reject calls 403, export works, `impersonation.blocked` audited in write mode, nothing changed) and › "sub: tenants cannot confirm or reject a payment, not even their own" (tenant with the profile's `user_id`: confirm, reject, record and the recorder refused; role matrix: tenant and property owner hold nothing).
- **Sub: a confirmed payment cannot be deleted:** `PaymentConfirmationTest` › "sub: never deletes or edits a confirmed payment, its allocations or credit entries" (soft and force delete, amount/status/tenant changes throw; allocations, entries, balances refuse; receipt id may be attached; policy update/delete false; raw delete refused by the FK; no non-GET payment route).
- **Credit applied exactly once per bill:** › "applies credit to a bill exactly once, however often the bill is announced" (listener handled again, event re-dispatched, credit added later: still one entry; a raw second entry refused by the unique `applied_bill_id`); › "keeps the credit balance equal to the sum of the tenant's credit entries".
- **Cross-org probes:** `CrossTenantAccessTest` (datasets `Payment`, `PaymentAllocation`, `TenantCreditEntry`, `TenantCreditBalance`); `PaymentAccessTest` › "answers 404 for another organization's payment guessed by ID…" (page 404, unknown/malformed ids, never listed/searched/filtered, form offers neither its tenant nor bill, tampered `paymentId` → 419 with debug off, writer refuses its payment, tenant, bill and explicit split, recorder refuses its tenant; a member of both sees each one's own).
- **Narrowed agents:** › "shows an agent assigned to buildings only their tenants' payments, and settles only their buildings' bills" (list, tenant filter, page 404, confirm 404, record refused for another building's tenant and for the tenant's bill in another building, export rows, a ₦150,000 confirmation settles only the flat in their building and puts ₦50,000 to credit; an unassigned agent sees all).
- **Suspended organization:** › "keeps payments read-only for members of a suspended organization, while payments the system records still land" (list/export work, no buttons, confirm/reject/record calls 403 and the service refuses; a verified Paystack payment through `recordConfirmed` without a member is recorded and confirmed; the next bill still takes the credit).
- **List exports:** `PaymentPagesTest` › "exports the payments the page shows, in Excel and CSV, with its filters" (headers, rows newest paid first, naira text, Lagos times, formula neutralised, queue filter); `ListExportCoverageTest` (a payment row; `payments` in the registry).
- Others: `PaymentConfirmationTest` (allocation order with the bill it was for first; explicit split and its five refusals; reject with reason; record validation (11 cases) and normalisation; kobo to the last kobo of credit); `PaymentPagesTest` (record from `?bill=`, errors shown, saved pending in the queue; open bills of the chosen tenant; record and confirm; confirm/reject from the page incl. a stale page; queue order, filters and search with fixed contact data; no N+1); `PaymentRecorderTest` (idempotent record; system `recordConfirmed` once incl. a replay under another key with the same Paystack reference; confirming an earlier pending payment with a member's explicit split; a split that does not fit records nothing; invalid drafts); `PaymentAccessTest` role matrix (5 roles × 8 abilities) and pages (owner/admin/agent vs property owner/tenant/stranger 404).
- Existing tests changed (none weakened): `WorkspaceNavigationTest` (sidebar gains `payments` for owner, admin, agent), `ListExportCoverageTest` (a pending payment; `payments` in the expected registry), `OrganizationScopedModels` (+4 models).
- MySQL JSON key order: no test compares a map read from a JSON column with `toBe` (audit properties are read by key; allocations as a list).

**Orchestrator review:** merged `main` (with P03-03b) into the branch; resolved a PLATFORM_CONSOLE.md § 6 conflict by combining both sides (payments added to the exportable lists and to the blocked money actions, beside the contract-PDF rule). The merge made the payment drill fail on cleanup (activating a lease now issues a contract), fixed as in the billing drill: `lease_contracts` cleared before `leases`, `RenderLeaseContract` faked. `composer ci` locally: Pint passed, Larastan 0 errors, Pest 2,491 tests: 2,457 passed, 34 skipped (Gotenberg). **GitHub Actions** run [40548054485](https://github.com/thekingchild/Goproperty/actions/runs/40548054485): green on MySQL 8.0/8.4 and MariaDB 10.6/11.4.

**Decisions to confirm** (orchestrator decisions relied on, and choices made here):
- Relied on **decision 9 (narrowing):** a payment follows its tenant; agents see the payments of the tenants they see (Tenants' narrowing on `BuildingAccess`, through two new `TenantDirectory` methods), and a narrowed agent's confirmation settles only their buildings' bills (the rest goes to credit): an agent never changes a bill they cannot open.
- Relied on **decision 8 (gating):** nothing in BILLING § 1.2 gates recording or confirming payments (every plan); own-Paystack gating belongs to P03-08 → nothing for P06-01 from this task.
- Relied on **decision 4 (people and roles):** tenants never get workspace rows, so they confirm nothing (they submit proofs, P03-09); receipt voids (decision 4's two-approver rule) are P03-10b. A confirmed payment is never voided or deleted here; undoing it will be a reversal.
- Relied on the **product owner rule on handovers:** payments, allocations and credit entries/balances are history (see below). No evidence files in this task (proofs are P03-09).
- **Suspended organizations (asked to decide):** members only read and export — recording, confirming and rejecting by hand wait for reactivation (`payments.confirm` is dropped by P01-06's `allowedWhileSuspended()`, unchanged); the system keeps working: payments recorded through `PaymentRecorder` without a member (a verified Paystack webhook: money already in the organization's own account) are recorded and confirmed, and new bills take credit, so tenants are not punished (ONBOARDING § 3, § 5.5). Product owner: confirm, or allow members to confirm while suspended (would need `payments.confirm` in `allowedWhileSuspended()`).
- **`tenant_credit_balances` (not in DB § 4):** one row per tenant, locked to serialise credit; balance = sum of entries (tested). **Columns added to `payments`:** `bill_id`, `idempotency_key`, `note`, `rejected_at`, `rejected_by_user_id`; **`applied_bill_id`** on credit entries. `paid_at` is a date.
- **Allocation order:** the bill the payment was for, then oldest due first; the rest to credit. **Explicit splits** are refused whole if a share does not fit (never silently moved to credit), so the member sees it.
- **Credit is applied when a bill is issued** (ARCH § 6.4), not to bills already open; credit created while a bill is open waits for the next bill. A narrow race (a bill issued between a confirmation's open-bills read and its credit write) leaves credit for the next bill too. An "apply credit now" action is a follow-up.
- **Cash and POS need a note** already in this task (ONBOARDING § 4.3); P03-09 adds the receipt straight to the tenant.
- **Pending payments are not editable** either: reject with a reason and record again.
- **Recorder replays** return the first payment unchanged even if the replay's draft differs (callers compare); `recordConfirmed` confirms an earlier pending payment with the same key.
- Source values follow DB § 4 (`proof_upload`, `paystack_webhook`), not the brief's shorthand ("proof", "paystack").

**Management handover (P03-14) — what this task's tables need:**
- Nothing moves: `payments`, `payment_allocations`, `tenant_credit_entries` and `tenant_credit_balances` are history of the organization that recorded them (with the bills they settled, which stay too).
- Product-owner question for P03-14: a moved tenant's **unused credit** is money the old organization owes them; as built it stays with the old organization and the new organization's bills do not take it (refund it, or carry it as an opening credit in the receiving organization through P03-13b's import path).

**Follow-ups:**
- P03-10b: receipts on `PaymentConfirmed` (fill `payments.receipt_id`, add its FK), "confirmed by" from `confirmed_by_user_id`, notifications on `PaymentConfirmed`/`PaymentRejected`.
- P03-09: proofs record `proof_upload` payments through `PaymentService` (inside the module; evidence collection), cash/POS receipt to the tenant.
- P03-07b: Collections calls `PaymentRecorder` (`statement_match`, key `statement-line:{id}`, `bankStatementLineId`, `billId` from the reference, explicit `allocations` for a manual split, `by` = the member); add the FK on `bank_statement_line_id` once the table exists.
- P03-08: OnlinePayments calls `recordConfirmed` (`paystack_webhook`, key `paystack:{reference}`, `paystackReference`, `billId`, `by` null).
- P03-13b: payment history and opening credit imports (provenance columns exist; `TenantCredit` needs an opening-credit method; rollback needs reversing entries, since entries are never deleted).
- Reversal/refund of a confirmed payment (`refunded`, `BillLedger::release`, refund/credit record) — not built; "apply credit now" to an open bill.
- Links: a bill's page → "Record a payment" (`/payments/create?bill={id}`, Billing module), the tenant's page → their payments (`/payments?tenant={id}`, Tenants module); the home "Today" inbox count of payments to confirm (UX § 1).
- P06-09: compliance flags on confirmation (`payer_type` is recorded for the cash thresholds).
- MySQL 8.0/8.4 not run locally: watch the locking reads, the unique keys and the drill on the first CI run.

**Next:** P03-03b runs alongside; then wave 6 (P03-05c ‖ P03-10b).

### P03-03b — Complete (2026-10-27): lease contract PDF: `LeaseContractDocument` in three layouts with signature blocks, the agency's clause template (organization setting, whitelisted placeholders only, Settings → Lease contract), contracts issued after activation (renewals too) into `lease_contracts` with a branding snapshot and verification code, PDF rendered once on the `pdf` queue and kept as evidence, re-print from the kept file, `lease_contract` on `/verify`

With it the parent **P03-03 is complete** (P03-03a lease engine + P03-03b contract PDF; the parent AC "invalid lease transitions rejected; renewal links to the previous lease; contract PDF renders with both drivers" all pass).

**Delivered** ([BRANDING_DOCUMENTS.md § 4, § 5](../BRANDING_DOCUMENTS.md) incl. the new "How it is built (P03-03b: lease contracts)"; [ARCHITECTURE.md § 6.5, § 8](../ARCHITECTURE.md); [DATABASE_SCHEMA.md § 3](../DATABASE_SCHEMA.md) `lease_contracts`; AGENT.md § 3 rules 7 and 10, § 4 Documents; `docs/plans/P02-P03-subtasks.md` P03-03b):
- **Schema (module migration, one change):** `Modules/Leasing/database/migrations/2026_11_10_030201_create_lease_contracts_table` — one row per activated lease: `organization_id`, `lease_id` (FK restrict, unique), `contract_number` (unique per org), `verification_code` (char 10, unique), `branding_snapshot` json, `details` json (tenant, building, unit, landlord, renewal_of, terms incl. first due date), `clauses` text (the template at issue), `issued_at` (`useCurrent()`: a bare NOT NULL timestamp would rewrite itself on every update on MariaDB 10.6, `TimestampColumnsTest`), `document_media_id` (no FK), timestamps; index `(organization_id, lease_id)`.
- **Model** `Modules\Leasing\Models\LeaseContract` (organization-scoped, `HasMedia` via `HasOrganizationMedia`; refuses delete and every change except filling `document_media_id` once). Added to `tests/Support/OrganizationScopedModels.php` (all `CrossTenantAccessTest` probes).
- **Document** `Modules\Leasing\Documents\LeaseContractDocument` (Core `Document`): number, issue date, renews, landlord (building owner, else the agency), tenant, premises, term, cycle, rent (figures + words), when rent is due, first due date, paid up to, deposit, clauses, verification code. Views `Modules/Leasing/resources/views/documents/contract/{modern,classic,compact}.blade.php` + `sections/` (header, parties, schedule, clauses, signatures, styles) on the kit's `partials/page`, letterhead, signature and verify-box partials: **signature blocks** for the landlord (agency as managing agent, with its signature/stamp images) and the tenant, each with name, date and witness lines; signatures + verify box kept together. Core `partials/page` gained `@yield('document-styles')` for a document's own styles (same lint rules). Later pages of a PDF start under a 40 px top margin (`@page :first` keeps the brand bar at the edge).
- **Clause template** `Modules\Leasing\Documents\ContractClauses`: organization setting `leasing.contract_clauses` (string, required, ≤ 10,000 characters; default = nine standard residential clauses); `render()` fills only the 14 whitelisted `{placeholders}` (`tenant_name`, `tenant_phone`, `landlord`, `agency_name`, `property`, `unit`, `address`, `rent`, `rent_in_words`, `cycle`, `deposit`, `start_date`, `end_date`, `contract_number`) by regex (a `{name}` touching another brace is left alone), leaves everything else literal, splits clauses on blank lines; the views escape every clause (`e()` then `nl2br`). Never Blade, `@php`, `{{ }}` or `eval`.
- **Settings → Lease contract** (`/o/{slug}/settings/documents/lease-contract`, `Livewire\ContractSettingsPage`): textarea, placeholder list, unknown-placeholder hint, preview with sample details, save, "Restore the standard clauses"; `viewSettings` to see, `updateBranding` to save (org-owner, org-admin); read-only while suspended; audited by the settings store (`settings` log, `updated`/`reset`). **Core `App\Navigation\SettingsTabs`** (+ `SettingsTab`): the settings tabs are now a registry (Core's four tabs + modules' from their providers, like `WorkspaceNavigation`); the `x-workspace.settings-nav` component renders it; the sidebar's Settings item also lights for `workspace.settings.documents.*`.
- **Issue and render:** `Listeners\IssueLeaseContract` (`ShouldHandleEventsAfterCommit`, on `LeaseActivated`, so a renewal's activation issues the renewal's contract) → `Documents\LeaseContracts::issue()` (lease row locked; once per lease; `BrandingSnapshots::take()` of the freshly read organization, `VerificationCodes::issue('lease_contract')`, parties/schedule through the Tenants and Properties contracts, the clause template) → `Jobs\RenderLeaseContract` on `pdf` (afterCommit, 3 tries, backoff 30/120 s, timeout 50 s) → `LeaseContracts::pdf()` = `DocumentRenderer::stored($contract, 'lease-contract', …)` (rendered once into the contract's `issued_documents` media, evidence) and `document_media_id` recorded. An issue failure is reported and never undoes the activation. `LeaseService` is **unchanged** (no transition touched; the listener does the work after commit).
- **Re-print:** `GET /o/{slug}/leases/{id}/contract` (`Http\Controllers\LeaseContractController`, throttle 60/min) → `LeasePolicy::downloadContract` → redirect to a signed, expiring `MediaUrls` link of the kept PDF; renders on the spot (once, same lock) only if the queue has not yet; a lease activated before this task gets its contract issued on first download. "Contract (PDF)" button on the lease page once activated.
- **Policy:** `LeasePolicy::downloadContract` = reachable (org + `BuildingAccess` narrowing → 404) and activated and `leases.view`; `#[BlockedWhileImpersonating]` (tenant contact details, as list exports); a `download…` ability, so allowed while suspended. No new permission.
- **Verify:** `Documents\LeaseContractVerifier` registered as kind `lease_contract` in `DocumentVerifiers`: "Tenancy agreement", issuer as printed, number, issue date, term, rent, lease state (In force / Renewed / Ended on … / Ended early on …); **valid**, or **superseded** with the renewal's contract number once the renewal is active. No tenant, landlord or address.
- **Docs:** BRANDING_DOCUMENTS.md § 4 (row) and § 5 "How it is built (P03-03b)"; ARCHITECTURE.md § 6.5 (contract row as built), § 8 (module settings tabs); DATABASE_SCHEMA.md § 3 (`lease_contracts`, domain map, `leases.document_media_id` now unused); AGENT.md § 4 (module document views); PLATFORM_CONSOLE.md § 6 (contract download blocked while impersonating).
